### [1] [Is Szilard Engine Really Broken?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2111.12300)
*Srinivasa Rao. P*

Main category: physics.class-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于范德华表面能原理（Vanderwalls surface energy principle）重新论证了麦克斯韦妖信息引擎（Maxwell demon information engine）的可工作性，回应了近期基于无内部结构活塞假设对该引擎操作性的质疑，并证明在特定条件下该引擎可借助传统活塞正常工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热力学第二定律（Second Law of Thermodynamics）规定了孤立系统中熵只能增加，而麦克斯韦妖思想实验曾对该定律提出挑战。虽然Szilard和Landauer的工作已从信息论角度调和了这一矛盾，但近期有文献基于无内部结构活塞的假设否定此类信息引擎的可操作性，因此需要更基础的理论依据来重新验证该引擎的可行性。

Method: 作者采用范德华表面能原理这一更为基础的概念，取代近期批评中所依赖的活塞无内部结构假设，重新分析并检验麦克斯韦妖信息引擎在标准活塞构型下的运行条件。

Result: 研究表明，当采用范德华表面能原理进行分析时，麦克斯韦妖信息引擎可以在与传统活塞（established piston）配合、满足特定定义条件的情况下正常工作，从而反驳了近期文献中对该引擎操作性的否定结论。

Conclusion: 麦克斯韦妖信息引擎的可工作性可以通过更基本的范德华表面能物理原理得到支撑，而非依赖活塞内部结构的具体假设，这为信息与热力学关系的理论提供了更坚实的物理基础。

Abstract: In the Second Law of Thermodynamics which is a macroscopic physical law that states that in any isolated system, entropy can only increase and order, or organization, can only decrease. This means that in the long run, energy will always dissipate and systems will always become less organized. James Maxwell proposed the idea of Maxwell demon through his renowned thought experiment. Leo Szilard, on the other hand, demonstrated this idea with another thought experiment using a theoretical model of an information engine integrating Maxwell demon and a simplified one-particle engine. This demonstration satisfactorily resolved the contradiction of the second law of thermodynamics. Landauer established the relation between the theory of information and thermodynamics, and proved that information is physical. However, recent publication contradicts the operation of such engine based on the principle of considering a piston without internal structure. In the present work, it is studied with the more fundamental idea of Vanderwalls surface energy principle to substantiate the workability of such engine and has been observed that the engine can work with a similar established piston under defined conditions.

</details>

### [2] [An Inverse Theorem for Partially Symmetric Two-Dimensional Semiclassical Schrödinger Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04796)
*Kuo Wang*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个二维半经典薛定谔算子在势阱具有单一反射对称性时的局部逆谱结果：如果势函数的三次项系数 $a_{30}$ 非零，则量子Birkhoff正规型（quantum Birkhoff normal form）的前两层系数（$b_{0,k,\ell}$ 和 $b_{1,k,\ell}$）连同 $a_{30}$ 的符号及横线数据 $\{a_{1,2k}\}$，可以唯一确定势函数的完整 Taylor 级数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在逆谱问题（inverse spectral problem）研究中，如何从谱信息（spectral data）恢复势函数的完整信息是一个核心难题。本文针对一类具有单一反射对称性（single reflection symmetry）的二维势阱，探讨量子Birkhoff正规型中有限层系数能否唯一决定整个势函数的Taylor展开，为逆谱问题提供了新的充分条件。

Method: 本文将二维半经典薛定谔算子（semiclassical Schrödinger operator）经过调和线性归一化（harmonic linear normalization）后，利用量子Birkhoff正规型将其Weyl符号表示为形式级数。通过分析正规型的结构，在 $a_{30}\neq 0$ 的条件下，证明前两层系数（包含 $\hbar^0$ 层与 $\hbar^2$ 层）可唯一重建势函数的全部Taylor系数。

Result: 当势函数三次项系数 $a_{30}$ 非零、且 $v_1/v_2\notin\mathbb{Q}$ 时，量子Birkhoff正规型前两层的系数（$b_{0,k,\ell}$ 和 $b_{1,k,\ell}$）与预设的横线数据 $\{a_{1,2k}\}$ 及 $a_{30}$ 的符号一起，唯一确定了势函数 $V$ 的完整 Taylor 级数。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了量子Birkhoff正规型的有限层信息与势函数完整Taylor级数之间的确定性对应关系，为具有单一反射对称性的二维势阱的局部逆谱理论（local inverse spectral theory）提供了形式化的充分条件。

Abstract: We prove a formal local inverse spectral result for a two-dimensional semiclassical Schrödinger operator whose potential well possesses a single reflection symmetry. After a harmonic linear normalization, the potential can be written as $V(x_1,x_2)=\frac{1}{2}(v_1x_1^2+v_2x_2^2)+\sum_{j+2k\ge 3}a_{j,2k}\,x_1^j x_2^{2k}$, with $v_1/v_2\notin\mathbb{Q}$. The operator can be brought into a quantum Birkhoff normal form whose Weyl symbol is a formal series $B \equiv H_2 + \sum_{2r+k+\ell \ge 2} b_{r,k,\ell}\, \hbar^{2r} Ω_1^k Ω_2^\ell$. If the coefficient $a_{30}$ of the cubic term $x_1^3$ is non-zero, then the first two layers of the quantum Birkhoff normal form (i.e., the coefficients $b_{0,k,\ell}$ and $b_{1,k,\ell}$) uniquely determine the full Taylor series of $V$, once the sign of $a_{30}$ and the transverse-line data $\{a_{1,2k}\}_{k\ge 1}$ are prescribed.

</details>


### [3] [Qualitative analysis, chaotic structure and exact solution of the nonlinear seventh-order Caudrey-Dodd-Gibbon-KP equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04846)
*S. S. Samanta,S. Sahoo,Vijil Kumar,Rajib Mia*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了(2+1)维七阶Caudrey-Dodd-Gibbon-KP（sCDG-KP）方程的行波解与动力学特性，利用($\frac{G^{\prime}}{G^{\prime}+G+A}$)方法获得精确解，并通过分岔分析、相图、混沌吸引子等手段系统分析了约化系统的动力学行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非线性高维偏微分方程的行波解及其丰富的动力学特性，为理解该方程所描述的物理现象提供理论依据。

Method: 通过适当的行波变换将(2+1)维sCDG-KP方程约化为常微分方程（ODE），应用($\frac{G^{\prime}}{G^{\prime}+G+A}$)方法求精确解；再将ODE转化为二维系统，采用分岔分析（bifurcation analysis）、相图、吸引子分析、时间序列、敏感性分析、分形维数（fractal dimension）、回归图（recurrence plot）和功率谱等方法研究动力学行为；并通过2D、3D及热力图进行图形化表征。

Result: 获得了亮孤子（bright soliton）和反扭结孤子（anti-kink soliton）解；通过二维/三维相图、时间序列、混沌吸引子、分形维数、回归图及功率谱等多种可视化手段揭示了约化动力学系统的分岔与混沌特性。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法成功求解了sCDG-KP方程并获得孤子解，同时系统展示了该方程在参数变化下所呈现的丰富非线性动力学行为，增强了所得解的实际可应用性理解。

Abstract: The main objective of this work is to investigate the traveling wave solution and dynamic characteristics of the (2 + 1)-dimensional seventh-order Caudrey-Dodd-Gibbon-KP (sCDG-KP) equation. Applying the ($\frac{G^{\prime}}{G^{\prime}+G+A}$) method, we examine the exact solution of the (2 + 1)-dimensional seventh-order Caudrey-Dodd-Gibbon-KP (sCDG-KP) equation by altering it into a reduced ODE via a suitable wave transformation. Graphical representations, such as 2D, 3D, and a heat map of the ascertained solution, are present to facilitate comprehension of the empirical relevance of the obtained solutions. As a result, we acquired a bright and anti-kink soliton solution. Next, we alter the ODE into a 2D system of equations to analyze the dynamical behavior of the reduced system via bifurcation analysis, phase portrait, and attractor analysis. During this process, we portray the graphical visualization of the bifurcation phase portrait, 2D phase portrait, 3D phase portrait, time series, chaotic attractor, sensitive analysis, fractal dimension, recurrence plot, and power spectrum of the dynamical system.

</details>

### [4] [Emergent Replica Clock Unifies Many-Body Localization and Thermalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04762)
*Tong Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文提出通过复制与折叠孤立幺正动力学，构造一个可统一刻画多体局域化（many-body localization, MBL）与特征态热化（eigenstate thermalization, ETH）的单一序参量——涌现时钟（emergent clock）。该时钟关联函数在MBL、临界态和热化相中呈现三种截然不同的渐近行为，并将历史相干性、缺陷线张力与转移谱统一在一个逆关联长度公式中；复制序同时提供第二离散坐标，可区分具有相同空间关联长度的局域化动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MBL与ETH传统上依赖一组分离的诊断指标而非单一序参量来区分，缺乏统一的序参量框架。本文旨在通过构造一个复杂统计力学模型，将这两类量子动力学相的区分归约为单个序参量的行为。

Method: 作者对孤立幺正动力学进行复制与折叠操作，生成前向-后向历史配对（forward-backward history pairings）的复杂统计力学描述，并在封闭循环红外扇区中涌现出时钟变量$r_x\in\mathbb{Z}_t$。通过受控的$l$-比特（$l$-bit）约化使时钟作用量变为准局域，并推导出逆关联长度与弹性系数之间的关系式。

Result: 时钟关联函数呈现三种截然不同的渐近行为：MBL相中指数衰减、临界点处无标度衰减、热化相中长期锁定（long-range locking）。逆关联长度满足$\xi_{\rm pair}^{-1}=\kappa_{\rm el}=\ln|\rho_0/\rho_q|$，将历史相干性、缺陷线张力与转移谱统一描述。复制序额外提供了第二离散坐标，能区分具有相同空间关联长度的局域化动力学。

Conclusion: 该框架首次以单一序参量（涌现时钟）统摄MBL与热化相，并揭示了复制序可作为分辨不同局域化动力学的额外判别坐标，为量子动力学相结构提供了更精细的刻画工具。

Abstract: Many-body localization (MBL) and eigenstate thermalization (ETH) are traditionally distinguished by a collection of separate diagnostics, not by a single order parameter. We show that replication and folding of isolated unitary dynamics generate a complex statistical mechanics of forward--backward history pairings and, in a closed cyclic infrared sector, an emergent clock $r_x\in\mathbb Z_t$. Its correlator has three distinct asymptotics: exponential decay in MBL, scale-free decay at criticality, and long-range locking in the thermal phase. A controlled $l$-bit reduction makes the clock action quasi-local, while its inverse correlation length obeys $ξ_{\rm pair}^{-1}=κ_{\rm el}=\ln|ρ_0/ρ_q|$, unifying history coherence, the defect line tension, and the transfer spectrum. Replica order supplies a second, discrete coordinate---the first order at which a hidden dynamical invariant becomes visible---and can distinguish localized dynamics that share the same spatial correlation length.

</details>

### [5] [Microscopic theory of the field-induced instability of the vortex-free state in superconducting thin-film strips](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04508)
*Takayuki Kubo*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文在Pearl–London理论之外，直接从无涡旋超导态的微观稳定性问题出发，消除了核心截断（core cutoff）的不确定性，在整个温度范围和所有宽度区间内确定了超导薄膜条带的失稳场B_s，并揭示了三种不同的宽度区间对应不同的失稳模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pearl–London理论将涡旋视为点对象处理，导致边缘势垒消失场的计算依赖于任意设定的短距离核心截断。该理论既无法确定截断形式，也无法确定其对温度T的依赖关系，因此本质上无法预测失稳场随温度的变化规律。

Method: 作者直接针对无涡旋超导态（vortex-free superconducting state）建立微观稳定性问题，从而绕开核心截断的模糊性。对于自场可忽略的均匀脏条带（homogeneous dirty strip），在全温度范围和所有考虑到的宽度区间内求解失稳场B_s。

Result: 识别出三种宽度区间及其对应的失稳模式：当W<W₁(T)时，超导性通过一维（1D）不稳定性连续消失进入正常态；当W₁(T)<W<W₂(T)时，边缘选择性的二维（2D）长波长模式失稳；当W>W₂(T)时，临界波数有限且失稳模式局域在边缘附近。在宽条带极限下恢复Pearl–London标度B_s∝1/W，但足够窄的条带中Pearl–London边缘势垒图像定性失效。

Conclusion: 该微观方法消除了核心截断带来的不确定性，统一描述了从窄到宽条带全宽度区间的失稳行为，并指出Pearl–London边缘势垒图像在窄条带情形下不再适用。

Abstract: In the Pearl--London theory, the edge-barrier-disappearance field of a superconducting thin-film strip depends on an arbitrary short-distance core cutoff because the vortex is treated as a point object. The theory does not determine the cutoff or how it depends on temperature $T$, and therefore cannot determine the $T$ dependence of the instability field. Here we formulate the microscopic stability problem directly for the vortex-free superconducting state. This removes the core-cutoff ambiguity and determines the instability field $B_s$ over the full temperature range and across all width regimes considered here. For a homogeneous dirty strip with negligible self-field, three width regimes occur. For $W<W_1(T)$, superconductivity disappears continuously into the normal state through a one-dimensional (1D) instability. For $W_1(T)<W<W_2(T)$, an edge-selective two-dimensional (2D) long-wavelength mode becomes unstable. For $W>W_2(T)$, the critical wave number is finite and the unstable mode is localized near an edge. In the wide-strip limit, $B_s\propto1/W$, recovering the Pearl--London scaling. In sufficiently narrow strips, however, the Pearl--London edge-barrier picture fails qualitatively.

</details>


### [6] [In search of novel ductile superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04789)
*Yiming Zhang,Samuel Poncé*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究基于实验合成的超导材料数据库（PRX Energy 4, 033012, 2025），对声子介导型超导体（phonon-mediated superconductors）进行了第一性原理高通量筛选，系统评估其力学性能，最终发现HfPd₂Al、TiRuSb和ZrNi₂Ga等兼具高临界温度（T_c）与延展性的新材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导材料的实际应用不仅取决于其超导临界温度，还受制于力学性能（如延展性）。此前缺乏对超导材料力学性能的大规模定量评估，本研究旨在建立超导材料力学性能的高通量筛选框架，为发现兼具高T_c与良好力学性能的超导材料提供依据。

Method: 该研究开发了结合第一性原理（first-principles）弹性常数计算与广义堆垛层错能（generalized stacking fault energies）计算的工作流程，用于评估候选超导材料的延展性。从250种具有超导临界温度的材料出发，计算其弹性张量以评估体模量（bulk modulus）、剪切模量（shear modulus）、Pugh比（Pugh's ratio）和Pettifor比（Pettifor's ratio）；进一步对选定材料计算堆垛层错能（stacking fault energy）和表面能（surface energy），从而估算Rice比（Rice's ratio）和延展性指标。

Result: 研究发现了若干同时具备高T_c与延展性的新材料，包括HfPd₂Al、TiRuSb和ZrNi₂Ga，其各向同性超导临界温度（isotropic T_c）分别预测为6.80 K、12.88 K和8.23 K。

Conclusion: 该工作为广泛超导体体系提供了力学性能的定量图谱，为识别具有力学应用潜力的新型超导材料提供了参考基准，有助于推动超导体在工程领域的实际应用。

Abstract: We performed a first-principles high-throughput screening of the mechanical properties of phonon-mediated superconductors selected from the recent experimentally synthesized superconducting materials database PRX Energy 4, 033012 (2025). We developed the workflows that combine first-principles calculations of elastic constants and generalized stacking fault energies to assess the ductility of superconducting candidates. Starting from the 250 materials identified with promising superconducting critical temperatures, we computed their elastic tensors to evaluate bulk and shear moduli, Pugh's ratio, and Pettifor's ratio from first principles. To further characterize their plastic deformation behavior, we calculated the stacking fault energy and surface energy for selected materials and slip directions, allowing the estimation of Rice's ratio and ductility indicators. We found that several new materials simultaneously exhibit high-T$_c$ and ductility including HfPd$_2$Al, TiRuSb, and ZrNi$_2$Ga with predicted isotropic T$_c$= 6.80K, 12.88K, and 8.23K, respectively. This work offers a quantitative mapping of mechanical performance across a wide range of superconductors and provides a reference to identify new mechanically promising superconductors.

</details>


### [7] [Nonlinear nanoelectromechanics of a movable Cooper-pair box](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05012)
*S. Park,A. Patra,L. Y. Gorelik,M. J. Park,H. C. Park,R. I. Shekhter*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过半经典方法理论研究了与正常金属柱耦合的可移动库珀对盒（Cooper-pair box）的动力学，分析了由机械运动与非弹性安德烈夫隧穿（Andreev tunneling）之间的非线性纳米机电耦合所引发的动力学稳定性，发现了以η（静电能与约瑟夫森耦合能之比）为变量的重入稳定性（reentrant stability）现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在将此前关于绝热自振动的研究拓展到非绝热（nonadiabatic）区域，并揭示超导器件中电子与机械自由度相互作用所导致的丰富非线性动力学行为。

Method: 采用半经典（semiclassical）方法对系统进行建模，通过线性稳定性分析（linear stability analysis）和分岔分析（bifurcation analysis）研究非线性纳米机电耦合诱导的动力学稳定性。

Result: 系统在η较小时通过超临界Hopf分岔失去不动点稳定性并产生自持振动；随着η进一步增大，出现第二个临界点使不动点重新获得稳定性。该第二转变在绝热区域对应于逆亚临界Hopf分岔，在非绝热区域对应于逆超临界Hopf分岔。

Conclusion: 结果表明系统以η为控制参数展现出重入稳定性，揭示了超导器件的丰富非线性动力学相图，将绝热自振动研究成功拓展至非绝热区域。

Abstract: We theoretically study the dynamics of a movable Cooper-pair box coupled to a normal-metal pillar using a semiclassical approach. We analyze the dynamical stability induced by the nonlinear nanoelectromechanical coupling between the mechanical motion and an inelastic Andreev tunneling through linear stability and bifurcation analyses. As a function of $η$, defined as the ratio of electrostatic energy to Josephson coupling energy, the system exhibits reentrant stability. At small $η$, the fixed point loses stability through a supercritical Hopf bifurcation, giving rise to self-sustained vibrations. With a further increase of $η$, a second critical point appears, at which the fixed point regains stability. We show that this second transition corresponds to an inverse subcritical Hopf bifurcation in the adiabatic regime and to an inverse supercritical Hopf bifurcation in the nonadiabatic regime. These results extend previous studies of adiabatic self-vibrations to the nonadiabatic regime and reveal a rich nonlinear dynamical phase diagram arising from the interplay between electronic and mechanical degrees of freedom in superconducting devices.

</details>

### [8] [Ideal points, directed completion and the case of the maximally extended Schwarzschild spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04718)
*Nicola Gigli,Argam Ohanyan,Marco Picerni,Zhe-Feng Xu,Matteo Zanardini*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究洛伦兹预长度空间（Lorentzian pre-length spaces）的定向完备化，证明在覆盖光滑全局双曲时空的自然假设下，该定向完备化与Geroch–Kronheimer–Penrose的未来因果完备化一致，并将其应用于克鲁斯卡尔–塞克雷斯时空的径向零测地线刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 洛伦兹预长度空间是广义相对论中时空几何的一种抽象化框架，其完备化概念对于理解时空边界（尤其因果边界）具有重要意义。作者希望建立这一抽象框架下的定向完备化与经典未来因果完备化的等价关系，并验证其在具体时空中（如Kruskal–Szekeres时空）的应用可行性。

Method: 本文采用数学证明的方法，首先在洛伦兹预长度空间中定义定向完备化（directed completion），然后在覆盖光滑全局双曲时空（globally hyperbolic spacetimes）的自然假设下，逐条证明该定向完备化与Geroch–Kronheimer–Penrose未来因果完备化（future causal completion）等价。最后将该结论应用于Kruskal–Szekeres时空，通过其径向零测地线（radial null geodesics）刻画其定向完备化结构。

Result: 在适当的自然假设下，洛伦兹预长度空间的定向完备化与Geroch–Kronheimer–Penrose的未来因果完备化重合。此外，Kruskal–Szekeres时空的定向完备化可通过其径向零测地线得到完整刻画。

Conclusion: 本文确立了洛伦兹预长度空间框架下定向完备化与经典因果完备化之间的理论联系，为该抽象框架在实际时空分析中的应用提供了证明基础，并具体展示了其在Kruskal–Szekeres时空中的可行性。

Abstract: We study the directed completion of Lorentzian pre-length spaces, and we show that, under some natural assumptions which cover the case of smooth globally hyperbolic spacetimes, it coincides with the future causal completion of Geroch--Kronheimer--Penrose. Moreover, we provide applications of our findings by characterizing the directed completion of the Kruskal--Szekeres spacetime in terms of its radial null geodesics.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，引力波观测能为这些源的致密双星本质提供直接证据，并能通过预测脉冲周期、天体位置和轨道性质来指导未来的射电巡天观测。

Abstract: The recently-discovered long-period radio transients represent a puzzling new class of astrophysical sources, some of which are thought to be compact binary systems emitting a pulse once per orbit. If this interpretation is correct, the orbital period--and therefore the gravitational-wave frequency--is directly encoded in the radio signal, which typically lies in the millihertz band. In this work, we explore whether these systems can be detected by the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA). Assuming that this phase-locking between radio pulses and orbital motion applies broadly across the population, we construct synthetic source catalogues informed by both observations and theoretical models of related systems, such as cataclysmic variables. We estimate that between $\sim 0.05\%$ and $\sim 6\%$ of long-period transients will be detectable within four years of observation with LISA, depending on astrophysical assumptions. For detectable systems, we find injected frequencies are recoverable to one part in $\sim 10^{5}$, amplitudes to within a factor $\sim 2$, and the sky positions to within $\sim$30 square degrees. Our results demonstrate that gravitational-wave observations can provide direct evidence for the binary nature of these sources and, importantly, can guide future radio surveys by predicting pulse periods, sky positions, and orbital properties. The mismatch between extracted frequency derivatives and that imposed by gravitational-wave decay is also computed to show that the likelihood of electromotive losses driving orbital evolution can be assessed for each detectable candidate.

</details>

Conclusion: 该工作为无截断的Vlasov-Poisson方程推导提供了新的证明途径，拓展了均值场极限理论的适用范围，对更广泛相互作用系统的动力学分析具有理论意义。

Abstract: We obtain the first derivation of the 2D Vlasov-Poisson equation for classical particles with Coulomb interactions without any microscopic cutoff. The proof relies on our recent dual hierarchical approach to mean-field limits, together with a refined analysis of dual BBGKY hierarchies on linearized correlations, based on kinetic regularization effects. The result holds more generally for arbitrary singular interaction forces $K\in L^{2-η}_{loc}$ with $η>0$ small enough, in any dimension, and it extends to Brownian particles. It holds globally in time as long as the mean-field solution is regular enough.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在液晶的Landau-de Gennes理论中，理解极限调和映射的奇异点结构是核心问题。此前单轴（uniaxial）情形已知存在点缺陷，而本文旨在回答双轴（biaxial）情形下极限映射是否存在内部点奇异，从而填补该理论在几何与变分层面的空白。

Result: 作者证明了在小弹性常数极限下，双轴真空流形上的局部能量极小化调和映射Q₀没有内部点奇异。该结论替换了传统的圆球测试场方法，采用了针对目标流形结构的构造性证明，且在单轴理论中已知点缺陷存在的前提下，表明该结果是尖锐的（sharp）。

Abstract: We study local minimizers of a sextic-potential Landau--de Gennes energy for nematic liquid crystals in the small-elastic-constant limit. Under the uniform energy and $L^\infty$ bounds, these minimizers converge to a locally energy-minimizing harmonic map $\mathbf{Q}_0$ into a biaxial vacuum manifold. The main result of this paper is that such a limiting map $\mathbf{Q}_0$ has no interior point singularities. The proof relies on a geometric identification of the lifted Frobenius metric on the universal cover $\mathbb{S}^3$ with a rescaled Berger metric. For a hypothetical tangent cone at a point singularity, its link is a nonconstant harmonic two-sphere into the Berger sphere. We construct a smooth variation field adapted to the Hopf direction and show that it induces a quantitative instability estimate, in contradiction with the shifted stability inequality inherited from local minimality. This replaces the usual round-sphere test fields by a target-specific construction and rules out interior point defects in the biaxial setting. The result is sharp in view of the well-known existence of point defects in the uniaxial theory.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相位约化（phase reduction）在理论上建立了高维振荡器动力学与Kuramoto模型等低维相位模型之间的联系，但将Kuramoto框架的分析结果与实验数据对接仍是一个开放挑战，因为许多物理平台违反标准相位约化所依赖的弱耦合假设（weak-coupling assumptions）。因此需要一种实验平台来系统验证复杂网络中同步的理论结果。

Method: 作者构建了一个基于电子正交振荡器的实验平台，通过设计非扩散耦合电路，使相位动力学在特定假设下与Kuramoto模型在弱耦合和强耦合两种情形下均保持等效。该平台的设计方法支持任意加权且有向的网络拓扑（network topologies），同时保持可扩展性和振荡规律性。

Result: 在不同耦合方案下，该平台能够可靠复现Kuramoto模型对全局同步（global synchronization）和集群同步（cluster synchronization）所预测的相变行为。

Conclusion: 该成本有效的实验平台为复杂网络中同步现象的分析和数值结果提供了系统性的实验验证途径。

Abstract: Phase reduction provides a principled relationship between high-dimensional oscillator dynamics and low-dimensional phase models such as the Kuramoto model. However, connecting analytical results derived from the Kuramoto framework to experimental data remains an open challenge, as many physical platforms violate the weak-coupling assumptions underlying standard phase reduction. Here, we present an experimental platform based on electronic quadrature oscillators whose phase dynamics are, under certain assumptions, equivalent to the Kuramoto model. Crucially, this equivalence is achieved for both weak and strong coupling through a non-diffusive coupling circuit, and the design approach supports arbitrary (weighted and directed) network topologies while preserving scalability and oscillation regularity. Across different coupling schemes, we demonstrate that the platform reliably reproduces phase transitions predicted by the Kuramoto model for both global and cluster synchronization. This cost-effective platform enables systematic experimental validation of analytical and numerical results for synchronization in complex networks.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在系统地分析两种热力学情形下（孤立系统与恒温系统）对应的吉布斯变分原理，并建立经典不可逆定理与变分原理之间的理论联系，以统一理解熵增与自由能降低的统计力学基础。

Method: 采用吉布斯不等式作为核心工具，将其应用于求解具有竞争相互作用的伊辛模型的自由能并分析相图；同时借助科尔莫戈罗夫方程描述概率分布的演化，从而在数学上建立玻尔兹曼不可逆定理与吉布斯变分原理的连接。

Result: 科尔莫戈罗夫方程预测：孤立系统熵随时间单调增加，而与热库接触的系统的自由能随时间单调降低；这两个结论分别对应了两种情形下的吉布斯变分原理。

Conclusion: 吉布斯变分原理与玻尔兹曼不可逆定理在数学上具有内在一致性，熵增与自由能降低均可经由概率分布演化的科尔莫戈罗夫方程统一导出。

Abstract: We analyze the Gibbs variational principles associated with the probability distributions of (i) an isolated system and (ii) a system at constant temperature. We give an example of using the Gibbs inequality to obtain the free energy and analyze the phase diagram of an Ising model with competing interactions. We also review the Boltzmann irreversible theorem, and show how it is connected to the Gibbs variational principles. This connection is established by using the Kolmogorov equation for the evolution of the probability distribution, which predicts a monotonic increase of entropy for an isolated system, and a decrease of the free energy for a system in contact with a thermal reservoir.

</details>


### [15] [The two-particle-irreducible vertex of the two-dimensional lattice $φ^4$ model across the Ising transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04497)
*Lode Pollet*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）测量二维单分量 φ⁴ 格点场论中的连通二粒子关联函数，重构了二粒子不可约（2PI）顶点函数 Γ(k,p;q)，并追踪其在伊辛（Ising）相变中的行为。研究发现失稳由 A₁（铁磁）通道驱动，软模是多维的；在临界点顶点出现幂律尾部；剥离交叉通道梯子后获得的全不可约顶点可近似为局域接触相互作用，以此作为动力学局域顶点近似（DΓA）的首性原理基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强关联量子多体系统中相变和临界行为的微观机制是凝聚态物理的核心问题之一。二粒子顶点函数包含了超越单粒子自能的双粒子关联信息，对识别相变驱动通道和理解软模结构至关重要。然而，在高维或强耦合系统中直接从第一性原理重构完整顶点函数一直缺乏可行方法。

Method: 作者从蒙特卡洛模拟中测量二维单分量 φ⁴ 格点场论的连通二粒子关联函数，通过矩阵求逆重构 2PI 顶点 Γ(k,p;q)。将顶点按 C₄ᵥ 点群的不可约表示分解，提取对称化的 Bethe–Salpeter 核特征值；通过剥离交叉通道梯子（crossed-channel ladders）获得完全不可约顶点，并将其代入 parquet 方程和 Schwinger–Dyson 方程检验。

Result: 失稳由零转移动量处的 A₁（铁磁）通道驱动，其对称化 Bethe–Salpeter 核的领先特征值趋近于 1。B₁（向列）和 B₂（对角向列）通道在所有系统尺寸下均与 A₁ 合作，表明软扇区是多维的。实空间中顶点在非临界时短程，在临界点发展出幂律尾部。有序相中 q=0 特征值坍塌（铁磁权重凝聚到单粒子可约序参量中），但有限动量涨落持续。剥离梯子后的全不可约顶点近似为局域接触相互作用，代入 parquet 和 Schwinger–Dyson 方程后以优于千分之一的精度重现蒙特卡洛自能。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了 DΓA 的首性原理基准，证明局域接触近似在全不可约顶点层面是高度有效的。此外，临界区中 parquet 方程的物理解表现为排斥型不动点（repulsive fixed point），其初始驱动力来自单一序参量模式，为理解临界动力学提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We reconstruct the 2PI vertex $Γ(k,p;q)$ from Monte Carlo measurements of the connected two-particle correlator for the two-dimensional single-component $φ^4$ lattice field theory and follow it across the Ising transition. Resolving the vertex in the irreducible representations of the point group $C_{4v}$, we find that the instability is driven by the $A_1$ (ferromagnetic) channel at zero transfer, whose leading eigenvalue of the symmetrized Bethe--Salpeter kernel approaches unity. Substantial $B_1$ (nematic) and $B_2$ (diagonal nematic) contributions cooperate with $A_1$ across all system sizes, highlighting that the soft sector is
  multidimensional. In real space, the vertex is short-ranged away from criticality while it develops a power-law tail at the critical point. In the ordered phase, the $q=0$ eigenvalue collapses because the ferromagnetic weight has condensed into the (one-particle-reducible) order parameter (or collective coordinate for a finite system), although finite-momentum fluctuations persist. Inserted into the parquet and Schwinger--Dyson equations, this contact reproduces the Monte Carlo self-energy with an accuracy better than one-tenth of a percent. This provides a first-principles benchmark of the dynamical local-vertex approximation (D$Γ$A). Additionally, we demonstrate that in the critical region, the physical solution of the parquet equations behaves as a repulsive fixed point, driven initially by a single order-parameter mode.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We derive an analytical expression for the steady-state quantum statistics of periodically driven quantum systems coupled to a bath using the nonequilibrium Green's function (NEGF) formalism. By embedding Floquet theory into NEGF, we obtain closed expressions for the retarded, advanced, and lesser Green's functions in the Floquet representation, yielding the Floquet Fermi distribution in which the steady-state occupation is expressed as a weighted sum of Fermi functions shifted by integer multiples of the driving frequency. In contrast to classical mean-field theories, the improved method is well suited for models with inhomogeneous stationary states and able to capture the essential properties of phase separation, e.g. in models with slow-to-start rules. The improved accuracy and new physical insights are illustrated through an application to the VDR model with $v_{\text{max}}=1$.

In three dimensions, finite-size corrections decay more rapidly ($δ\approx 0.75$), while the asymptotic residual saturation remains finite and depends on coordination number. Abstract: Transport in fluids is generally reduced to continuum laws parametrized by bulk coefficients and effective interfacial parameters, such as viscosities, diffusivities, slip lengths, and interfacial resistances. Here we formulate coupled transport in nanoconfined fluids as a space--time-resolved Onsager response matrix and extract it from equilibrium molecular dynamics simulations. Applied to a confined charged fluid, the framework resolves the nonlocal and transient pathways coupling particle, solute, heat, and charge transport. Momentum transport appears as a long-lived, nonlocal hydrodynamic mode, whereas charge transport relaxes rapidly through localized ionic friction.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Fix a positive integer $k\ge2$, and for $n\ge k$, consider a row of $n$ molecules. From among the $n-k+1$ nearest-neighbor $k$-tuples of molecules, select one uniformly at random and bond the $k$ molecules. Now, from all the remaining nearest-neighbor $k$-tuples, again select one uniformly at random and bond the $k$ molecules. Continue like this until there are no nearest-neighbor $k$-tuples left. Let $M^{(n)}_k$ denote the expected value of the number of bonded molecules. An explicit integral formula for $m_k:=\lim_{n\to\infty}\frac{M^{(n)}_k}n$ is known, and an explicit formula for $m_\infty:=\lim_{k\to\infty}m_k$ is known. The constant $m_\infty$, known as the Rényi parking constant, arises as the limiting packing density for a continuous analog of the above discrete packing problems. These are all models of what is called random sequential adsorption (RSA). The first part of this paper studies the gaps of sizes $0,1,\cdots, k-1$ that arise between bonded $k$-tuples and shows that after scaling the $k$-grid, when $k\to\infty$ the empirical distribution of expected gaps in the discrete problem on the lattice
  converges weakly to an appropriate gap distribution that is known to hold for the above noted continuous analog. The second part of the this paper considers two different models of the discrete bonding problem when both $k_1$-bonding and $k_2$-bonding occur, with $2\le k_1<k_2$. Explicit formulas are obtained for the analogs of $m_k$, and the asymptotic behavior of these analogs is studied both when $k_2\to\infty$ with $k_1$ fixed, and when $k_1,k_2\to\infty$ at certain ratios.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 对足够大的面积倾斜强度（对应于 3D Ising 模型足够低的温度），证明了无限线系综相关函数的指数衰减，以及对有限线系综建立了一致（关于线数）的严格正谱间隙（spectral gap）。

Abstract: Random surfaces on a hard substrate often exhibit entropic repulsion, wherein the surface is propelled upwards to allow entropically preferable downward fluctuations. A particularly rich class of examples arises from the low-temperature 3D Ising model. A powerful approach to studying such surfaces is through their level curves, which form a family of non-intersecting random curves. In [CIW18, CIW19], an ensemble of Brownian lines with geometrically increasing area tilts was proposed as a putative limiting model in this case. This model falls outside the scope of techniques based on integrable or SDE structures, which have been key ingredients in the study of the Airy line ensemble.
  A particularly intriguing question about such line ensembles concerns their mixing properties when viewed as a Markov process, and in particular the rate of decay of correlations in time. For the Airy line ensemble, this decay is known to be inverse quadratic. The first quantitative bound on the decay of correlations in the area-tilted model, established in [CG25], was slower than polynomial in time. An earlier result [DLZ24] had established positivity of the spectral gap for the finite-line version of the ensemble, without quantitative bounds. This left open the important question of the true decay rate of correlations for the infinite ensemble. Settling this question for sufficiently large area-tilt strength, corresponding to sufficiently low temperature for the 3D Ising model, we prove exponential decay of correlations for the infinite ensemble and a uniform (in the number of lines) positive spectral gap for the finite ensemble. Our proof is based on establishing a precise form of separation of scales between curves of different indices, using a novel probabilistic approach involving embedding supercritical branching processes in the line ensemble.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该过程将簇的随机游走与连通性合并相结合，反映了物理中粒子凝聚、凝胶化等非平衡相变现象。理解无限簇是否会在有限时间内自发出现，以及何时发生有限时间爆炸，对于刻画此类随机过程的临界行为至关重要。

Method: 设 $d\ge1$，每个簇 $C$ 以速率 $|C|^{-\alpha}$ 做连续时间简单随机游走；若碰撞到另一簇则连接形成新簇。作者对参数 $\alpha$ 的不同区间分别建立概率估计，并通过遍历性与平移不变性论证来刻画爆炸事件。

Result: 在所有维度中，当 $\alpha\ge0$ 时几乎必然不会在有限时间内自发产生无限簇；当 $\alpha\le-1-2/d$ 时几乎必然发生有限时间爆炸；在 $\alpha\in(-1,0)$ 区间内，行为强烈依赖于初始构型；一维情形下给出精确相图。

Conclusion: 该过程展现出由参数 $\alpha$ 控制的分phase行为：较大的 $\alpha$ 阻止无限簇涌现，较小的 $\alpha$ 导致有限时间爆炸，中间区域存在对初始状态的敏感性，表明系统具有丰富的临界现象。

Abstract: We consider a stochastic process on $\mathbb{Z}^d$ for $d \geq 1$. Given a translation invariant and ergodic starting configuration of finite clusters, each cluster $C$ performs a continuous time simple random walk with rate $|C|^{-α}$. If it attempts to move to a vertex occupied by another cluster, it does not move, and instead the two clusters connect via a new edge. In all dimensions, we show that if $α\ge 0$, there is almost surely no spontaneous creation of an infinite cluster within finite time. Moreover, for any $α\le-1-2/d$ there is a finite-time blowup almost surely. In the regime $α\in(-1,0)$ we show that the behavior greatly depends on the initial configuration. In addition, in dimension one, we establish the exact phase diagram.

TL;DR: 本文推导了随机递归树（random recursive tree）中节点秩（rank）和层（layer）分布的精确递推表达式，证明了秩分布呈阶乘衰减而层分布呈几何衰减，并证明了具有固定秩或层节点数的自平均性质（self-averaging）。与以往研究不同，该方法不依赖标签或根节点，适用范围更广。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Seismic Newtonian noise, arising from fluctuating gravitational forces on detector test masses due to ambient seismic activity, represents a fundamental sensitivity limit for low-frequency gravitational-wave observatories such as the Einstein Telescope. In this work, the gravitoelastic correlation tensors (the cross-correlations between the Newtonian acceleration perturbation and the seismic displacement field) are derived and numerically validated for Rayleigh waves and body waves in half-space and full-space media, considering test masses located above and below ground, with and without a spherical cavity. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维 ${\cal N} = 4$ 超对称杨–米尔斯理论的格点化构造中，仅一个标量超对称能在非零格距下精确保持，其余七个超对称被格距阶 ${\cal O}(a)$ 的格点赝象所破坏。核心问题是这些超对称在连续极限下是否会自动恢复，抑或需要对格点耦合进行微调（fine-tuning）。

Method: 将连续理论的离散 R-对称与标量超荷的作用相结合，系统地推导出其余七个扭曲超对称（twisted supersymmetries）的显式形式。基于此构造，分析旋转对称恢复与 R-对称恢复之间的逻辑关联，论证二者在连续极限下的一致性。

Result: 研究表明，连续极限中旋转对称（rotational symmetry）的恢复将蕴含 R-对称的恢复，从而自动导致完整 ${\cal N} = 4$ 超对称的增强，无需对格点耦合进行额外微调。

Conclusion: 这些结果可能使三维超对称规范理论在非微扰（nonperturbative）框架下的格点研究成为可能，相关理论与弦理论（string theory）及镜像对称（mirror symmetry）有着密切联系。

Abstract: Topological twisting provides a powerful framework for constructing lattice formulations of supersymmetric gauge theories. In three dimensions, a twisted version of ${\cal N} = 4$ super Yang--Mills theory can be discretized so that one nilpotent scalar supersymmetry is preserved exactly at nonzero lattice spacing. The remaining seven supersymmetries are broken by lattice artifacts of ${\cal O}(a)$, where $a$ is the lattice spacing. An important question is whether these supersymmetries are automatically restored in the continuum limit $a \to 0$, or whether fine-tuning of the lattice couplings is required. In this work, we derive the additional twisted supersymmetries by combining discrete $R$-symmetries of the continuum theory with the action of the scalar supercharge. This construction suggests that restoration of rotational symmetry in the continuum limit implies restoration of $R$-symmetry, leading to an automatic enhancement to the full ${\cal N} = 4$ supersymmetry without further tuning. These results may enable nonperturbative lattice studies of three-dimensional supersymmetric gauge theories relevant to string theory and mirror symmetry.

</details>

### [37] [Variational Bounds for Perceptron Learning from Structured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04882)
*Francesco Camilli,Pierluigi Contucci,Federica Gerace,Emanuele Mingione*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文引入了一个变分方法（variational approach），用于分析在有限温度（finite-temperature）下、基于高斯混合（Gaussian mixture）数据训练的连续自旋感知机（continuous-spin perceptron）模型。通过组合插值方法（interpolation method）与对数凹（log-concavity）和集中性（concentration）估计，推导出极限淬火压力的上下极小极大变分界。两个界仅在一个变分参数的优化顺序上存在差异，当两个优化可交换时两者匹配并确定模型解，且同一势函数可统一计算基态能量、训练损失和泛化误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限温度连续自旋感知机模型的精确统计力学分析颇具挑战性，尤其是处理高斯混合数据与一般凹效用、对数凹可分离先验测度时的淬火压力（quenched pressure）求解仍缺少统一框架，因此需要发展一种可解的变分途径。

Method: 将插值方法与对数凹性和集中性估计相结合，为极限淬火压力构造下界与上界的极小极大变分估计（minimax variational bounds）。模型允许一大类凹效用函数和对数凹可分离先验测度，两个界仅在两个变分参数的优化顺序上不同，其余极值由变分势函数的凹-凸结构控制。

Result: 当两个变分参数优化顺序可交换时，上下界匹配并完全确定该模型的解。同一变分势函数同时给出定点方程（fixed-point equations）作为驻点条件，并提供计算基态能量（ground-state energy）、训练损失（training loss）和泛化误差（generalization error）的统一路径。

Conclusion: 该方法为有限温度连续自旋感知机在广义数据先验下的统计力学分析提供了一个统一且可操作的变分框架，其边界匹配条件为模型精确解的确定提供了充分条件。

Abstract: We introduce a variational approach to a finite-temperature continuous-spin perceptron trained on a Gaussian mixture. The model allows for a broad class of concave utilities and log-concave separable prior measures on the spins. By combining the interpolation method with log-concavity and concentration estimates, we derive lower and upper minimax variational bounds for the limiting quenched pressure. Remarkably, the two bounds differ only in the order of optimization of two variational parameters, while all remaining extrema are controlled by the concave--convex structure of the variational potential. Whenever the two optimizations commute, the two bounds match and identify the solution of the model. The same potential yields the fixed-point equations as stationarity conditions and provides a unified route to the computation of the ground-state energy, training loss, and generalization error.

</details>


### [38] [Multimodal Spatiotemporal Atmospheric Data Assimilation with Latent Flow-matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05103)
*Dibyajyoti Chakraborty,Romit Maulik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于潜变量视频流匹配（latent video flow-matching）的大气数据同化（data assimilation）统一方法，通过从ERA5再分析数据训练的先验中采样时间上一致的轨迹，并利用后验采样融合真实观测，可统一处理滤波（filtering）和平滑（smoothing）任务，并直接从稀疏观测生成全状态集合预报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据同化（DA）依赖贝叶斯推断更新数值预报模型状态，但不同同化任务需要不同的处理框架。本文旨在提出一种根本不同的统一方法，以更灵活的方式处理大气数据同化的多类任务。

Method: 利用潜变量视频流匹配（latent video flow-matching）从以ERA5再分析数据（69个变量、8天窗口）训练的先验中采样时间上一致的轨迹；通过后验采样（posterior sampling）同化真实观测源，如NOAA综合无线电探空档案（Integrated Global Radiosonde Archive）和综合地面数据库（Integrated Surface Database）中的观测数据。

Result: 由于先验生成连续轨迹，信息可在已观测与未观测帧之间自然传播，因而仅通过改变观测帧即可完成滤波和平滑等同化任务。该方法还能直接从稀疏观测生成全状态集合预报（ensemble forecasts），性能可与当前最先进的观测到预报模型相竞争。

Conclusion: 该统一方法简化了数据同化的流程，为从观测直接生成集合预报提供了新途径，并在预报性能上达到了与最先进模型相当的水平。

Abstract: Data assimilation (DA) uses Bayesian inference to update the state of a numerical forecast model with observed data. In this study, we propose a fundamentally different, unified approach to atmospheric data assimilation. We use latent video flow-matching to sample temporally consistent trajectories from a prior trained using ERA5 reanalysis (69 variables over an 8-day window). We also use posterior sampling to assimilate real observation sources, such as those from the NOAA Integrated Global Radiosonde Archive and the Integrated Surface Database. Because the prior generates a continuous trajectory, it naturally propagates information between observed and unobserved frames. Therefore, we can perform various DA tasks, such as filtering and smoothing, simply by changing the observed frames. Moreover, we generate full-state ensemble forecasts directly from sparse observations, achieving performance competitive with state-of-the-art observation-to-forecast models.

</details>


### [39] [Active Learning Guided Design Space Refinement for Scalable Multi-Objective Bayesian Optimization in Materials Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04651)
*Alexandros Ntagiantas,Panagiotis Tsilimidos,George Giannakopoulos,Christoforos Rekatsinas,Panagiotis Krokidas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合主动学习（active learning）引导的自适应搜索空间细化框架与多目标贝叶斯优化（multi-objective Bayesian optimization）的方法，以加速材料优化并保留帕累托相关区域。在COF材料CH4/N2分离和压力容器设计两个案例中，该框架将候选空间缩小约一半，同时保留了原超体积（hypervolume）的99%以上，并改善了贝叶斯优化的早期收敛与累积帕累托前沿发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯优化在候选空间增大时效率下降，常常在到达信息丰富区域之前评估大量低价值区域，难以满足有限评估预算下大规模离散材料设计空间的高效探索需求。

Method: 提出一种主动学习引导的自适应搜索空间细化框架，与多目标贝叶斯优化相结合。该框架通过主动学习动态筛选并收缩候选空间，同时保持帕累托相关区域的完整性，从而引导优化聚焦于高价值区域。

Result: 在共价有机框架（covalent-organic frameworks）的CH4/N2分离任务和包含材料方向应力分量与厚度目标的多目标压力容器设计中，AL引导的细化将候选空间缩小约一半，同时保留超过99%的原始超体积。缩小空间策略还改善了贝叶斯优化的早期收敛性和累积帕累托前沿发现。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架在受限自主材料发现场景中实现了高效的大规模材料优化，证明了主动学习引导的空间细化与多目标贝叶斯优化结合的有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Advanced materials discovery increasingly relies on machine learning and Bayesian optimization to explore large discrete design spaces under limited evaluation budgets. However, conventional Bayesian optimization (BO) can become inefficient as candidate spaces grow, often evaluating low-value regions before reaching informative areas. We propose an active-learning (AL)-guided adaptive search-space refinement framework combined with multi-objective BO to accelerate materials optimization while preserving Pareto-relevant regions. We evaluate the approach on CH4/N2 separation in covalent-organic frameworks and pressure-vessel design with material-direction stress components and thickness objectives. Results show that the AL-guided refinement reduces the candidate space by approximately half while preserving more than 99 percent of the original hypervolume. The reduced-space strategy improves early convergence and cumulative Pareto-front discovery from the BO, demonstrating efficient large-scale materials optimization across constrained autonomous materials discovery settings.

</details>


### [40] [CheMLFlow: An Open-Source Platform for Cheminformatics and Materials Informatics Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04942)
*Brendan Smith,Susana Lopez-Moreno,Eric Dolores-Cuenca,Sangil Kim,Jose L. Mendoza-Cortes,Nijamudheen Abdulrahiman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CheMLFlow是一个开源平台，用于构建和执行端到端、高通量及智能体驱动的科学机器学习工作流，涵盖数据获取、表示、训练、验证、筛选、解释和报告等完整流程，并已在量子力学、物理化学和生物活性性质预测任务上达到文献水平性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学机器学习开发中常见的瓶颈在于，研究者需要组装数据采集、数据处理（data curation）、表示（representation）、模型训练、验证、筛选、解释和报告等多个环节，即使其核心科研贡献仅涉及其中一个阶段。CheMLFlow旨在降低这种编排（orchestration）开销，使研究者能够更专注于自己的核心贡献。

Method: CheMLFlow提供模块化工作流组件、可直接运行的参考流水线（reference pipelines）、标准化产物（artifacts）和评估输出，减少了流水线组装成本并支持跨方法与数据集的基准测试（benchmarking）。平台设计强调可扩展性、可复现性和自动化友好性，包含可插拔的表示与模型、确定性数据划分（deterministic splits）、显式运行产物、批量执行和报告生成功能；其配置驱动的工作流和结构化输出也为编码智能体（coding agents）在人工监督下辅助用户构建实验、检查结果和总结发现提供了实用接口。

Result: 该平台在量子力学（quantum mechanical）、物理化学（physicochemical）和生物活性（bioactivity）性质预测任务上达到了文献水平的基准性能（benchmarks），并展示了超越分子化学数据集的用例，包括涉及时间序列（time series）数据集的应用。

Conclusion: 文章系统地描述了CheMLFlow的系统架构、核心工作流和基准测试结果，表明该平台不仅适用于分子化学领域的性质预测，还可扩展到时间序列等其他数据类型，为科学机器学习流水线编排和智能体辅助实验提供了通用基础设施。

Abstract: CheMLFlow is an open-source platform for building and executing end-to-end, high-throughput, and agentic workflows for scientific and technological applications. CheMLFlow targets a common bottleneck in scientific machine learning development, where researchers often need to assemble data acquisition, curation, representation, model training, validation, screening, interpretation, and reporting into a reproducible pipeline, even when their primary research contribution concerns only one stage. CheMLFlow provides modular workflow components, ready-to-run reference pipelines, standardized artifacts, and evaluation outputs that reduce orchestration overhead and support benchmarking across methods and datasets. The platform is designed to be extensible, reproducible, and automation friendly, with pluggable representations and models, deterministic splits, explicit run artifacts, batch execution, and report generation. As scientific software increasingly moves toward agent assisted experimentation, CheMLFlow's configuration driven workflows and structured outputs also provide a practical interface for coding agents to help users construct experiments, inspect results, and summarize findings under human supervision. This article describes the system architecture, core workflows, and benchmarks that reach literature performance for quantum mechanical, physicochemical and bioactivity property prediction, and use cases involving time series datasets demonstrating applications beyond molecular chemistry datasets.

</details>

Zummo,V. Zutshi,R. Zwaska*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本研究使用贝叶斯马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（Bayesian Markov Chain Monte Carlo, MCMC）方法评估深地下中微子实验（DUNE）对中微子振荡的灵敏度，展示了振荡参数的四维后验概率分布，并首次研究了DUNE对Jarlskog不变量（J系数的灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前DUNE灵敏度研究基于频率学派（frequentist）方法，本研究旨在探索采用贝叶斯框架所能获得的额外推断能力，特别是参数相关性、后验约束整合以及推导量（如Jarlskog不变量）的分布构造。

Method: 采用贝叶斯MCMC方法进行灵敏度分析，使用与先前DUNE研究（Eur. Phys. J. C 80, 978 (2020)）相同的灵敏度输入；生成四维振荡参数后验概率分布，并利用贝叶斯框架的灵活性在事后引入参数约束，特别是反应堆短基线θ₁₃限制；后验分布中直接构造Jarlskog不变量J的分布。

Result: 四维后验分布揭示了振荡参数空间中尤其是sin²θ₂₃与sin²θ₁₃之间广泛的相关性；引入反应堆θ₁₃约束后，对θ₂₃八分圆（octant）的灵敏度显著提高；MCMC结果可便捷地构造推导量的后验分布，并首次提供了DUNE对Jarlskog不变量J的灵敏度研究。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯MCMC方法为DUNE灵敏度分析提供了超越传统频率学派方法的额外推断能力，特别是在参数相关性可视化、后验约束整合及推导量分布构造方面，为表征轻子扇区电荷宇称（CP）破坏提供了新途径。

Abstract: The sensitivity of the Deep Underground Neutrino Experiment (DUNE) to neutrino oscillation is evaluated using a Bayesian Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) approach. This analysis uses the same underlying sensitivity inputs as previous DUNE studies [Eur. Phys. J. C 80, 978 (2020)], and therefore does not present updated DUNE sensitivities, but instead explores the additional inferences accessible using a Bayesian approach. We present four-dimensional posterior probability distributions of the oscillation parameters, highlighting the breadth of correlation in the parameter space of interest, especially between $\sin^2 θ_{23}$ and $\sin^2 θ_{13}$. We exploit the flexibility of the Bayesian framework to incorporate parameter constraints post hoc and assess the impact of applying a reactor short-baseline $θ_{13}$ constraint. A significant increase in the sensitivity to the $θ_{23}$ octant is found when including the constraint. Posterior distributions of derived quantities can be easily constructed from MCMC results. This work presents the first study of DUNE's sensitivity to the Jarlskog invariant, $J$, a quantity that provides a parametrisation-independent measure of charge-parity violation in the leptonic sector.

</details>

### [42] [News on charged and neutral hadron production from NA61/SHINE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04758)
*Yuliia Balkova,Maciej P. Lewicki*

Main category: nucl-ex

TL;DR: 本文报告了NA61/SHINE实验在CERN SPS能区关于中等尺寸核（如Ar+Sc和Xe+La）中央碰撞中强子产生的多项新结果，包括K⁺/π⁺比的"尖角"结构、（等等）K介子横向质量谱的反斜率参数、质子快度谱、Λ超子产生以及带电/中性K介子比率的各向同性（isospin）对称性异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能强子与核碰撞中的奇异粒子（strangeness）产生是研究极端条件下强相互作用物质的核心课题。NA61/SHINE实验提供了跨碰撞能量和系统尺寸的系统性扫描数据，本文旨在通过中等尺寸核的中央碰撞结果，填充从小型到大型系统的过渡区域数据，深化对QCD相图和重子输运的理解。

Method: 基于NA61/SHINE实验在SPS能区采集的数据，对Ar+Sc、Xe+La等中等尺寸核的中央碰撞事件进行分析，涉及带电和中性强子产额、K⁺/π⁺比值、K介子横向质量谱反斜率参数、归一化质子快度谱、Λ超子产生截面及带电/中性K介子比率等多个可观测量的系统分析，并与模型预测及已有数据进行比较。

Result: 结果证实K⁺/π⁺比在中央Pb+Pb碰撞中呈现"尖角"（horn）结构而在较小系统中缺失；中等尺寸系统的K介子反斜率参数达到与中央Pb+Pb碰撞相当的水平；质子快度谱展示了随系统尺寸变化的能量依赖重子输运；Λ超子产生在SPS能量范围内被系统表征；并在中央Ar+Sc碰撞中发现了带电/中性K介子比率R_K>1的异常超过，暗示可能存在超出预期的同位旋（isospin）对称性破坏。

Conclusion: 中等尺寸核碰撞结果提供了从轻系统到重系统过渡的关键信息，其中R_K>1的同位旋异常尤为值得关注，可能指向未知的物理机制，需要进一步的理论解释和实验验证。

Abstract: Strangeness production in high-energy hadronic and nuclear collisions remains a central topic in the study of strongly interacting matter under extreme conditions. The data collected by the NA61/SHINE experiment at the CERN SPS allow for a comprehensive scan of hadron production across various collision energies and system sizes. This article focuses on new results on charged and neutral hadron production in central collisions of medium-sized nuclei, such as Ar+Sc and Xe+La, in the SPS energy range. First, the energy and system-size dependence of the $K^+/π^+$ ratio is discussed, confirming the ``horn'' structure in central Pb+Pb collisions and its absence in smaller systems. Furthermore, the inverse slope parameter $T$ of kaon transverse mass (transverse momentum) spectra is analyzed, showing that intermediate-size systems reach values comparable to those observed in central Pb+Pb collisions. Second, normalized proton rapidity spectra $(\text{d}n/\text{d}y)/\langle W \rangle$ in central Be+Be, Ar+Sc, and Pb+Pb collisions are presented, demonstrating energy-dependent baryon transport as a function of system size. Building on this baseline, $Λ$ hyperon production in central Ar+Sc collisions is explored across SPS energies, where rapidity spectra, mean multiplicities ($\langle Λ\rangle$), and ratios to pions are compared with model predictions and existing data. Finally, an anomalous excess of charged over neutral kaons ($R_K > 1$) in central Ar+Sc collisions is reported, highlighting a potential unexpectedly large violation of isospin symmetry.

</details>

### [43] [Cosmological constraints and standard sirens forecasts for non-dynamical dark energy in Horndeski gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04079)
*Marcello Miranda,Ruchika,Ivan De Martino,Daniele Vernieri,Salvatore Capozziello*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究扩展Cuscuton模型中四个基准子模型，利用宇宙学计时器、Ia型超新星和重子声学振荡等背景观测数据，结合理论可行性、月球激光测距和大爆炸核合成约束进行参数限制，并预测第三代亮标准汽笛（如爱因斯坦望远镜和宇宙探测器网络）对模型参数的约束能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索ΛCDM模型之外的非动力学暗能量（non-dynamical dark energy）实现，检验扩展Cuscuton模型在可解析处理扇区中的可观测效应，并评估未来第三代引力波观测对模型判别的潜力。

Method: 选取扩展Cuscuton模型的四个基准子模型，使用宇宙学计时器（cosmic chronometers）、Ia型超新星（Type-Ia supernovae）和重子声学振荡（BAO）等背景探针进行拟合，同时施加理论可行性、月球激光测距（Lunar Laser Ranging）和大爆炸核合成（Big Bang Nucleosynthesis）约束；随后利用爱因斯坦望远镜（Einstein Telescope）和宇宙探测器（Cosmic Explorer）网络，针对瞬发辐射、余辉和千新星（kilonova）对应体，预测第三代亮标准汽笛（bright standard siren）的约束精度。

Result: 当前数据已将可行参数空间限制在ΛCDM的小偏移范围内，且未消除CC+SN与CC+BAO之间由校准差异导致的H₀偏移；未来亮标准汽笛可显著强化约束，尤其对千新星目录和扩展探测器网络。在所有预测配置中H₀的相对不确定度低于13.18%，最优情形的可低至0.21%，Ω_Λ在最优情形下可恢复至百分比精度。

Conclusion: 第三代标准汽笛能为超越ΛCDM的非动力学暗能量模型提供精确的互补检验，扩展Cuscuton模型有望通过未来引力波观测得到有效判别与约束。

Abstract: We investigate an analytically tractable sector of the Extended Cuscuton model, a non-dynamical dark-energy realization within the framework of viable Horndeski gravity. We focus on four benchmark submodels and constrain them with current background probes, namely cosmic chronometers, Type-Ia supernovae, and BAO, while imposing theoretical viability, Lunar Laser Ranging, and Big Bang Nucleosynthesis bounds. We then forecast third-generation bright-standard-siren constraints with Einstein Telescope and Cosmic Explorer networks, considering prompt-emission, afterglow, and kilonova counterparts. Current data already restrict the viable parameter space to small departures from $Λ$CDM and do not remove the calibration-driven offset between the CC+SN and CC+BAO determinations of $H_0$. In principle, future bright sirens substantially sharpen the constraints, especially for kilonova catalogues and extended detector networks. Across the forecast configurations, the relative uncertainty on $H_0$ remains below $13.18\%$ and can reach $0.21\%$ in the most constraining cases, while $Ω_Λ$ is recovered at the percent level in the best cases. These results show that third-generation standard sirens can provide a precise complementary test of non-dynamical dark energy beyond $Λ$CDM.

Abstract: In the standard model of cosmology, it is assumed that dark matter is pressureless with equation of state $w=0$ and dark energy has $w=-1$. We test these assumptions jointly using DESI DR2 distance measurements, including the recent Lyman-$α$ full-shape Alcock-Paczynski (AP) information, DES supernovae, and two complementary CMB treatment. With an alternative CMB treatment that marginalizes over the lensing spectrum, the corresponding constraints are $w_{dm}=0.000870^{+0.000408}_{-0.000410}$ and $w_{de}=-0.9353^{+0.0258}_{-0.0254}$. Both standard $Λ$CDM values are disfavored at approximately $2σ$ in the joint extension. Neither parameter departs significantly from its standard value when only that parameter is varied. This behavior arises because late-time distances favor $w_{de}>-1$, while maintaining the early-Universe physical matter density requires a compensating positive $w_{dm}$, which changes the mapping to the matter density today. Allowing dynamical dark energy clarify further on complexity of the situation: phantom crossing for dark energy makes $w_{dm}=0$ consistent with the data, whereas a positive $w_{dm}$ preference persists when crossing is forbidden. Interestingly, the Pad'e-$w$ parameterization that provides a flexible description of a class of quintessence models (with no phantom crossing), along with $w_{dm}$ free, is even mildly favored over the phantom-crossing $w_0w_a$ model according to both the best-fit $χ^2$ and the DIC under both CMB treatments. One can conclude that the apparent preference for phantom crossing may instead reflect deviations in the dark-matter sector rather than dark-energy dynamics alone. [abridged]

Abstract: Recent observational data arising from the DESI collaboration has hinted at a possible departure from the standard $Λ$CDM cosmological model, preferring instead the presence of a dynamical dark energy component. Specifically, the associated equation of state of the dark energy features a crossing into the so-called phantom regime, which is challenging to accommodate in canonical single scalar-field scenarios. However, this behavior can be effectively described by an interacting dark sector, where the specific dark energy equation of state remains above the phantom divide whilst the dark matter component deviates from the standard cold dark matter evolution. In this work, we explore this possibility in the context of an axion dark sector, where both the dark energy and dark matter are represented by two interacting axion-like fields. We show that given the required mass hierarchy for these fields to play such roles, their dynamics can be effectively placed in the coupled quintessence framework, where their motion follows from a sourced continuity equation in the fluid description. In this regime, we perform a statistical analysis of this scenario with current data, finding that a sub-Planckian dark energy axion decay constant stays well within the observational bounds without the need to fine-tune the associated field's initial conditions. We also perform a comparison with $Λ$CDM, where we find that the model provides a better fit to the data while staying competitive from a Bayesian perspective.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究PT对称非厄米多通道Kondo模型的精确可解性，理解非厄米性如何影响强关联杂质系统中的重整化群行为、对称性破缺和缺陷共形场论的预言。

Method: 采用精确Bethe Ansatz求解PT对称非厄米多通道Kondo模型，并利用推广的热力学Bethe Ansatz（thermodynamic Bethe Ansatz）计算杂质自由能和Affleck–Ludwig g函数；通过重整化群流分析各相的结构。

Result: 模型存在四种杂质相（过屏蔽Kondo、零模、YSR、局域矩），其中YSR相自发破缺PT对称。Kondo相中杂质熵从2ln2流向2ln[2cos(π/(n+2))]，与缺陷共形场论一致；零模相中零能杂质弦组织谱为多个激发塔；局域矩相中RG流循环回归未屏蔽局域矩不动点。在零模和局域矩相中，杂质熵是非单调的。

Conclusion: 作者推测广义Affleck–Ludwig g定理在Kondo相中成立，但精确解表明实数和与缺陷CFT一致的缺陷熵并不能保证重整化群不可逆性——在零模和局域矩相中杂质熵呈现非单调行为。

Abstract: We study a $\mathscr{PT}$-symmetric non-Hermitian multichannel Kondo model consisting of a pair of spin-$\frac12$ impurities coupled to $n$ conduction-electron channels through complex-conjugate Kondo couplings. The impurity renormalization-group (RG) flow is characterized by the Kondo scale $T_K$ and a dimensionless non-Hermiticity parameter $α$. As $α$ increases, the exact Bethe Ansatz solution exhibits four impurity phases: overscreened Kondo, zero mode, Yu--Shiba--Rusinov (YSR), and local moment. The Kondo, zero-mode, and local-moment phases are $\mathscr{PT}$-unbroken, whereas the YSR phase spontaneously breaks $\mathscr{PT}$ symmetry. Using a generalized thermodynamic Bethe Ansatz, we determine the impurity free energy and Affleck--Ludwig $g$-function throughout the $\mathscr{PT}$-unbroken phases. In the Kondo phase, the defect RG flow connects the ultraviolet and infrared conformal fixed points, with the impurity entropy flowing from $2\ln2$ to $2\ln\left[2\cos\left(\fracπ{n+2}\right)\right]$, in agreement with defect conformal field theory. In the zero-mode phase, zero-energy impurity strings reorganize the spectrum into multiple excitation towers, while in the local-moment phase, the RG flow becomes cyclic, returning to the unscreened local-moment fixed point. We conjecture that RG irreversibility, and hence a generalized Affleck--Ludwig $g$-theorem, survives throughout the Kondo phase. Our exact solution nevertheless shows that a real spectrum and defect entropies consistent with defect CFT do not guarantee RG irreversibility: the impurity entropy is non-monotonic in both the zero-mode and local-moment phases.

</details>

Abstract: Low dimensionality enhances quantum fluctuations, triggering novel states of quantum matter to emerge. In real materials, low dimensionality usually arises from spatially strongly anisotropic couplings. Here, we demonstrate a different mechanism: in two-dimensional systems with coupled alternating ferromagnetic (FM) and antiferromagnetic (AFM) spin-$1/2$ chains, an applied magnetic field may drive a dimensional reduction. Under magnetic field, the FM chains polarize and stiffen, suppressing the propagation of transverse AFM fluctuations from one chain to another, and effectively induce one-dimensional behavior at low energies. For a model describing botallackite, Cu$_2$(OH)$_3$Br, quantum Monte Carlo dynamics show that beyond a critical magnetic field, the low-energy spectrum reduces to that of a one-dimensional AFM Heisenberg spin-$1/2$ chain with field-dependent incommensurate two-spinon fluctuations, providing clear signatures for inelastic neutron scattering.

Abstract: The subsystem-size dependence of ground-state entanglement entropy and its crossover to thermal entropy as a function of temperature are well understood for one-dimensional (1D) gapless systems described by conformal field theory (CFT), and for free fermions with a Fermi surface in any dimension. However, little is known about the entanglement entropy for gapless fermionic systems without quasi-particles, such as a strange metal. Here we study the entanglement entropy of fermions in a solvable large-$N$ 1D lattice model akin to the Yukawa-Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (Yukawa SYK) model. In this model, two Fermi points are coupled to scalar bosons via spatially random Yukawa interactions, providing a solvable model of a strange metal when the bosons become critical at a quantum critical point. We exactly compute the second Rényi entropy of fermions in a spatial subregion in this model. Our results unravel crucial role of intra-subregion entanglement between fermionic and bosonic degrees of freedom along with the inter-subregion entanglement in understanding the ground states of such strongly coupled fermion-boson systems. We show that the crossover from thermal entropy to entanglement entropy, is captured by a single scaling ansatz, that collapses the second Rényi entropy of fermions for different subregion sizes and temperatures into a single universal curve. We further show that the universal scaling curve for the critical strange metal is well described by the standard CFT formula, albeit with an effective central charge substantially larger than the non-interacting value.

In this work, we show that on a programmable quantum hardware platform, the more exotic FQH excitations are the less costly ones to prepare: clustered non-Abelian FQH states admit parallel quantum preparation circuits whose two-qubit depth is independent of system size, while constructing the more common Abelian Laughlin state requires a sequential circuit chain with linear depth. The centerpiece of this work is our new systematic framework for cataloging possible FQH states and preparing them on quantum circuits at unprecedented scale and variety. Our prepared parafermionic Read--Rezayi $\mathbb{Z}_3$ state holds depth 3 from 8 to 118 qubits, and full root sampling extends to a 154-qubit, 104-electron Read--Rezayi $\mathbb{Z}_4$ state. In all, our demonstrated 18-family catalog of prepared FQH states extends to all 156 qubits of an IBM Heron processor, limited only by existing hardware scale. Measurements on the prepared states recover the expected fractional quasihole charges, with the charge estimator exact in every symmetry-selected shot for the clustered states, and braiding data of the non-Abelian $e/4$ quasihole measured via interferometric extensions. Our work establishes a scalable route to studying FQH physics on quantum processors and opens new avenues for preparing and probing non-Abelian topological matter far beyond the reach of conventional platforms.

We introduce the fractional inhomogeneous Rouse model (FIRM), which uses a generalized Langevin equation driven by fractional Gaussian noise of exponent $α$, while retaining heterogeneous bead friction to represent sticky cross-links. For homogeneous chains, it recovers two power-law regimes, $t^{-α/2}$ and $t^{-2α}$, on either side of the terminal relaxation time. It also accommodates both Arrhenius and non-Arrhenius temperature dependence in the rheological activation energy. We derive expressions for dynamic properties such as mean-squared displacement and dielectric response and outline how generalized memory kernels extend the framework to real materials. Together, these results suggest novel ways in which data from rheology, dielectric spectroscopy, scattering, and other experimental methods may be incorporated into a chemistry-specific molecular model.

These findings establish a unified strain-based framework for viscous resuspension and clarify the role of particle anisotropy in dense suspension dynamics.

Abstract: We investigate the structural and dynamical behavior of methane water systems under nanoconfinement using molecular dynamics simulations across pore widths from 1 to 5 nm. Radial distribution functions show that three dimensional correlations are suppressed under strong confinement, whereas lateral ordering persists, indicating a reduction in the effective dimensionality of structural organization. Transport properties reflect the same structural competition. Parallel diffusion is nonmonotonic with pore size, while perpendicular motion is subdiffusive due to confinement induced trapping and heterogeneity. Methane exhibits stronger subdiffusion and remains dynamically coupled to the water matrix. A characteristic confinement length scale emerges at which structural ordering, dynamical heterogeneity, and solvent solute decoupling are simultaneously maximized. At strong confinement, three dimensional correlations are suppressed, leading to dimensional reduction, frustrated ordering, and inhibited nucleation.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We study the prediction of plastic activity in the confined channel flow of two-dimensional amor- phous soft particles around a circular obstacle. Using datasets generated with a particle-based bubble model, we formulate the prediction problem within two supervised-learning frameworks: re- gression of the non-affine displacement and binary classification of neighbor change events. A key technical challenge is that the obstacle and the confining walls explicitly break translational and rotational symmetries. We address this issue by introducing additional structural descriptors that encode the positions of particles relative to these boundaries. Starting from simple linear models, we systematically increase the complexity of the learning framework by considering a logarithmic transformation of the target variable, the incorporation of particle-size information, the addition of symmetry-breaking obstacle and wall descriptors, the coarse-graining of local structural descrip- tors, and nonlinear neural-network models. We find that the obstacle and wall descriptors provide the largest improvement in predictive performance. Nevertheless, the models considered here cap- ture mainly the overall localization of plastic activity near the obstacle and do not fully reproduce its detailed heterogeneous pattern in individual configurations. A perturbation analysis indicates that this heterogeneity is robustly encoded in the initial structure, suggesting that further progress requires more expressive structural representations and machine-learning architectures.

</details>


### [61] [Scaling behavior in non-reciprocal and odd conserved dynamics near criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05027)
*Martin Kjøllesdal Johnsrud,Giulia Pisegna,Ramin Golestanian*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文利用微扰动力学重整化群（perturbative dynamical renormalization group）技术，研究了非互易Cahn-Hilliard（NRCH）模型在临界点附近的标度行为。研究发现结构相关性和动力学相关性受不同关联长度控制，二者均在临界点发散但遵循不同的标度律：结构关联长度始终由温度控制并服从经典Wilson-Fisher临界指数，而动力学关联长度则表现出多个标度区间，可由温度或非互逆耦合主导。临界点对应具有奇性迁移率的守恒型类平衡动力学，即奇Cahn-Hilliard（OCH）模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互易性（non-reciprocity）被广泛视为微观尺度上非平衡活性的普遍表现，涉及酶、胶体、液滴及光控活性胶体和机器人超材料等多种活性物质系统。NRCH模型作为描述具有非互逆相互作用的二元守恒物种混合物的常用最小模型，其临界行为尚未被系统研究，理解其标度特性对揭示活性系统调控相分离和时空斑图形成的机制具有重要意义。

Method: 采用微扰动力学重整化群（perturbative dynamical renormalization group）技术，研究NRCH模型在临界点附近的标度行为。模型由类温度调控参数（可触发相分离）和非互逆耦合（作为非平衡活性的内源来源，破坏宇称和时间反演对称性）共同刻画，分析结构相关性与动力学相关性的分界及各自关联长度的标度律。

Result: 结构相关性和动力学相关性由不同的关联长度控制，二者在临界点均发散但遵循不同的标度律。结构关联长度始终受温度控制，服从经典Wilson-Fisher临界指数；动力学关联长度表现出多个标度区间，在这些区间中温度或非互逆耦合均可作为主导调控参数，伴随新的临界指数表征其发散行为。临界点对应守恒型类平衡动力学，具有奇性迁移率，即奇Cahn-Hilliard（OCH）模型。

Conclusion: NRCH模型在临界点附近展现出结构-动力学标度解耦（structural-dynamical scaling decoupling）的丰富行为，结构关联遵循经典Wilson-Fisher标度，而动力学关联呈现由温度或非互逆耦合主导的多重标度区间。该发现对理解生物系统如何通过催化反应速率乃至一般代谢过程作为调控参数来控制相分离和时空斑图形成具有重要启示意义。

Abstract: In recent years, non-reciprocity has been explored as a ubiquitous manifestation of non-equilibrium activity at the microscopic scales for various active matter systems, from mixtures of chemically active enzymes, colloids, and droplets, to engineered light-controlled active colloids and robotic meta-materials. A commonly used minimal model to describe the dynamics of a binary mixture of conserved species with non-reciprocal interactions is the non-reciprocal Cahn-Hilliard (NRCH) model. The model is characterized by a temperature-like tuning parameter, which can trigger phase separation, and a non-reciprocal coupling, which can lead to the formation of spatio-temporal patterns, as it represents an intrinsic source of non-equilibrium activity and breaks parity and time-reversal symmetries. Here, we study the scaling behavior of the NRCH model near the critical point using perturbative dynamical renormalization group techniques. We find that structural and dynamical correlations are controlled by different correlation lengths, both of which diverge at the critical point, but governed by different scaling laws. In particular, while the structural correlations are always controlled by temperature and the classical Wilson-Fisher critical exponent, the dynamical correlation length exhibits multiple scaling regimes in which either temperature or non-reciprocal coupling can dominate as the key tuning parameter, with a new critical exponent characterizing the divergence. The critical point corresponds to a conserved equilibrium-like dynamics with odd mobility, which we denote as the odd Cahn-Hilliard (OCH) model. Our findings may have important implications on how living systems can control phase separation and spatio-temporal pattern formation using the rates of catalytic reactions, and in general metabolism, as control parameters.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 实验结果在最小化系统中为电容器模型提供了强有力的实验支持，并证实导电材料与绝缘材料表现出不同的起电行为，有助于厘清竞争性摩擦起电模型之间的争议。

Abstract: The mechanisms governing triboelectric charging between insulating particles of the same material remain an open question in nonequilibrium physics, with several competing models proposed to explain observed charging behaviour. Here, we investigate charge evolution in a minimal system consisting of two acoustically levitated particles undergoing repeated binary collisions. To quantify particle charge transfer, purpose-built Faraday cage picoammeters were developed and calibrated. The MultiLev acoustic levitation system was used alongside Ultraino simulations to generate transducer control signals, enabling controlled collision and separation of polystyrene (PS) particles. Although individual collision events exhibit stochastic charge transfer, described by skew-normal distributions, we demonstrate for the first time that the cumulative charge evolution of an individual acoustically levitated particle pair follows the saturation behaviour predicted by the condenser model of triboelectric charging, with fits achieving $R^2 > 0.99$. Under identical conditions, conductive graphite-coated PS particles also undergo charge transfer, but accumulate substantially less charge, consistent with the distinct charging behaviour expected for conductive materials.

### [63] [Tetrahedral linkage as an intrinsic measure of glycan antifreeze behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05130)
*Aakash Kumar,Shoumik Saha,Dilip Gersappe*

Abstract: Antifreeze materials prevent ice-formation by disrupting the ice-formation by binding to certain ice-planes. Cellulose, the most abundant biopolymer, has shown the ability to bind to ice-planes but the exact mechanism of this binding is far from being understood. Molecular dynamics simulations are used to investigate the hydration water of chains of cellulose-type glycans and its significance in the expression of the antifreeze behavior of sugar-derivatives found in some antifreeze materials. We find that glycans are able to prevent water from freezing near its surface by preventing their rearrangement to achieve a highly tetrahedral structure at temperatures well-below the freezing point of water. This validates our hypothesis on the role of tetrahedral coordination based on previous $\textit{ab initio}$ calculations that demonstrated cellulose prefers to bind to ice basal and prismatic planes using a tetrahedral geometry. Our findings suggest that the tetrahedral ordering of water around glycans is the key to understanding and designing cellulose-based antifreeze materials.

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文通过第一性原理计算结合自旋哈密顿量建模，研究了静水压（hydrostatic pressure）对关联绝缘体交变磁体（altermagnet）La₂O₃Mn₂Se₂中电子和磁振子输运的影响，发现压缩可显著增强交换各向异性（exchange anisotropy），使自旋塞贝克效应（spin Seebeck effect）响应提升近四倍，同时保持反常霍尔效应（anomalous Hall effect）的磁对称性选择规则并产生能量依赖的符号反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁体（altermagnet）作为新型磁性材料，其电子和磁振子输运特性的调控机制尚不清楚，特别是如何在不破坏对称性的前提下同时增强磁性和电子输运响应。作者希望探索一种保持对称性的外部调控手段，以揭示交换各向异性在增强磁振子响应中的微观作用。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）结合自旋哈密顿量建模（spin-Hamiltonian modeling），对La₂O₃Mn₂Se₂在0至40 GPa静水压下的电子结构和磁性交换相互作用进行系统研究，并分析压力对自旋塞贝克效应和反常霍尔电导的修饰作用。

Result: 从0到40 GPa的压缩使竞争性次近邻交换相互作用之间的不等价性显著增强，|J₂ₐ−J₂ᵦ|从1.97增大到9.38 meV，同时保持补偿反铁磁基态不变。交换各向异性的增强使两个手性磁振子支（chiral magnon branches）的动量依赖劈裂放大，导致100 K下纵向磁振子驱动的自旋塞贝克响应从3.68×10⁻¹增强至1.36 meV/K，提升近四倍；静水压保持反常霍尔效应的磁对称性选择规则，但重新分布电子贝里曲率（Berry curvature），使反常霍尔电导出现显著的能量依赖符号反转。

Conclusion: 交换各向异性被确认为压力增强磁振子响应的微观机制，静水压被证明是同时调控绝缘体交变磁体中电子和磁振子输运的有效手段。

Abstract: Hydrostatic pressure provides a symmetry-preserving route to engineer electronic and magnonic transport in the correlated insulating altermagnet La$_2$O$_3$Mn$_2$Se$_2$. Using first-principles calculations combined with spin-Hamiltonian modeling, we show that compression from 0 to 40 GPa markedly enhances the inequivalence between the competing second-neighbor exchange interactions, increasing $|J_{2a}-J_{2b}|$ from 1.97 to 9.38 meV while preserving the compensated antiferromagnetic ground state. The resulting exchange anisotropy amplifies the momentum-dependent splitting between the two chiral magnon branches, yielding a nearly fourfold enhancement of the longitudinal magnon-driven spin Seebeck response at 100 K, from $3.68\times10^{-1}$ to $1.36$ meV/K. In contrast, hydrostatic pressure preserves the magnetic-symmetry selection rules governing the anomalous Hall effect while redistributing the electronic Berry curvature, producing pronounced energy-dependent sign reversals in the anomalous Hall conductivity. These results identify exchange anisotropy as the microscopic mechanism underlying the pressure-enhanced magnon response and establish hydrostatic pressure as an effective means of simultaneously controlling electronic and magnonic transport in insulating altermagnets.

Result: 发现弱面内各向异性使声学支激发出现能隙，该能隙对外场呈非线性依赖并在B=B_c处闭合；利用Yuan等人非弹性中子散射实验所得参数，推得Néel温度约52 K。

Abstract: The Green's function theory of Lymar' and Rudoi [Theor. Math. Phys. vol. 21, 990 (1974)] is generalized to the case of multiple intra- and inter-sublattice magnetic interactions in a collinear spin-half antiferromagnet and applied to magnetic excitations in Bi$_2$CuO$_4$. The spin Hamiltonian includes both the out-of-plane and in-plane symmetric anisotropy terms and the Zeeman term, which describes the interaction with an external magnetic field $B$ applied along the Néel vector. Within the spin-wave approximation we calculate spin excitation dispersion, antiferromagnetic resonance frequencies and the critical field $B_c$ of the spin-flop metamagnetic transition. A weak in-plane anisotropy is shown to result in a gap in the acoustic-like branch of the excitations. The gap nonlinearly depends on the external field and closes at $B=B_c$. An expression for the Neel temperature in the Tyablikov random phase approximation at zero field is derived. For the parameters derived from the recent inelastic neutron scattering study by Yuan et al. [Phys. Rev. B vol. 103, 134436 (2021)] it gives $T_{\rm N}\approx 52$~K.

### [66] [Computational Microstructure Analysis of Sintered Ceramics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04216)
*Nikhil Dhanankam,Deeksha Kodangal,Rajendra K. Bordia,Ulf D. Schiller*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从扫描电子显微镜（SEM）图像中自动提取烧结陶瓷样品微观结构物理性质的工作流程，包括孔隙率、固相分数、晶粒尺寸分布和孔径分布。通过拓扑滤波与Sauvola阈值化方法的组合，该流程克服了SEM图像中由于晶粒和孔隙相强度范围重叠导致单峰直方图的挑战，并在1200°C和1400°C烧结样品上分别达到95.14%和99.85%的交并比（Intersection over Union, IoU）分数。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从微观结构图像中手动提取物理性质来描述材料是一个劳动密集型过程，亟需自动化方法来提高效率，并为机器学习生成材料数据集提供关键预处理步骤。

Method: 作者构建了自动化流水线，评估了多种降噪和局部阈值化（local thresholding）方法用于SEM图像分割。最终采用拓扑滤波（topological filtering）与Sauvola阈值化相结合的策略，实现晶粒相和孔隙相的精确分割，进而提取孔隙率、固相分数、晶粒尺寸分布和孔径分布等物理性质。

Result: 该自动化流水线在1200°C和1400°C烧结样品的验证中，与人工分析结果相比分别获得了95.14%和99.85%的IoU分数，表明分割精度高且方法可靠。

Conclusion: 该工作流为从SEM图像中自动提取微观结构性质提供了一种高效手段，是生成用于机器学习材料数据集的关键步骤。

Abstract: Characterizing materials through manual extraction of physical properties from microstructure images is a laborious process. This work presents a workflow to extract porosity, solid fraction, grain size distribution, and pore size distribution from scanning electron microscopy (SEM) images of sintered ceramic samples using an automated pipeline. The primary challenge for extracting physical properties from SEM images is the presence of unimodal histograms in SEM images as a result of the overlapping intensity ranges for the grain and pore phases. We evaluated several different methods for noise reduction and local thresholding of SEM images. We find that topological filtering in combination with Sauvola thresholding enables segmentation and extraction of physical property data from SEM images. We validated the automated pipeline by comparing our results with the results of manual analyses performed for samples sintered at 1200$^o$C and 1400$^o$C and achieved an Intersection over Union (IoU) score of 95.14% and 99.85%, respectively. The workflow provides an efficient means to automatically extract microstructure properties from SEM images as a crucial step in generating materials datasets for machine learning.
