### [67] [First-principles predictions of carrier mobility with record accuracy using GW perturbation theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04219)
*Nick Pant,Sabyasachi Tiwari,Steven G. Louie,Zhenglu Li,Feliciano Giustino*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文展示了在求解从头算玻尔兹曼输运方程（Boltzmann transport equation）时，将多体GW修正同时引入电子能带结构和电子-声子耦合，可使基准半导体（Si、GaAs、GaP、金刚石、SiC）的电子迁移率平均绝对相对误差降至11%，而忽略GW修正可能导致迁移率误差超过50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迁移率（carrier mobility）的准确预测对下一代电子材料的设计与发现至关重要。然而，现有从头算（ab initio）方法在密度泛函理论（DFT）层面对电子-声子相互作用的近似处理存在局限，亟需更高精度的计算方法。

Method: 将多体GW修正同时纳入电子能带结构和电子-声子耦合（electron-phonon coupling），并将其应用于从头算玻尔兹曼输运方程的求解，以计算载流子迁移率。

Result: 在Si、GaAs、GaP、金刚石和SiC等基准半导体中，电子迁移率的平均绝对相对误差仅为11%；而忽略GW对电子-声子相互作用的修正会导致迁移率误差超过50%。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调了多体GW自能效应（GW self-energy effects）在载流子输运模拟中的重要性，并为理解多体电子-声子相互作用如何主导晶态固体中电荷输运提供了基础性见解。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of carrier mobility is critical for the discovery and design of next-generation electronic materials. Despite sustained progress, state-of-the-art ab initio methods remain limited by the approximate treatment of electron-phonon interactions at the density functional theory level. Here, we demonstrate that incorporating many-body GW corrections to both the electronic band structure and electron--phonon couplings when solving the ab initio Boltzmann transport equation yields a mean absolute relative error of just 11% for electron mobilities across benchmark semiconductors, including Si, GaAs, GaP, diamond, and SiC. The common practice of neglecting GW corrections to the electron--phonon interaction can lead to mobility errors exceeding 50%. The present findings highlight the importance of many-body GW self-energy effects in carrier transport simulations, and provides fundamental insights into how many-body electron--phonon interactions govern charge transport in crystalline solids.

</details>


### [68] [A phase field model of coupled crack and dislocations: emission, blunting, and the necessity of dissipative toughening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04781)
*Khanh Chau Le,Thi My Kieu Tran*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一个相场（phase field）模型，将宏观裂纹单晶中的裂纹与几何必需位错（geometrically necessary dislocations）统一纳入单一能量泛函，通过能量最小化与闭式积分判据决定位错成核，并揭示了晶粒尺寸相关的屈服应力、两阶段裂纹扩展响应及断裂增韧机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统点强度条件无法捕捉位错成核的尺寸效应，需要一种基于能量泛函的框架，使裂纹与位错从同一能量函数出发，从而统一描述断裂与塑性行为。

Method: 构建包含裂纹和几何必需位错的相场（phase field）模型，二者源自单一能量泛函；通过沿滑移弦（slip chords）求值的闭式积分判据决定位错成核；采用全非光滑求解器（full non-smooth solver）验证耦合计算。

Result: 该判据给出点强度条件无法产生的尺寸效应，即晶粒尺寸相关的屈服应力；抑制滑移时模型重现Griffith断裂，抑制断裂时数值解与闭式成核判据误差小于4%；耦合计算显示两阶段响应：位错带从缺口尖端发射并钝化屏蔽裂纹，提高起裂载荷；裂纹扩展后位错带愈合，同号位错簇随裂尖移动，反号位错壁钉扎于晶界，耗散断裂阻力等于弹性断裂阻力。

Conclusion: 在纯能量、无耗散极限下，位错发射屏蔽裂纹但不产生增韧；增韧需要耗散，须通过后续引入的位错运动阈值阻力（threshold resistance）来表征。

Abstract: We propose a phase field model of a macrocracked single crystal in which the crack and the geometrically necessary dislocations descend from a single energy functional. Energy minimization alone then decides dislocation nucleation, through an integral criterion evaluated in closed form along slip chords. The criterion yields a size effect inaccessible to point-wise strength conditions: a grain-size-dependent yield stress. With slip suppressed the model reproduces Griffith fracture; with fracture suppressed, the nucleation load measured by the full non-smooth solver agrees with the closed-form nucleation criterion to four percent. The coupled computations produce a two-stage response: at loads an order of magnitude below cleavage, dislocation bands emitted from the notch tip blunt and shield it, raising the initiation load; once the crack grows, the bands heal; a compact cluster of like-signed dislocations travels with the tip, its canceling partner walls pinned at the grain boundary, and the dissipated fracture resistance equals the elastic one. In the purely energetic, dissipationless limit, emission shields the crack but does not toughen it; toughening requires dissipation, incorporated in the sequel through the threshold resistance to dislocation motion.

</details>


### [69] [Electron correlation in semiconductors and insulators via symbolic regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04291)
*Nick Pant,Viet-Anh Ha,Donghwan Kim,Feliciano Giustino*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出利用符号回归（symbolic regression）将GW自能近似为Kohn-Sham描述符的紧凑解析函数，仅需一次有序相GW计算即可学习，可在对称性破缺体系下保持精度，使包含数千原子复杂材料的GW计算成本降至与半局域密度泛函理论（semi-local density functional theory）相当的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测材料的准粒子能量需要对电子自能（electron self-energy）进行昂贵的数值计算，这限制了GW计算仅适用于具有小晶胞的有序体系。

Method: 采用符号回归方法，利用物理上合理的Kohn-Sham描述符构造GW自能的紧凑解析近似函数，并在有序相中通过单次GW计算学习这些表达式。

Result: 该方法在量子涨落、热涨落、弹性形变和非晶无序引起的对称性破缺条件下仍保持精度，并在共价半导体、离子绝缘体和二维材料中验证了准确性，实现了对包含数千原子复杂材料的常规GW计算。

Conclusion: 符号回归被确立为一种可行的途径，用于构建预测性、可解释且可迁移的多体电子结构（many-body electronic structure）模型。

Abstract: Predicting quasiparticle energies in materials requires expensive numerical evaluations of the electron self-energy. This limits calculations to ordered systems with small unit cells. Here, using symbolic regression, we show that the GW self-energy can be accurately approximated with compact analytical functions of physically motivated Kohn-Sham descriptors. These expressions can be learned from a single GW calculation in the ordered phase and remain accurate under symmetry breaking induced by quantum and thermal fluctuations, elastic deformations, and amorphous disorder. This development enables routine GW calculations of complex materials with thousands of atoms at a computational cost comparable to semi-local density functional theory. We demonstrate the accuracy of this approach for covalent semiconductors, ionic insulators, and two-dimensional materials. These results establish symbolic regression as a viable route to predictive, interpretable, and transferable many-body electronic structure models.

</details>


### [70] [Unveiling the Role of Friction in Coarse-Grained Clay: A Hybrid Framework Integrating Long-Range Interactions and Granular Contact Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04359)
*Wang-Qi Xu,Yijie Wang,Zhen-Yu Yin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种混合粗粒化分子动力学（coarse-grained molecular dynamics, CGMD）框架，显式耦合长程Buckingham势与Hertz颗粒接触力学，系统揭示了粒间摩擦（inter-particle friction）在黏土力学行为中的关键作用。单向压缩试验表明，显式摩擦可锁定滑动界面并显著提升承载力，而无摩擦模型仅依赖几何互锁，最终出现非物理的类流体屈服。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒间物理化学力在黏土行为中占主导地位，促使研究者广泛采用粗粒化分子动力学模拟。然而，由于方法学限制，粒间摩擦长期被忽略，且该忽略对模拟精度的影响程度尚是未解问题。

Result: 等向压缩下，模型的可压缩性及压缩指数（compression index, Cc）与宏观岩土观察一致。参数分析发现颗粒接触的粘弹性阻尼主导结构演化，高阻尼抑制致密化并导致无序高孔隙比构型；热涨落分析表明精确温控必不可少。单轴压缩试验证实粒间摩擦具关键重要性：显式摩擦锁定滑动界面并维持显著更高载荷，而无摩擦模型仅靠几何互锁，最终呈现非物理的类流体屈服。

Conclusion: 该框架将原子势与接触力学相衔接，凸显了粒间摩擦的基础性作用，为未来黏土集合体的多尺度模拟提供了重要指导准则。

Abstract: Given the predominant role of inter-particle physicochemical forces in governing clay behavior, researchers have increasingly utilized coarse-grained molecular dynamics (CGMD) simulations. However, inter-particle friction has been historically overlooked due to methodological limitations, and the extent to which this omission influences simulation accuracy remains an unresolved question. This study proposes a novel hybrid CGMD framework explicitly coupling long-range Buckingham potential with Hertzian granular contact mechanics. A baseline model was validated via isotropic compression, where the resulting compressibility and derived compression index (Cc) aligned with macroscopic geotechnical observations. Parametric analyses revealed that viscoelastic damping of particle contacts governs structural evolution. Elevated damping suppresses densification, trapping platelets in disorganized, high-void-ratio configurations. Furthermore, evaluating the interplay with thermal fluctuations underscores the necessity of precise temperature control to prevent such unphysical kinetic trapping. Finally, uniaxial compression tests demonstrate the critical importance of inter-particle friction. Explicit friction locks sliding interfaces and sustains significantly higher loads compared to frictionless models; the latter rely solely on geometric interlocking and ultimately exhibit unphysical fluid-like yielding. By bridging atomistic potentials with contact mechanics, this framework highlights the fundamental role of the inter-particle friction and offers essential guidelines for future multi-scale simulations of clay assemblies.

</details>


### [71] [Monolithic integration of optically anisotropic GeSe-based films on GaAs by templated solid-phase epitaxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04418)
*Kira J. Martin,Autumn Y. Lee,Pranav Mahaadev,Pooja D. Reddy,Kelly Xiao,Tri Nguyen,Ashlee M. García,Aaron M. Lindenberg,Kunal Mukherjee*

  <summary>Details</summary>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者从第一性原理出发，对自旋诱导的线性磁电响应进行动力学计算，系统比较了四种量子多体方法：独立粒子近似（IPA）、随机相位近似（RPA）、含时密度泛函理论（TDDFT）和Bethe–Salpeter方程（BSE），并将这些方法应用于原型磁电材料Cr₂O₃，与已有实验和理论研究结果进行对比。

Result: 研究发现：IPA和RPA无法复现之前报道的自旋诱导响应的有限静态极限；BSE框架下磁电谱中出现明显的激子共振，与实验定性一致；还识别出一个与横向自旋磁化率的极点重合的类磁振子（magnon-like）峰，该峰在光学吸收中基本为暗态，凸显了磁电响应对自旋激发的敏感性；TDDFT将该模式置于预期低能磁振子区域附近，并给出可观的静态自旋诱导响应。

Conclusion: 这些理论框架各自捕捉了动态磁电响应的互补方面：低能集体自旋激发（如磁振子）是恢复静态极限所必需的，而电子-空穴相互作用则对于复现激子共振至关重要。该工作为动态磁电响应的第一性原理计算提供了方法学上的系统比较与指导。

Abstract: The magnetoelectric (ME) effect provides a promising pathway for controlling magnetic functionalities using electric fields. While first-principles methods for the static linear ME response are well established, comparable approaches for the frequency-dependent response remain less developed, despite experiments showing pronounced finite-frequency resonances. Here, we investigate the dynamical spin-induced linear ME response from first principles and systematically compare the independent-particle approximation (IPA), random-phase approximation (RPA), time-dependent density functional theory (TDDFT), and the Bethe-Salpeter equation (BSE). We apply these methods to the prototypical ME material Cr$_2$O$_3$ and compare the results with available experimental and theoretical studies. We find that the IPA and RPA fail to reproduce the previously reported finite static limit of the spin-induced response. Within the BSE framework, pronounced excitonic resonances emerge in the ME spectrum, in qualitative agreement with experiment. We also identify a magnon-like peak that coincides with a pole of the transverse spin susceptibility while remaining essentially dark in optical absorption, highlighting the sensitivity of the ME response to spin excitations. TDDFT places this mode closer to the expected low-energy magnonic regime and yields a sizable static spin-induced response. Our results show that these frameworks capture complementary aspects of the dynamical ME response. Low-energy collective spin excitations are required to recover the static limit, whereas electron-hole interactions are essential for reproducing the excitonic resonances.

Abstract: The mechanical properties of ceramics under extreme conditions directly impact applications ranging from shielding spacecrafts, designing plasma facing materials in nuclear fusion to understanding the rheology of deep planetary interiors. Here, we use polycrystalline MgO as a model ceramic to understand the high-pressure-temperature mechanical behaviour of such materials under extreme strain rates. We use laser-driven shock compression up to 175(15) GPa on the principal Hugoniot along with ultrafast diagnostics at the European X-ray Free Electron Laser to probe the dominant deformation mechanisms with changing P -T conditions. These near-instantaneous time-resolved snapshots, coupled with elasto-viscoplastic self-consistent (EVPSC) simulations, strongly suggest that MgO attains plastic regime in the nanoseconds scale accompanied by a pressure-mediated change in dominant slip system between 95 and 175 GPa. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）常用于区分LLZO的导电立方相与低导电四方相，但其光谱差异的原子学起源及其与锂离子输运的直接联系尚未被阐明，这限制了拉曼光谱作为微观动力学探针的潜力。

Method: 采用MD-Raman方法，将机器学习分子动力学（machine-learning molecular dynamics）与第一性原理极化率（first-principles polarizability）计算相结合，对LLZO的四方相、立方相及Ta掺杂变体分别计算拉曼光谱，并与实验拉曼光谱进行对比；同时通过对称性分辨分析（symmetry-resolved analysis）解析实验峰对应的振动贡献。

Result: 研究发现，LLZO不同变体间截然不同的离子输运行为编码于锂亚晶格的振动动力学中，并在拉曼光谱中呈现独特特征。对称性分辨分析表明，实验观测的拉曼峰并非对应单个简正模式（normal mode），而是由多个对称允许振动的重叠贡献共同产生，这对传统的峰归属（peak assignment）方法构成了挑战。

Conclusion: 通过将实验可及的拉曼特征与底层原子尺度动力学显式关联，研究证明拉曼光谱能够超越经验性的相鉴别，成为锂石榴石电解质中锂离子动力学的微观探针。

Abstract: Lithium lanthanum zirconate (LLZO) garnets are among the most promising solid electrolytes for next-generation batteries owing to their high ionic conductivity, chemical stability, and compatibility with lithium metal. Raman spectroscopy is commonly employed to distinguish the highly conductive cubic phase from the poorly conductive tetragonal phase of LLZO, yet the atomistic origin of these spectral differences and their direct connection to Li-ion transport remain unresolved. Here, we close this gap by comparing computed and experimental Raman spectra for the tetragonal, cubic, and Ta-doped variants of LLZO, with the computed spectra obtained from the MD-Raman approach that combines machine-learning molecular dynamics with first-principles polarizability calculations. We show that the contrasting ionic transport behavior across these LLZO variants is encoded in the vibrational dynamics of the lithium sublattice and gives rise to distinct features in their Raman spectra. A symmetry-resolved analysis further reveals that experimentally observed Raman peaks do not correspond to individual normal modes, but instead arise from overlapping contributions of multiple symmetry-allowed vibrations, challenging conventional peak-assignment approaches. By explicitly connecting experimentally accessible Raman signatures to the underlying atomic-scale dynamics, our results show how Raman spectroscopy can move beyond empirical phase identification toward a microscopic probe of Li-ion dynamics in lithium garnet electrolytes.

</details>

Result: 测得MXene薄片与金探针间的界面剪切应力为399 MPa，并观察到纳米磨损局域化发生在MXene薄片中心区域。透射电子显微镜和电子能量损失谱结果证实，摩擦膜中的MXene薄片在磨损后仍保留了其结晶性。

Abstract: The demand for novel self-powering and sustainable electronics requires major efforts in identifying new advanced materials for nano-applications. MXene have gathered attention due to their electronic and mechanical properties, however, their nanomechanical compliance against a metal-like interface is still not clearly identified. In this work, we employ atomic force microscopy to characterize the nanomechanical properties of a self-assembled thin flake of titanium carbide MXene (Ti3C2Tx) against a gold probe. The investigation returns an interfacial shear stress of 399 MPa, and the observation of nano-wear localized in the center of the MXene flake. Nevertheless, MXene flakes retained their crystallinity in the tribofilms as confirmed by transmission electron microscopy and electron energy loss spectroscopy. The outcomes of this work set the basis for the use of Ti3C2Tx in novel nano harvesting systems involving metal interfaces (e.g., tribovoltaic nanogenerators) with large scope in nanoelectronics, wearable sensing, electric vehicles, and robotics.

### [77] [Performance Analysis of Double Perovskite-Based Solar Cells Using SCAPS-1D Simulation: A brief review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04736)
*H. Laltlanmawii,Lalrem Kima,Mahabur Rahman,Md. Ferdous Rahman,Dilshod Nematov,S. Bhattarai,C. V. M. Chaturvedi,Yazen M. Alawaideh,A. Laref,D. P. Rai*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文讨论了无铅双钙钛矿（lead-free double perovskites）太阳能电池的发展现状，指出其功率转换效率（power conversion efficiency, PCE）仍低于含铅钙钛矿，并重点分析了1D数值模拟软件SCAPS-1D在太阳能电池研究中作为低成本快速优化工具的优势，以及其仅限于1D配置、无法纳入原子相互作用和3D效应、且依赖输入参数准确性的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无铅双钙钛矿是下一代太阳能电池技术的重要方向，具有低毒性、高稳定性和良好光电潜力，但目前实验制备的器件在实际应用中的功率转换效率（PCE）仍低于含铅钙钛矿，需要高效手段找出制约性能的关键因素。

Method: 采用1D数值模拟软件SCAPS-1D作为研究工具，通过快速仿真来测试新型器件架构、优化大量光伏器件的性能，无需进行实时实验即可预估太阳能电池的制备方案及PCE。

Result: SCAPS-1D凭借其用户友好性和数秒内完成仿真的速度，成为太阳能电池研究领域广泛使用且功能强大的工具，能够节约时间和成本。但其主要局限在于仅支持1D构型，无法整合原子尺度的相互作用及3D效应，且仿真结果的有效性完全取决于用户提供的输入参数精度。

Conclusion: 数值模拟作为连接实验与理论、提供预测性信息的低成本途径，在无铅钙钛矿太阳能电池研发中发挥重要作用；SCAPS-1D虽高效便捷，但其1D局限性及对输入参数精度的强依赖要求研究者在使用时审慎考量，以避免结果偏差。

Abstract: Lead-free double perovskites are among the rapidly developing next-generation solar cell technologies, providing the required low toxicity, stability, as well as high optoelectronic potential. So far, experimentally prepared lead-free perovskite solar cell devices are reported to have low power conversion efficiency (PCE) for practical application as compared to the lead-based perovskites. In recent years, numerical simulations have emerged as a cost-effective approach that plays a crucial role in expediting scientific research, can bridge the gap between experiment and theory, and provide predictive information regarding the preparation of solar cells and their PCEs without undergoing real-time experiments. The tools, such as 1D numerical simulation software SCAPS-1D, are now needed to test newer architectures and determine what exactly is holding them back. So far in the field of solar cell research, SCAPS-1D has been extensively used and looks like a powerful software due to its user-friendliness and simulation of results in a few seconds. The speed and ease of simulation make SCAPS-1D a very popular tool; as a result, it enables rapid optimization of a large number of photovoltaic devices and their performances without undergoing any experimental work, which can save time and money. However, one serious drawback is that the SCAPS-1D simulator works only for 1D configurations. It is ineffective in incorporating atomistic interactions and 3D effects. Hence, the efficacy of the SCAPS-1D simulator solely relies on the accuracy of the input parameters that the user provides, failing which may give wrong results and large deviations from accuracy.

</details>

A systematic analysis of the complete symmetry-reduced ensemble of 94 non-adjacent four-vacancy configurations shows that the ordered pattern lies within a broad low-energy manifold and is not energetically anomalous, although it is not the thermodynamic ground state. As a result, ordered vacancy arrays exhibit electrical currents up to five orders of magnitude higher than statistically equivalent random distributions and can approach, or locally exceed, the transport performance of pristine MoS$_2$. These findings establish atomic-scale defect ordering as a powerful design principle for two-dimensional materials, demonstrating that the organization of defects, beyond their concentration alone, provides a route to simultaneously preserve functionality and high electrical conductivity in highly defective semiconductors.

Abstract: All-two-dimensional magnetic tunnel junctions promise atomically sharp interfaces, yet the role of interface-induced states in their spin transport is not fully understood. Conclusion: dCC被确立为一个统一的变分框架，能够同时处理极化子基态与动力学，适用范围从模型系统扩展到真实材料，兼具精度、可解释性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Polaron ground states and finite-temperature dynamics remain challenging to simulate because existing methods struggle to combine nonperturbative accuracy, systematic improvability, and scalability from models to materials-specific Hamiltonians. A projected tangent-space response formalism built on the same ansatz yields electron-addition spectral functions and optical conductivities at zero and finite temperature. The resulting spectra agree well with DMRG, Lanczos, and neural-network quantum-state benchmarks while retaining a physically interpretable excitation hierarchy, and extend to two-dimensional lattices at finite temperature beyond the practical reach of these methods. The same framework applies directly to \textit{ab initio} electron--phonon matrix elements, yielding LiF electron- and hole-polaron binding energies that match state-of-the-art many-body calculations. These results establish dCC as a unified variational framework for polaron ground states and dynamics, from model systems to real materials.

</details>


### [85] [X-ray Thermal diffuse scattering from real-space displacement correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05051)
*Benjamin Fahl,Jonathan Bulled,Artem Korshunov,Dmitry Chernyshov,Yevheniia Kholina,Arkadiy Simonov*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于三维差分对分布函数（3D-dPDF）计算X射线热漫散射（thermal diffuse scattering）的方法。在简谐近似下该方法理论上精确，包含了所有阶次的多声子散射，仅需一次傅里叶变换即可获得大范围倒易空间中的漫散射强度。在硅单晶上的实验验证中，无需额外精细参数调整，计算结果与实测强度的R2残差低于5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X射线热漫散射的计算在传统方法中往往受限于近似简化或计算成本，难以同时涵盖所有阶次的多声子散射贡献。作者希望发展一种在简谐近似下精确、计算高效，并且能够与已有静态无序分析体系对接的方法。

Method: 该方法基于三维差分对分布函数（three-dimensional difference pair distribution function, 3D-dPDF）框架，在简谐近似条件下纳入所有阶次的多声子散射（multi-phonon scattering），通过单次傅里叶变换将实空间的对关联信息转换为倒易空间中的漫散射强度分布。

Result: 以硅单晶为测试对象，将计算结果与实验测量的漫散射强度进行对比，仅需标度因子和背景两项参数，计算即可复现实验数据，R2残差低于5%。

Conclusion: 由于该方法采用与既有Yell程序相同的对关联（pair-correlation）语言，热致（thermal）与静态（static）关联无序可在同一分析框架下等权重地纳入处理，为统一描述两类无序散射提供了基础。

Abstract: We introduce a method for calculating X-ray thermal diffuse scattering based on the three-dimensional difference pair distribution function (3D-dPDF). Within the harmonic approximation the method is exact, as it includes all orders of multi-phonon scattering. A single Fourier transform delivers the diffuse intensity over large volumes of reciprocal space. We tested the method against experimental diffuse scattering measured on a silicon single crystal. With nothing refined beyond a scale and a background, the calculation reproduces the measured intensity to an R2 residual below 5%. Because the method uses the same pair-correlation language as the established Yell program, thermal and static correlated disorder enter the analysis on equal footing.

</details>


### [86] [HPHT growth of centimeter-sized cubic boron nitride crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05058)
*Andrey Katrusha,Weihua Peng,Jianguo Peng,Konstantin Iakoubovskii*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了利用高压高温（HPHT）温度梯度法，以镍铬（Ni-Cr）基溶剂催化剂成功生长出尺寸超过10毫米的立方氮化硼（cBN）单晶，相比此前约3毫米的最大尺寸实现了显著突破，并探讨了其伸长形态与硼、氮物种有效扩散系数较低之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 立方氮化硼单晶的尺寸限制制约了其在工业与科研领域的应用，此前最大晶体尺寸仅约3毫米，需要突破生长尺寸的瓶颈以拓展其应用前景。

Method: 采用高压高温（HPHT）温度梯度法，选用镍铬基溶剂催化剂，在源区温度1950 °C、连续生长一周的条件下，通过维持稳定的前驱体通量（precursor flux）获得大尺寸单晶；同时以相同装置和条件生长金刚石作为对照，比较两者的晶体形貌差异。

Result: 成功生长出尺寸超过10毫米的cBN单晶，远超此前约3毫米的最大纪录；与同装置生长出的近等轴状（nearly isometric）金刚石晶体不同，cBN晶体呈现伸长（elongated）形态。

Conclusion: cBN晶体的伸长形貌归因于硼和氮物种在金属溶剂中的有效扩散系数（effective diffusivity）相对较低，导致晶体生长局域化在BN源附近，从而形成非等轴的外形特征。

Abstract: Single crystals of cubic boron nitride (cBN) exceeding 10 mm in size were grown by the high-pressure high-temperature (HPHT) temperature-gradient method using a Ni-Cr-based solvent catalyst. Compared with the previously reported maximum crystal size of approximately 3 mm, this improvement was achieved by maintaining a stable precursor flux during one week of growth at a source temperature of 1950 °C. In contrast to diamonds, which were grown with the same HPHT cell and showed nearly isometric shapes, the cBN crystals had elongated shapes. We attribute this cBN morphology to a localized growth near the BN source due to the relatively low effective diffusivity of boron and nitrogen species in the metallic solvent.

</details>


### [87] [Effective single particle picture for anharmonic lattice dynamics: a Rosetta stone for electronic and ionic response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05068)
*Giovanni Caldarelli,Francesco Mauri*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个基于平均场（mean-field）方法的离子晶格动力学理论框架，通过自洽方式引入非谐性（anharmonicity）。将多体晶格动力学映射为两类6N维向量，并类比电子体系的时间依赖密度泛函理论（time-dependent density functional theory, TDDFT），建立非谐晶格响应的对应理论，进而给出了晶格光学与热导率的表达式，并证明可通过高斯密度矩阵近似还原时间依赖自洽谐波近似（time-dependent self-consistent harmonic approximation, TDSCHA）的相关方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非谐晶格动力学（anharmonic lattice dynamics）中离子多体相互作用在数学上与电子相互作用有结构相似性，但长期以来缺乏一个统一的、与电子密度泛函理论相平行的理论框架来刻画离子晶格的动力学响应。本文旨在通过构建声子凝聚态（phonon condensate）和声子旋量（phonon spinor）等概念，将非谐晶格动力学转化为密度泛函理论的语言，从而直接复用电子动力学领域成熟的理论和计算方法。

Method: 作者以平均场近似为基础，通过自洽性引入非谐性，将N个三维原子的多体动力学映射为两类6N维向量：声子凝聚态描述平均原子位置演化，声子旋量描述原子弹性常数演化，并按类似自旋的量子数——声子赝自旋（phonon pseudospin）进行分类。多体Liouville方程被替换为两个类波方程，等价于密度泛函理论中的时间依赖Schrödinger方程。在此基础上，作者将离子响应用外场与力算符的矩阵元表达，并引入声子版本的Hartree交换关联核（Hartree-exchange-correlation kernel）描述非谐性对外界扰动的屏蔽效应。作者还以高斯近似密度矩阵推导出时间依赖自洽谐波近似的线性响应方程。

Result: 本文给出了晶格光学电导率和热导率的解析表达式，揭示了热导率对声子赝自旋的依赖关系。通过高斯近似密度矩阵，恢复出TDSCHA的线性响应方程，其中非谐核包含了三声子和四声子散射过程。

Conclusion: 该工作将非谐晶格动力学转化为密度泛函理论的语言，证明电子相互作用体系中已发展的理论与计算进展可直接应用于相互作用离子体系，为非谐晶格动力学响应研究提供了一条全新的理论路径。

Abstract: We establish a theoretical framework for the dynamics of a lattice of ions in a mean-field approach, where anharmonicity is included via self-consistency. In this picture, the many-body dynamics of a system of $N$ atoms in three dimensions is mapped onto two kinds of $6N$-dimensional vectors: the phonon condensate, describing the evolution of the average atomic positions, and the phonon spinors, describing the evolution of the atomic elastic constants. The phonon spinors are classified by a quantum number that behaves as a spin: the phonon pseudospin.
  The many-body Liouville equation is replaced by two wave equations equivalent to the time-dependent Schrödinger equation for the electronic wave function in density functional theory. Exploiting this parallelism, we formulate the response of the anharmonic lattice in one-to-one correspondence with time-dependent density functional theory for electrons. In complete analogy with the electronic case, we express the ionic response in terms of matrix elements of operators representing external fields and forces. We show how anharmonicity screens external perturbations through a phonon analogue of the Hartree-exchange-correlation kernel.
  We provide expressions for the lattice optical and thermal conductivity, showing how thermal conductivity depends on the phonon pseudospin. By approximating the density matrix as a Gaussian, we recover the equations of the time-dependent self-consistent harmonic approximation. In this case, the linear-response equations are formulated in terms of an anharmonic kernel including three- and four-phonon scattering.
  By translating anharmonic lattice dynamics into the language of density functional theory, this work shows how theoretical and computational advances in modeling the dynamical response of interacting electrons can be directly applied to interacting ions.

</details>


### [88] [The crossmetric tensor and the geometrical meaning of the imaginary numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05113)
*Cyril Cayron*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文将哈密顿（Hamilton）的四元数（quaternion）乘积从笛卡尔基推广到非笛卡尔基，引入了"交叉度量张量"（crossmetric tensor），并确定了六大晶系对应的交叉度量张量。文章还证明了任意单位晶体学四元数可由无穷多对定向平面几何表示，并引入了互补四元数（complementary quaternions），解释了笛卡尔基下 i,j,k 为何同时满足四元数的平方、二元乘积和三元乘积规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四元数在笛卡尔基下的乘法可作为基于符号 1,i,j,k 构成的 4×4 矩阵的二次型表示，但这一表示在非笛卡尔基下尚未被系统研究。本文旨在将四元数乘法推广到晶体学框架下的非笛卡尔基，建立交叉度量张量概念，并给出其几何解释。

Method: 将笛卡尔四元数乘积的 4×4 矩阵形式推广到非笛卡尔基，证明该矩阵由度量张量和交叉张量构成，命名为交叉度量张量；其符号 s,a,b,c 被识别为基本晶体学四元数。文章为六大晶系分别确定了交叉度量张量，并引入 a',b',c' 作为互补四元数，对应平面对 ma, mb, mc。作者还利用"来源-目标"规则（source-target rule）描述四元数的复合，并将任意单位晶体学四元数几何表示为沿其矢量分量相交的无穷多对定向平面。

Result: 确定了六大晶系（six crystal families）各自的交叉度量张量；证明了任意单位晶体学四元数可看作无穷多对沿四元数矢量分量相交、夹角为旋转半角的有向平面的几何表示。基本四元数 a,b,c 遵循哈密顿关于虚数平方的规则，互补四元数 a',b',c' 遵循哈密顿关于二元乘积和三元乘积的规则。在笛卡尔基下 i,j,k 是由立方体 mx,my,mz 的垂直面组成，此时基本四元数与互补四元数相等（i=i',j=j',k=k'），这解释了仅用三个四元数即可同时满足哈密顿的全部乘法规则。

Conclusion: 晶系四元数（crystallographic quaternions）作为哈密顿四元数在非笛卡尔基下的推广，其乘法规律可以通过交叉度量张量和几何平面表示得到统一理解。笛卡尔基下的 i,j,k 仅是晶系四元数的一个特例，其特殊性源于基本四元数与互补四元数在该基下的重合。

Abstract: The product of two Cartesian quaternions can be written as a quadratic form based on a 4x4 matrix made of the symbols 1,i,j,k where i,j,k are imaginary numbers introduced by Hamilton. We generalized this matrix to non-Cartesian bases, and showed that the matrix is made of the metric and the cross tensors. We called it crossmetric tensor. Its symbols are s,a,b,c; they are the elementary crystallographic quaternions. We determined the crossmetric tensors for the six crystal families. We also showed that any unit crystallographic quaternion can be geometrically represented by an infinity of pairs of oriented planes intersecting along the vectorial component of the quaternion such that the angle between them is the semiangle of the rotation. The composition of quaternions follows the intuitive source-target rule. The elementary quaternions a,b,c are geometrically represented by pair of perpendicular and oriented planes intersecting along the axis a,b,c, respectively. Other complementary quaternions noted a',b',c' were also introduced. They are the pairs of planes ma, mb, mc. The elementary quaternions a,b,c follow Hamilton rules on the squares of imaginary numbers. The complementary quaternions follow Hamilton rules on the bi and tri-products. For Cartesian basis, the quaternions i,j,k appear as a specific case of crystallographic quaternions. They are formed by the pairs of perpendicular faces of the cube mx, my, mz. Since these planes intersect along the x, y and z axis, respectively, the elementary and complementary quaternions are equal, i.e. i=i',j=j',k=k', which explains why the square, bi and tri-product Hamilton rules are satisfied all together with only three quaternions i,j,k.

</details>

### [89] [Hidden Supersymmetry in Wigner-Yang Quantum Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04503)
*Georg Junker*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了实直线上服从Wigner于1950年提出的变形海森堡代数（deformed Heisenberg algebra）的量子系统，其坐标表示与Yang在1951年以及Dunkl在1989年引入的差-微分算子本质相同。在特定条件下该系统表现出超对称（SUSY）结构，反射算子充当分级算子。作者通过只考虑相空间中两条运动方程之一推广了Yang的表示，得到非相互作用系统对应Witten的超对称量子力学模型；同时将Wigner-Yang结果推广到实直线上的一般对称势，并以谐振子和吸引型类库仑势为例说明，还建立了二者之间的Hooke-Newton对偶关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望统一并推广Wigner（1950年）和Yang（1951年）关于变形海森堡代数坐标表示的经典结果，并将其与Dunkl算子（1989年）的现代框架联系起来，以揭示这类量子系统在超对称量子力学（SUSY quantum mechanics）中的深层结构。

Method: 作者采用两步推广策略：首先仅保留相空间中两条运动方程中的一条，从而获得Yang表示的推广形式，并识别出对应的非相互作用系统即为Witten的超对称量子力学模型；随后重新加入第二条运动方程，将Wigner-Yang的原始结果从谐振子情形推广到实直线上的任意对称势能，并以谐振子和吸引型类库仑势V(x)=-γ/|x|作为显式例子进行讨论。

Result: 研究结果表明，仅考虑单条运动方程时系统精确对应Witten的超对称量子力学模型；加入两条运动方程后，原Wigner-Yang结果可推广至一般对称势。对于类库仑势与Wigner-Yang谐振子系统，作者建立了Hooke-Newton对偶（Hooke-Newton duality）关系。

Conclusion: 该工作将Wigner-Yang形式的变形海森堡代数系统与Witten的超对称量子力学和Dunkl算子理论统一在同一个框架下，并通过Hooke-Newton对偶揭示了两类不同势能系统（谐振子与类库仑势）之间的深层数学联系，为该方向后续研究提供了推广基础。

Abstract: We consider a quantum system on the real line obeying a deformed Heisenberg algebra originally proposed by Wigner in 1950. Its explicit coordinate representation was provided by Yang in 1951 and in essence is identical in form with Dunkl's difference-differential operator introduced in 1989 in connection with roots systems of refection groups. Under certain conditions such quantum systems exhibit a supersymmetric (SUSY) structure where the reflection operator acts as the grading operator. We present a generalisation of Yang's representation by first considering only of one the two equations of motion in phase space. The corresponding non-interacting system is found to represent Witten's model of SUSY quantum mechanics. Imposing also the second equation of motion the original result of Wigner and Yang is reconsidered by extending their discussion to general symmetric potentials on the real line. As explicit example we discuss the harmonic oscillator and an attractive Coulomb-like potential $V(x)=-γ/|x|$. We also establish a Hooke-Newton duality between this Coulomb-like system and the original Wigner-Yang harmonic oscillator system.

</details>


### [90] [Multi-frequency far-field data enrichment for electromagnetic source reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04829)
*Atyab Khalifa Al-Shaqsi,Heba Mohammed Al-Subhi,Xianchao Wang,Shujaat Khan,Abdul Wahab*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段重建策略，利用紧支撑、几何稀疏源具有有限创新率（finite rate of innovations, FRI）的物理特性，通过构建螺旋结构 Hankel 矩阵并采用消零滤波器低秩 Hankel 矩阵补全（ALOHA）方法补全欠采样多频远场数据，再结合傅里叶反演重建未知电磁源，在 30%-50% 样本可用率和 10 dB SNR 强噪声下取得优于标准 ℓ₁ 压缩感知基准的稳定重建结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际应用中，在奈奎斯特（Nyquist）采样率下采集密集多频远场测量值往往不可行，而欠采样或稀疏数据会引入非辐射源分量（non-radiating source components），破坏解的唯一性并导致标准反演技术产生严重伪影。因此需要一种能够在欠采样条件下可靠重建未知电磁源的方法。

Method: 方法分为两个阶段：第一阶段利用紧支撑、几何稀疏源具有有限创新率（FRI）的特性构建螺旋结构化 Hankel 矩阵，利用该矩阵的低秩性质将缺失多频远场数据恢复转化为约束矩阵补全问题，并通过消零滤波器低秩 Hankel 矩阵补全方法（ALOHA）求解；第二阶段采用傅里叶反演（Fourier inversion）方案从补全后的数据集重建电流源密度。

Result: 在电磁源模型上的大量数值评估表明，所提出的补全框架能有效消除欠采样伪影并解决非唯一性挑战。在高欠采样率（仅 30% 至 50% 可用样本）和强噪声（10 dB 信噪比）条件下，该方法能实现准确且稳定的重建，性能优于标准 ℓ₁ 压缩感知基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于 FRI 特性和低秩 Hankel 矩阵补全的两阶段重建策略为从欠采样远场辐射模式中重建未知电磁源提供了一种有效途径，克服了非唯一性和伪影问题，在强欠采样与高噪声环境下仍保持良好的重建质量。

Abstract: Reconstructing unknown electromagnetic sources from far-field radiation patterns is a fundamental inverse problem with broad applications in biomedical imaging, non-destructive testing, and telecommunications. In practical settings, however, collecting dense multi-frequency far-field measurements at the Nyquist sampling rate is often infeasible. Under-sampled or sparse data introduce non-radiating source components that sever the uniqueness of the solution, creating severe artifacts when standard inversion techniques are applied. To overcome this limitation, we present a two-stage reconstruction strategy exploiting the physical property that compactly supported, geometrically sparse sources exhibit a finite rate of innovations (FRI). In the first stage, we construct an associated wrap-around structured Hankel matrix. By leveraging the low-rank property of the matrix due to FRI of the unknown sources, we enrich the sub-sampled data. To that end, we convert missing multi-frequency far-field data recovery into a constrained matrix completion task solved via Annihilating Filter-based Low-rank Hankel Matrix Completion Approach (ALOHA). In the second stage, a Fourier inversion scheme reconstructs the current source density from the enriched dataset. Extensive numerical evaluations on electromagnetic source models show that our enrichment framework effectively eliminates under-sampling artifacts and resolves non-uniqueness challenges. The method delivers accurate and stable reconstructions under high sub-sampling rates (e.g., with $30$\% to $50$\% available samples) and strong noise conditions ($10$ dB SNR), outperforming standard $\ell_1$-compressed sensing baselines.

</details>


### [91] [Phase transitions in generalized XY models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04972)
*Fabio Plaga*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将高度函数模型（height function model）与一系列广义二维 XY 模型（generalized two-dimensional XY model）建立关联，并证明高度函数的去局域化（delocalisation）排除了初级自旋模型（primal spin model）中有序参量（nematic order parameter）的指数衰减，推广了 van Engelenburg 和 Lis（2023）的结果，并恢复了向列相区域（nematic region）的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义 XY 模型中向列有序与自旋去局域化之间的关联尚未被完整刻画，作者希望通过高度函数模型提供统一的分析框架，并推广已有的结果。

Method: 采用广义环表示（generalized loop representation）将两个面之间高度差的方差（variance）与向列有序参量相关联；同时利用 Pfister（1982）的经典比较论证（comparison argument）恢复向列区域的存在性。

Result: 证明高度函数模型的去局域化可排除向列有序参量的指数衰减，并将结果推广到涵盖 Korshunov 以及 Lee 和 Grinstein 独立提出的广义 XY 模型；该模型中相邻自旋铁磁性地偏好平行排列、向列性地偏好平行或反平行排列。

Conclusion: 该工作推广了 van Engelenburg 和 Lis（2023）的结果，为更广泛的广义 XY 模型建立了去局域化与向列有序之间的严格联系，并提供了向列区域存在性的证明。

Abstract: We associate height function models to a range of generalized two-dimensional XY models and prove that delocalisation of the height function model rules out exponential decay of the nematic order parameter in the primal spin model. The argument is based on a generalized loop representation which relates the variance of a height difference between two faces to the nematic order parameter. This generalizes the results by van Engelenburg and Lis (2023). The range of models also includes the much-studied generalized XY model as proposed by Korshunov and independently by Lee and Grinstein, where neighbouring spins interact ferromagnetically favouring parallel alignment and nematically favouring parallel or antiparallel alignment. Moreover, we recover the existence of a nematic region by a classical comparison argument of Pfister (1982).

</details>

Conclusion: 一致复因子化条件为超对称理论中的粒子耦合提供了强有力的约束，足以从第一性原理推导出大质量超多重态的结构，并完全确定N=4规范极大约束超引力的微扰Minkowski真空结构。

Abstract: I study constraints from consistent complex factorisation of $2\rightarrow 2$ scattering amplitudes in $4d$ and their relationship with supersymmetry. I complete the argument demonstrating that a massless helicity-$3/2$ particle must be a gravitino in a theory of supergravity and derive the structure and couplings of massive supermultiplets from first principles. For massive BPS particles in theories with extended supersymmetry, further constraints on the couplings are derived from consistent factorisation of massive superamplitudes. Among these is the requirement that BPS vector bosons must have couplings conforming to Lie algebra structure constants or generalised Chern-Simons terms. The gravitational coupling is shown to participate and draws the graviphotons into the Lie algebra. All $2\rightarrow 2$ tree-level amplitudes of massive particles with spin $s\leq 1$ are calculated in (super)gravity using on-shell methods and the double copy. These are assembled and decomposed into (super)amplitudes with varying amounts of supersymmetry. Finally, I study scattering of BPS gravitinos with unbroken $\mathcal{N}\geq 4$ supergravity and show that consistent factorisation leads to the full reconstruction of the super-Higgs mechanism. This argument completely determines the perturbative structure of all $\mathcal{N}=4$ Minkowski vacua of gauged maximal supergravity. Gravitinos in theories not admitting a super-Higgs mechanism are ruled-out.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文基于Drinfel'd扭转（Drinfel'd twist）实现的星积（star-product），构造了场论和规范理论的非对易（non-commutative）形变，涵盖共形对称性和超对称性构造的广泛扭转族，并提出了比文献中更弱的幺模条件（unimodularity condition）以及平面等价定理（planar equivalence theorem）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非对易形变方法适用范围有限，许多由对称性（特别是共形对称性和超对称性）构造的扭转无法直接应用于规范理论。作者旨在提出一种更普适的构造框架，以扩展可应用的扭转族，并确保规范不变性的一致性。

Method: 采用从未形变理论的对称性（以主动对称变换形式实现）构造的Drinfel'd扭转来实现星积，从而定义场论和规范理论的非对易形变。为与规范不变性保持一致，提出了比文献中通常采用的更弱的幺模条件；同时证明了平面等价定理，即形变理论的费曼图保持未形变的内部结构，扭转仅作用于外腿。

Result: 所提出的构造框架可涵盖大规模扭转族，包括由共形对称性和超对称性构造的扭转，显著扩展了已知规范理论形变的应用范围；所建立的更弱幺模条件允许使用此前被排除在外的扭转；平面等价定理表明形变理论费曼图内部结构不变，扭转仅影响外腿。

Conclusion: 这些结果对识别和研究与AdS5×S5超弦齐次Yang-Baxter形变对偶的N=4超杨-米尔斯（N=4 super Yang-Mills）形变具有重要意义，且该构造框架的适用范围超出上述具体情形。

Abstract: We construct non-commutative deformations of field and gauge theories based on star-products implemented by Drinfel'd twists. We are able to encompass a large family of twists, including those built out of conformal symmetries and supersymmetries. The main idea behind our construction is to work with twists constructed from symmetries of the undeformed theory, that are realised as active symmetry transformations. We argue that our construction amounts to a reformulation of known deformations of gauge theories, and that it significantly extends the range of applicable examples. To ensure consistency with gauge invariance, we also identify a unimodularity condition that is weaker than the one that is normally employed in the literature, so that we can apply twists that would otherwise be left out. Finally, we also prove a planar equivalence theorem stating that the Feynman diagrams of the deformed theories retain an undeformed internal structure, with the twist acting only on their external legs. All these results are important to identify and work with deformations of $\mathcal N=4$ super Yang-Mills that are proposed to be dual to homogeneous Yang-Baxter deformations of the $AdS_5\times S^5$ superstring, but the applicability of our construction and results goes beyond that.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文利用机器学习发现流水线（机器学习发现管道，machine-learning discovery pipeline）——物理信息符号回归（physics-informed symbolic regression）结合整数关系检测（integer-relation detection），对ABJM理论在通用扭曲（universal twist）下的有效扭曲超势（effective twisted superpotential）进行了研究。研究发现其微扰部分终止于两项，型IIA属展开在所有属上均有闭合系数公式且为渐近级数，并在k=1,2,4时将整个有限N余项化为单个绝对收敛的除数求和q级数，精度达到~10^{-797}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效扭曲超势在Cardy极限下支配三维N=2理论的超对称分配函数，并在大N极限下控制超对称AdS_4黑洞的熵函数中的引力块（gravitational blocks）。确定其在通用扭曲下U(N)_k×U(N)_{-k} ABJM理论的壳上结构，是理解这些物理量解析性质的关键。

Method: 采用机器学习发现流水线——物理信息符号回归（physics-informed symbolic regression）与整数关系检测（integer-relation detection）相结合的方案，将其应用于高达800位数字的Bethe真空（Bethe-vacuum）数据，以确定ABJM理论在通用扭曲下的有效扭曲超势的壳上结构。

Result: 微扰部分终止于两项：一个移位秩3/2幂项和一个与N无关的常数映射，二者在任意整数水平k下均有闭式表达。型IIA属展开在任意属下均存在包含二项式系数和伯努利数（Bernoulli number）的闭合系数公式，且为渐近级数。对于k=1,2,4，整个有限N余项由单个数论函数刻画：对权重四的Eichler积分（Eichler integral）——定义在Γ_0(2)或Γ_0(4)上的爱森斯坦级数（Eisenstein series）——施加一阶微分算子，得到绝对收敛的除数求和q级数，该闭合形式复现数据精度达~10^{-797}。

Conclusion: 通过机器学习发现流水线成功获得了ABJM理论通用扭曲下有效扭曲超势的完整壳上结构，包括任意属展开的闭式公式以及特定水平下有限N余项的精确q级数表示，揭示了该超势与模形式理论（如Eichler积分和Eisenstein级数）之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: The effective twisted superpotential governs the supersymmetric partition functions of three-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=2$ theories in the Cardy limit and, at large $N$, the gravitational blocks that are glued into the entropy function of supersymmetric $\mathrm{AdS}_4$ black holes. We determine its on-shell structure for $\mathrm{U}(N)_k\times\mathrm{U}(N)_{-k}$ ABJM theory at the universal twist, using a machine-learning discovery pipeline---physics-informed symbolic regression with integer-relation detection---applied to Bethe-vacuum data of up to $800$ digits. Its perturbative part terminates after two terms, a shifted-rank $3/2$-power and an $N$-independent constant map, which we obtain in closed form for every integer level. Its type-IIA genus expansion admits a closed coefficient formula at arbitrary genus, involving binomial and Bernoulli-number terms, and is asymptotic. For $k=1,2,4$ the entire finite-$N$ remainder is a single absolutely convergent divisor-sum $q$-series, obtained by applying a first-order differential operator to the Eichler integral of a weight-four Eisenstein series on $Γ_0(2)$ or $Γ_0(4)$. This closed form reproduces the data to $\sim\!10^{-797}$.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者从高精度Bethe-Ansatz数值中解析地重构这些常数，并在非微扰修正的层级上验证其结果；同时利用ABJM与ADHM理论之间的3d镜像对称性（3d mirror symmetry）作为非平凡检验条件，并通过近期建立的因子化关系（factorization relations）推导出挤压三球面配分函数的常数贡献。

Result: 所得ABJM与ADHM常数通过3d镜像对称性检验；通过因子化关系，这些常数还以闭合形式确定了挤压三球面配分函数在大挤压展开前两个主导阶中的N无关常数贡献，并验证其精确到非微扰修正层级。

Conclusion: 这些常数为近期3d超对称配分函数在全阶1/N展开中的精确结果补齐了此前未确定的常数项，标志着在场论与其对偶量子引力描述中完善这些结果的重要一步。

Abstract: We determine in closed form the $N$-independent constant terms in the all-order $1/N$ expansions of the topologically twisted index and of the associated Bethe potential for the ABJM theory, as well as the Bethe potential constant of the ADHM theory. We reconstruct these constants analytically from high-precision Bethe-Ansatz numerics, verify them down to the level of non-perturbative corrections, and find them to be closely related to the constant map function $A$ governing the round three-sphere partition function. The resulting expressions for the ADHM and ABJM constants pass the non-trivial test dictated by 3d mirror symmetry. Via recently established factorization relations, they also determine in closed form the $N$-independent constant contribution to the squashed three-sphere partition function, through the first two leading orders in its large-squashing expansion. These constants supply precisely the piece left undetermined in the recent exact results for 3d supersymmetric partition functions to all orders in the $1/N$ expansion, and thereby mark an important step toward completing them, both in field theory and in the dual quantum gravity description.

</details>


### [97] [Boundary Conditions and Entanglement in Anti-de Sitter Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04229)

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究 (3+1) 维全局坐标 AdS 空间中，共形耦合标量场基态的纠缠熵（entanglement entropy），聚焦于原点处球面纠缠面并允许共形边界上的一般边界条件。通过数值与解析方法，作者发现熵的紫外发散部分具有普适形式，而紫外有限部分对边界条件敏感，并确定了混合边界条件（在 Dirichlet 与 Neumann 之间插值）对该部分的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弄清楚边界条件如何影响纠缠熵的紫外有限部分，以深化对 AdS/CFT 对应关系中全息纠缠熵的理解。

Method: 采用数值与解析相结合的方法，在 (3+1) 维全局坐标 AdS 空间中研究共形耦合标量场基态的纠缠熵，考虑球面纠缠面，并引入介于 Dirichlet 与 Neumann 之间的混合边界条件。

Result: 纠缠熵的紫外发散部分具有普适（universal）形式，与边界条件无关；紫外有限部分则对边界条件敏感，且其依赖关系可由混合边界条件参数化刻画。

Conclusion: 作者确定了混合边界条件对纠缠熵紫外有限部分的具体依赖形式，表明边界条件在 AdS 空间中纠缠熵的有限部分扮演关键角色。

Abstract: We study the entanglement entropy of a conformally coupled scalar field at its ground state in $(3+1)$-dimensional AdS space in global coordinates. We consider spherical entangling surfaces centered at the origin of AdS and allow for general boundary conditions at the conformal boundary. Through numerical and analytical means, we show that the UV-divergent part of the entropy has a universal form, while the UV-finite part is sensitive to the boundary conditions. We determine the dependence of the latter part on mixed boundary conditions that interpolate between Dirichlet and Neumann.

</details>


### [98] [Tilts from 2-Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04248)
*Sven Harder,Theodore Jacobson,Zhengdi Sun*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文分析了2-群（2-group）全局对称性中，携带1-形式（1-form）子群电荷的线缺陷如何普遍性地显式破坏0-形式（0-form）对称性，并将这种对称性破缺归因于一种称为"族反常"（family anomaly）的拓扑障碍，其存在在重整化群流下保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2-群全局对称性以有趣的方式将0-形式和1-形式对称性交织在一起，但携带1-形式电荷的线缺陷对0-形式对称性的约束尚缺乏普适性理解。作者旨在建立这类线缺陷与0-形式对称性破缺之间的通用约束机制。

Method: 主要工具包括Wess-Zumino一致性条件和针对缺陷与边界的广义反常流入（anomaly inflow）形式论。在连续非阿贝尔2-群情形下，族反常源于缺陷模空间上的高阶Berry联络（higher Berry connection）；连续阿贝尔情形则利用微分上同调（differential cohomology）处理2-群背景场；离散情形将问题转化为对称缺陷的结合性表述。

Result: 证明了携带1-形式子群电荷的线缺陷普遍性地显式破坏0-形式对称性，产生一族由破缺对称性生成元参数化的线缺陷，其存在性在重整化群流下不变。计算了若干实例中倾斜算符（tilt operator）及其高阶推广的显式形式，并讨论了简单与非简单线的区分以及Postnikov类分辨等微妙问题。

Conclusion: 携带1-形式电荷的线缺陷导致的对称性破缺与体中的对称性破缺层级相关，族反常是对称缺陷的拓扑阻碍，提供了统一的框架来理解2-群对称性中线缺陷的普遍行为。

Abstract: 2-group global symmetries intertwine 0-form and 1-form symmetries in an interesting way. We analyze universal constraints on lines which are charged under the 1-form subgroup of a 2-group, and show that they generically break the 0-form symmetry explicitly. This gives rise to a family of line defects parameterized by the broken symmetry generators, whose existence is invariant under the renormalization group flow of the bulk-defect system. The symmetry breaking is enforced by a `family anomaly,' which is a topological obstruction to a symmetric defect. Our main tools are the Wess-Zumino consistency condition and a generalized anomaly inflow formalism for defects and boundaries. For nonabelian continuous 2-groups the family anomaly stems from a higher Berry connection on the moduli space of defects, and constrains response functions that probe the local action of the broken symmetry. In the continuous abelian case we apply differential cohomology to the 2-group background fields to uncover a subtle generalization of the Wess-Zumino condition, while in the discrete case we recast it in terms of the associativity of symmetry defects. We give numerous illustrative examples, computing when possible explicit forms of the tilt operator (which probes the linear response of the defect to the broken symmetry) and its higher analogs, which are crucial for matching the family anomaly. We also discuss universal features such as how symmetry violation by charged line defects is related to symmetry breaking hierarchies in the bulk, and highlight a number of subtle points including the distinction between simple and non-simple lines, and Postnikov class resolution.

</details>

*Andreas Gustavsson*

Result: 在大$N$极限、$SU(N)$基本表示条件下，两个球面威尔逊面之间存在一阶Gross-Ooguri相变，其对应的临界分离距离已通过数值计算获得。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了六维(2,0)理论中威尔逊面在临界距离处经历一阶相变，这是类似威尔逊线（Wilson lines）Gross-Ooguri相变的面版本推广。

Abstract: We use the AdS/CFT correspondence to show that for two spherical Wilson surfaces, both in the fundamental representation of $SU(N)$ gauge group, in the six-dimensional $(2,0)$ theory, in the large $N$ limit there is a first order Gross-Ooguri phase transition at a certain critical separation distance that we obtain numerically.

</details>

*Yasuyuki Hatsuda,Kengo Kikuchi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种非微扰解析张量网络（tensor network）表述，用于定义在复积分轮廓上的$\mathcal{PT}$对称标量场论。作者将其应用于二维$\mathcal{PT}$对称$φ^4$理论（负四次方耦合），推导出初始张量的显式解析表达式，并证明张量分量按指标之和的奇偶性分离为偶、奇扇区。此外，作者在任意维度下解析证明了该轮廓上的晶格配分函数与厄米晶格$φ^4$配分函数解析延拓至负四次方耦合后的实部之间存在精确的有限体积关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $\mathcal{PT}$对称量子场论通过复积分轮廓定义，传统微扰方法在处理此类非厄米理论时面临困难，需要发展非微扰的解析工具。张量网络方法为研究这类理论提供了新的可能路径，但此前缺乏系统的解析构造。

Method: 作者提出一种非微扰解析张量网络表述，适用于定义在复积分轮廓上的$\mathcal{PT}$对称标量场论。针对二维$\mathcal{PT}$对称$φ^4$理论，推导出初始张量的显式解析表达式，利用张量指标和的奇偶性将分量分离为偶、奇两扇区。同时，在任意维度下构造替代复轮廓，并建立晶格配分函数之间的精确解析关系。

Result: 获得了初始张量的显式解析表达式，其分量按指标之和奇偶性自然分解为偶、奇两个独立扇区。在任意维度下，解析证明了所构造的复轮廓上的有限体积晶格配分函数等于厄米晶格$φ^4$配分函数解析延拓至负四次方耦合后实部。

Conclusion: 该工作为$\mathcal{PT}$对称标量场论提供了一种非微扰的解析张量网络构造，并在有限体积水平上建立了与厄米$φ^4$理论解析延拓之间的精确对应关系，为进一步研究此类非厄米理论的非微扰性质奠定了基础。

Abstract: We develop a non-perturbative analytic tensor network formulation of $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetric scalar field theories defined on complex integration contours. Applying this formulation to the two-dimensional $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetric $φ^4$ theory at negative quartic coupling, we derive an explicit analytic expression for the initial tensor and show that its components separate into even and odd sectors according to the parity of the sum of the tensor indices. We further formulate the theory on an alternative complex contour and analytically establish, in arbitrary dimensions, an exact finite-volume relation between the lattice partition function defined on this contour and the real part of the analytic continuation of the Hermitian lattice $φ^4$ partition function to negative quartic coupling.

</details>


### [101] [Critical and multicritical Kasner scaling in holographic phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04522)
*Ling-Long Gao,Yan Liu,Hong-Da Lyu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了全息相变（holographic phase transitions）如何将标度律（scaling laws）印刻在Einstein-scalar黑洞内部的局域Kasner几何上，推导了临界标度关系并在数值上验证了普通临界性和三临界情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞内部几何结构如何受全息相变影响，特别是相变的临界标度如何在Kasner几何中被体现。

Method: 在固定双迹耦合（double-trace coupling）条件下，解析推导第一纪元Kasner指数偏离Schwarzschild值的近临界标度关系，并通过超指数标量势生成多个Kasner纪元，结合数值解验证预测。

Result: 推导出 $p_t^{(1)}+\frac{1}{3} \propto O^2 \propto (T_c-T)^{1/r}$ 的标度关系，其中普通临界性对应 $r=1$、三临界性对应 $r=2$、更高多临界性对应 $r\geq3$；发现跨越相变可连续追踪的后续纪元继承相同的温度指数；数值验证了普通和三临界情形的预测；解析证明红外不动点（infrared fixed point）的主导非相关指数（leading irrelevant exponent）控制低温Kasner标度。

Conclusion: 全息相变的标度律确实在黑洞内部Kasner几何中留下可观测的印记，且该标度由红外不动点的主导非相关指数所决定，本研究为黑洞内部几何与边界全息理论之间的对应提供了新的定量联系。

Abstract: We study how holographic phase transitions imprint their scaling laws on the local Kasner geometry inside Einstein-scalar black holes. At fixed double-trace coupling, we derive the near-critical scaling of the deviation of the first-epoch Kasner exponent from the Schwarzschild value: $p_t^{(1)}+\frac{1}{3} \propto O^2 \propto (T_c-T)^{1/r}$, where $O$ denotes the boundary condensate and $r$ labels ordinary criticality ($r=1$), tricriticality ($r=2$), and higher multicriticality ($r\geq3$). A super-exponential scalar potential generates a sequence of Kasner epochs separated by scalar-field bounces. We further find that any later epoch that can be tracked continuously across the transition inherits the same temperature exponent, while its coefficient depends on the epoch. Numerical solutions confirm these predictions for the ordinary and tricritical cases. Finally, we show analytically that the leading irrelevant exponent of the infrared fixed point governs the low-temperature Kasner scaling, and verify the resulting scaling numerically for the first Kasner exponent.

</details>


### [102] [Quantization of Algebraic Varieties Defined by Casimir Polynomials via Matrix Regularization: Fuzzy $S^7$ and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04717)
*Akifumi Sako*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了由紧致半单李代数（compact semisimple Lie algebra）的Casimir多项式定义的代数簇的量子化问题，利用基于可约表示的李-泊松代数（Lie-Poisson algebra）矩阵正则化方法，通过将其分解为余伴随轨道（coadjoint orbit）来构造量子化，并以模糊七维球面（fuzzy S^7）为例进行了详细展示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代数簇的量子化是量子几何与数学物理中的核心问题之一，尤其涉及与李代数结构相关的非交换几何构造。作者旨在利用李-泊松代数的矩阵正则化方法来系统构造由Casimir多项式定义的代数簇的量子化，特别是处理包含奇异轨道的情况。

Method: 采用最近发展的李-泊松代数矩阵正则化（matrix regularization）方法，基于可约表示（reducible representation）的表述，将代数簇分解为余伴随轨道（包括奇异轨道）后分别进行量子化构造，最终以模糊S^7为具体算例展示该构造过程。

Result: 成功建立了由紧致半单李代数Casimir多项式定义的代数簇的量子化框架，该框架能够处理包含奇异余伴随轨道在内的分解情形，并给出了模糊S^7的详细构造。

Conclusion: 论文表明，通过基于可约表示的李-泊松代数矩阵正则化，可以实现由Casimir多项式定义的代数簇的系统性量子化，并涵盖奇异轨道情形，从而为更广泛的非交换几何构造提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We study the quantization of algebraic varieties defined by equations involving Casimir polynomials of compact semisimple Lie algebras. The Casimir polynomials belong to the Poisson center of the corresponding Lie-Poisson algebra. For this purpose, we employ a recently developed matrix regularization of Lie-Poisson algebras. In particular, using its formulation based on reducible representations, we construct quantizations of these algebraic varieties through their decomposition into coadjoint orbits, including singular orbits. As a concrete example, we present the construction of fuzzy $S^7$ in detail.

</details>


### [103] [On the Fermion Level Crossing in the Electroweak Instanton Background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04595)
*P. E. Mogaddam,S. S. Gousheh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文研究了电弱瞬子（electroweak instanton）背景中的费米子能级穿越现象，在完整考虑费米能量欧几里德时间依赖的前提下，证明了费米子能谱从一支连续谱流向另一支的持续时间恰好对应瞬子的陈-西蒙斯数（Chern-Simons number）改变一个单位，并在数值上确认了指标定理（index theorem）在瞬子背景中的成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在标准模型框架下，电弱瞬子背景中的拓扑过程与费米子能级穿越密切相关，但此前对费米子能量欧几里德时间依赖的考虑不充分，导致费米子零模数量与瞬子绕数之间的严格一一对应关系尚未被清晰建立。

Method: 从场方程到费米子能级的光谱流（spectral flow），全程纳入费米子能量的欧几里德时间（Euclidean time）依赖；对标准费米子拟设（ansatz）进行修正，并在瞬子背景下进行数值计算以验证指标定理。

Result: 研究表明，无论模型参数如何取值，费米子能谱从一支连续谱流向另一支所经历的持续时间恰好对应瞬子陈-西蒙斯数改变一个单位；纳入时间依赖后，费米子零模数量与瞬子绕数（winding number）之间建立了一一对应关系，数值上确认了指标定理的有效性。

Conclusion: 费米子能量的欧几里德时间依赖是刻画瞬子背景下费米子能级穿越行为的关键因素，忽略这一依赖将无法在瞬子背景中正确建立费米子零模与拓扑量子数之间的对应，从而无法精确再现指标定理。

Abstract: We investigate fermion level crossing in the electroweak instanton background, taking into account the Euclidean-time dependence of the fermion energy throughout our analysis, from the field equations to the spectral flow of fermion energy levels. Modifying the standard fermion ansatzes, we show that, irrespective of the model parameters, the duration over which the fermion energy spectrum flows from one continuum to another corresponds to exactly one unit change in the Chern--Simons number of the instanton. We further demonstrate that incorporating this time dependence is essential for establishing a one-to-one correspondence between the number of fermion zero modes and the instanton winding number, providing a numerical confirmation of the index theorem in the context of instanton backgrounds.

</details>


### [104] [Probe Dependence of the Imaginary Part of HTEE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04599)
*Mohammad Ali-Akbari*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在五维各向异性引力背景（对偶于强耦合各向异性等离子体）中，利用复极值面方法解析计算了高温度、小各向异性极限（aT≪1）下的全息类时间纠缠熵（holographic timelike entanglement entropy, HTEE）。研究发现HTEE虚部（源于紫外对数发散）不是几何的普适性质，而是依赖于极值面相对于各向异性方向的取向，并可作为极值面与对偶场论各向异性自由度耦合的诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作的动机在于探究全息类时间纠缠熵在存在各向异性的背景下是否具有普适性，尤其是其虚部的行为，以理解UV对数发散引发的量是否依赖于探针（即极值面）的取向。

Method: 采用复极值面（complex extremal surface）方法，在五维各向异性引力背景中解析计算HTEE；选取高温度、小各向异性极限（aT≪1）以简化计算；分别考虑垂直于和平行于各向异性方向的两种边界类时间隔（timelike interval）取向。

Result: 研究发现HTEE的虚部并非几何的普适性质，而是强烈依赖于极值面相对于各向异性方向的取向。具体而言，垂直于和平行于各向异性方向两种取向给出了不同的虚部行为。源于紫外对数发散的虚部被证明是探针依赖（probe-dependent）的量。

Conclusion: HTEE的虚部可以作为极值面与对偶场论中各向异性自由度之间耦合强度的诊断工具。该结果深化了我们对类时间纠缠熵在非各向同性全息背景中性质的认识。

Abstract: We investigate the holographic timelike entanglement entropy (HTEE) in a five-dimensional anisotropic background, dual to a strongly coupled anisotropic plasma. Using the complex extremal surface method, we compute the HTEE analytically in the high-temperature, small-anisotropy limit $aT \ll 1$. We consider two different orientations of the boundary timelike interval: one perpendicular to the anisotropy direction and one parallel to it. We find that the imaginary part of the HTEE is not a universal property of the geometry but depends sensitively on the orientation of the extremal surface relative to the anisotropy. This demonstrates that the imaginary part arising from the UV logarithmic divergence is a probe-dependent quantity. Our results suggest that the imaginary part of HTEE can serve as a diagnostic of the coupling between the extremal surface and the anisotropic degrees of freedom of the dual field theory.

</details>


### [105] [Wavefunctions of AdS$_3$ Universes and $T\bar{T}$-deformed Torus Partition Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04665)
*Shinji Hirano*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了渐近AdS₃时空中量子引力的波函数与T\bar T形变环面配分函数之间的关系，证明了形变配分函数可通过可逆积分变换由体波函数得到，核函数编码了T\bar T耦合与体径向/时间坐标之间的联系；该构造可延拓至de Sitter环形宇宙，对de Sitter全息具有启示意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子引力波函数与T\bar T形变理论之间的精确关系，并借此深化对全息原理（holographic principle）的理解，尤其是在有限形变（finite deformation）情形下全息屏（holographic screen）如何偏离渐近边界并扩展为有限体内的区域。

Method: 在渐近AdS₃时空中构造引力量子波函数，通过可逆积分变换（invertible integral transform）将T\bar T形变环面配分函数与体波函数联系起来；核函数编码T\bar T耦合与体径向或时间坐标的关系。在欧氏（Euclidean）框架下源于Rindler AdS₃波函数，经双重Wick转动获洛伦兹（Lorentzian）解释，并推广至de Sitter环形宇宙。

Result: 在CFT极限下核函数定位于渐近边界，恢复标准全息对应关系；在有限形变下，核将全息屏从渐近边界移开并扩展为有限体区域，固定耦合的形变配分函数对应以形变尺度为中心、有限宽度的体波包。该构造经双重Wick转动可给出无渐近边界的封闭AdS₃环形宇宙。

Conclusion: 该构造建立了T\bar T形变配分函数与体波函数之间的精确可逆关系，为de Sitter全息（de Sitter holography）提供了新途径，并有望推广至更一般的空间拓扑与更高维时空。

Abstract: We study wavefunctions of quantum gravity in asymptotically AdS$_3$ spacetimes and their relation to $T\bar T$-deformed torus partition functions. We show that the deformed partition function is given by an invertible integral transform of bulk wavefunctions, with a kernel encoding the relation between the $T\bar T$ coupling and the radial or temporal coordinate in the bulk. The invertibility of the transform allows the bulk wavefunction to be reconstructed from the family of $T\bar T$-deformed partition functions. In the CFT limit, the kernel effectively localizes at the asymptotic boundary, recovering the standard holographic relation in which the partition function on the torus is determined by the boundary value of the bulk wavefunction. For finite deformation, the kernel shifts the effective holographic screen away from the asymptotic boundary and broadens it into a finite bulk region. A $T\bar{T}$-deformed partition function at fixed coupling corresponds to a finite-width bulk wavepacket centered around a radial or temporal location set by the deformation scale. In Euclidean signature, the construction arises naturally from wavefunctions of Rindler AdS$_3$, while after a double Wick rotation it admits a Lorentzian interpretation in terms of closed AdS$_3$ torus universes without asymptotic boundaries. Finally, we discuss the extension of the present construction to de Sitter torus universes and its implications for de Sitter holography, and comment on possible generalizations to more general spatial topologies and higher-dimensional spacetimes.

</details>


### [106] [Corrections to the Unruh Effect from Robin Boundary Conditions in Punctured Minkowski Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04705)
*Nickolas P. Botta,João Paulo M. Pitelli,Ricardo A. Mosna*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究穿孔闵可夫斯基时空（punctured Minkowski spacetime）中带Robin边界条件的无质量实标量场，推导了边界诱导的静态基态Wightman函数的闭合形式，并分析了均匀加速Unruh-DeWitt探测器对该场的响应。研究发现边界诱导贡献非平稳且可增强或抑制标准闵可夫斯基响应，并证明对于无偏移径向轨迹该贡献在探测器全历史积分下绝对可积、保持O(1)。Neumann极限因s波红外奇异性而对数发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非平凡拓扑或边界条件如何影响量子场真空涨落及其与Unruh-DeWitt探测器的相互作用，特别是边界对均匀加速探测器标准Unruh响应的修正机制。

Method: 在穿孔闵可夫斯基时空中选取稳定单参数族的Robin边界条件G(0)−βG'(0)=0（β≥0），以闭合形式（closed form）推导边界诱导的静态基态Wightman函数，并研究均匀加速Unruh-DeWitt探测器，通过适当时间内减法提取边界贡献。

Result: 边界诱导的拉回项在固有时间内非平稳，其对探测器响应的贡献可增强或抑制普通闵可夫斯基响应，取决于Robin参数、探测器能隙及采样轨迹部分。对无偏移径向轨迹，证明边界诱导拉回在双固有时间变量上绝对可积，全历史平滑相互作用贡献趋于有限极限并保持O(1)，不产生额外的线性时间项。Neumann极限奇异：边界诱导两点函数因s波红外奇异性而对数增长。

Conclusion: 边界条件可系统性修正均匀加速探测器在闵可夫斯基真空中的响应，且Neumann边界对应奇异极限，其响应增长具有对数行为。

Abstract: We consider a real massless scalar field in punctured Minkowski spacetime, endowed at the removed origin with the stable one-parameter family of Robin boundary conditions $G(0)-βG'(0)=0$, $β\geq0$. We obtain the boundary-induced part of the static ground-state Wightman function in closed form and study the response of a uniformly accelerated Unruh-DeWitt detector. The puncture breaks the boost symmetry underlying the stationary Unruh response, so the boundary-induced pullback is nonstationary in proper time. The corresponding subtracted detector contribution can either enhance or suppress the ordinary Minkowski response and depends on the Robin parameter, the detector gap, and the portion of the trajectory sampled. For the unshifted, radially aligned trajectory studied in detail, we prove that the boundary-induced pullback is absolutely integrable in the two proper-time variables. Consequently, as the smooth interaction is extended over the full detector history, this contribution approaches a finite limit and remains $O(1)$, producing no additional term linear in the interaction duration. The formal Neumann limit is singular: the boundary-induced two-point function grows logarithmically because of an infrared singularity in the $s$-wave sector, and the numerical response exhibits growth consistent with this asymptotic behavior.

</details>


### [107] [Quantum gravity around ultracold black holes from DSSYK](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04745)
*Francesca Mariani,Thomas G. Mertens,Jacopo Papalini,Thomas Tappeiner*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过双缩放SYK模型（double-scaled SYK model）的特定平直空间极限，提出超冷Reissner-Nordström de Sitter黑洞的量子描述，并利用规范化的正弦膨胀子引力（sine dilaton gravity）模型提出一种新的近水平动力学量子化方案，从而得到与冷黑洞和Nariai物理截然不同的非微扰低温配分函数行为，并将其解读为一种在量子系统中动态实现宇宙监督（cosmic censorship）的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超冷Reissner-Nordström de Sitter黑洞的量子描述尚不清晰；在平直空间极限下，其低能涨落约化为平直JT引力（flat JT gravity），而该理论作为量子系统存在病态行为，需要寻找修正方案以解决其结构性问题。

Method: 首先利用双缩放SYK模型的特定平直空间极限建立对超冷黑洞的量子描述；随后在膨胀子势（dilaton potential）中加入首阶次领头修正，检验其对Hagedorn简并（Hagedorn degeneracy）的破坏效果；最终借助正弦膨胀子引力模型的规范化（gauged）描述，提出对超冷黑洞近水平（near-horizon）动力学的全新量子化方案。

Result: 首阶次领头修正打破了领头阶Hagedorn简并，但并未解决平直JT引力的结构性问题。新提出的量子化方案在谱的渐近区域动态抑制了态密度，并产生了离散时空的特征；由此得到配分函数的非微扰低温行为，其物理显著不同于冷黑洞和Nariai情形。

Conclusion: 该结果可被解读为一种在量子系统中动态实现宇宙监督的机制，为超冷黑洞的近水平动力学提供了新的量子描述。

Abstract: We propose a quantum description of the ultracold Reissner-Nordström de Sitter black hole via a specific flat space limit of the double-scaled SYK model. Fluctuations in this regime reduce to flat JT gravity, which is ill-behaved as a quantum system. We show that including the first subleading correction to the dilaton potential breaks the leading order Hagedorn degeneracy but does not resolve the structural problem. Finally, we leverage the gauged description of the sine dilaton gravity model to propose a novel quantization of the near-horizon dynamics of ultracold black holes. This leads to a new proposal for the partition function where states in the asymptotics of the spectrum are suppressed dynamically, and features of a discretized spacetime arise. We obtain a non-perturbative low temperature behavior of the partition function, which is quite different than the cold and Nariai physics. Our result can be interpreted as a dynamical way to enforce cosmic censorship on the quantum system.

</details>


### [108] [Generalized comodule tube algebras for boundary and domain wall defects of (2+1)D topological order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05071)
*Zhian Jia,Sheng Tan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将管代数（tube algebra）框架推广到(2+1)维可间隙相（gapped phase）中的余维2缺陷（codimension-2 defect），构建了缺陷管代数（defect tube algebra），并证明其自然带有弱Hopf管代数上的余模代数（comodule algebra）结构，同时与Drinfeld double及N元代数建立了联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 管代数刻画拓扑相中的拓扑激发，但对更高阶缺陷（如余维2缺陷）的描述尚不完善。作者旨在将这一工具推广到余维2缺陷，以统一描述边界缺陷、畴壁缺陷及其复合结构。

Method: 在Turaev–Viro–Barrett–Westbury TQFT（以Levin–Wen弦网模型为代表）框架下构造缺陷管代数，利用(2+1)维Heegaard图及其对偶刻画缺陷；通过表示论刻画边界与畴壁缺陷，并将畴壁缺陷管代数推广为N元代数及多元余模代数结构。

Result: 余维2缺陷由带有$C^*$弱Hopf管代数上余模代数结构的缺陷管代数描述；边界与畴壁缺陷由其对应缺陷管代数的表示刻画，畴壁缺陷管代数可视为相邻边界缺陷管代数的广义Drinfeld double；对连接N个体相的畴壁，其管代数为N元代数；对一般k缺陷，其广义管代数具有多元余模代数（multicomodule algebra）结构。

Conclusion: 该工作为拓扑相中高阶缺陷的代数分类提供了统一框架，自然推广了管代数方法，并为理解边界–畴壁–体相之间的代数层级关系提供了新的视角。

Abstract: The tube algebra, which carries the structure of a $C^*$ weak Hopf algebra, is a fundamental tool for characterizing topological excitations in topological phases. In this work, we generalize the tube algebra framework to codimension-2 defects in $(2+1)$D gapped phases described by Turaev--Viro--Barrett--Westbury TQFTs, with particular emphasis on Levin--Wen string-net models. We show that such codimension-2 defects are described by a \emph{defect tube algebra}, which naturally carries the structure of a comodule algebra over the weak Hopf tube algebra associated with the topological excitations. Boundary and domain wall defects are then characterized by the representations of the corresponding boundary and domain wall defect tube algebras. In particular, a domain wall defect tube algebra can be regarded as a generalized Drinfeld double of the tube algebras associated with the two adjacent boundary defects. More generally, for a domain wall joining $N$ bulk phases, the associated domain wall defect tube algebra can be viewed as an $N$-tuple algebra. This perspective extends naturally to general $k$-defects, namely codimension-2 defects joining $k$ codimension-1 defects, for which the resulting generalized tube algebra carries the structure of a multicomodule algebra.

</details>


### [109] [Machine-Learning Search for Lax Connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05146)
*Osamu Fukushima,Tomohiro Shigemura,Ryosuke Suda,Norihiro Tanahashi,Kentaroh Yoshida*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用机器学习框架，基于局部流数据搜索二维非线性sigma模型（non-linear sigma model）中的Lax联络（Lax connection）。在$SU(2)$主手征模型和对称陪集空间$S^2=SU(2)/U(1)$中成功恢复了完整谱参数族；在非对称陪集$T^{1,1}$中虽得到低损失解，但解析验证表明其为不编码运动方程的“伪Lax”联络。研究凸显了低平坦损失不能单独证明真正可积性，解析验证不可或缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法发现Lax联络通常依赖谱曲线的先验知识，本文旨在探索机器学习能否仅从局部流数据出发自动搜索Lax联络，从而验证数据驱动方法在可积系统发现中的潜力与局限。

Method: 采用机器学习框架，以二维非线性sigma模型的局部流数据为输入，对多种模型（$SU(2)$主手征模型、对称陪集$S^2=SU(2)/U(1)$以及非对称陪集$T^{1,1}$）进行优化搜索，寻找满足平坦性条件的Lax联络候选，并对所得候选进行解析验证。

Result: 方法在$SU(2)$主手征模型和$S^2$陪集模型上成功恢复完整谱参数家族（spectral-parameter family），且未使用已知谱曲线作为训练目标；在$T^{1,1}$模型上优化收敛到可复现的低损失映射，归纳出紧凑的分块对角ansatz，但解析验证显示这是“伪Lax”联络——满足壳上平坦性却不编码二维运动方程，不过其点粒子约化确实给出真正的力学Lax对。

Conclusion: 机器学习能有效提出候选ansätze并识别谱结构，但仅凭低平坦损失不能认证真正的可积性（integrability），解析验证仍是确认结果的必要环节。

Abstract: We apply a machine learning framework to search for Lax connections in two-dimensional non-linear sigma models using local current data. For the $SU(2)$ principal chiral model and the symmetric coset $S^2 = SU(2)/U(1)$, the method successfully recovers the full spectral-parameter families without using the known spectral curves as training targets. For the non-symmetric coset $T^{1,1}$, the optimization converges to reproducible low-loss maps that distill into a compact block-diagonal ansatz. However, analytic verification shows that this candidate is a ``fake Lax'' connection which satisfies on-shell flatness but fails to encode the two-dimensional equations of motion, whereas its point-particle reduction yields a genuine mechanical Lax pair. These results demonstrate that machine learning can effectively propose candidate ansätze and identify spectral structures, but low flatness loss alone does not certify genuine integrability, underscoring the necessity of analytic validation.

</details>
