### [110] [Moving a Detector to Probe New Neutrino Interactions: IWCD at Hyper-Kamiokande](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04091)
*Thomas Schwemberger,Volodymyr Takhistov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用可移动探测器（如Hyper-Kamiokande的中间水切伦科夫探测器IWCD）在能量谱空间变化的束流中运动，通过测量多个离轴位置的中性流与带电流事件率比，实现对中微子非标准相互作用（NSI）的新探测方式，并给出了对轴向、矢量及带电流NSI的95%置信水平灵敏度预估值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，可移动中微子探测器主要用于控制系统不确定性。本文指出探测器运动本身可以探测基本相互作用的精细结构，因为当探测器在能量谱随空间变化的束流中移动时，同一靶标和装置会暴露于不同的入射能谱，从而实现相互作用光谱学（interaction spectroscopy）。

Method: 以Hyper-Kamiokande在J-PARC离轴束流中的中间水切伦科夫探测器（Intermediate Water Cherenkov Detector, IWCD）为例，以中微子非标准相互作用（non-standard interactions, NSI）为基准模型。利用中性流与带电流事件率之比来降低共同的归一化不确定性，并结合多个离轴位置的测量来打破单一入射谱下存在的简并性。

Result: 在结合三个离轴位置并采用5%相关归一化不确定性的基准下，论文给出95%置信水平对轴向中性流NSI的灵敏度为-0.07 ≲ ε^{uA}_{μμ} ≲ 0.06，矢量NSI为-0.10 ≲ ε^{uV}_{μμ} ≲ 0.12，带电流NSI为-0.05 ≲ ε^{udL}_{μμ} ≲ 0.05。

Conclusion: 轴向NSI不贡献普通物质势（ordinary matter potential），因此与中微子振荡和高能散射测量互补。更广泛地，探测器运动提供了一种区分不同能量依赖相互作用的新途径。

Abstract: Moving a detector through a beam with a spatially varying energy spectrum exposes the same target and apparatus to distinct incident spectra, enabling interaction spectroscopy. While movable neutrino detectors were put forth primarily to control systematic uncertainties, we show that detector motion enables probing the structure of fundamental interactions. We demonstrate this with the Intermediate Water Cherenkov Detector (IWCD), the movable detector of Hyper-Kamiokande in the J-PARC off-axis beam, considering neutrino non-standard interactions (NSI) as a benchmark. Exploiting ratios of neutral current to charged current event rates reduces common normalization uncertainties, while measurements at multiple off-axis positions can break degeneracies that persist for a single incident spectrum. Combining three off-axis positions and adopting a $5\%$ correlated normalization uncertainty benchmark, we project $95\%$ CL sensitivities to axial neutral current NSI of $-0.07 \lesssim \varepsilon^{uA}_{μμ} \lesssim 0.06$ and vector NSI of $-0.10 \lesssim \varepsilon^{uV}_{μμ} \lesssim 0.12$, as well as for charged current NSI of $-0.05 \lesssim \varepsilon^{udL}_{μμ} \lesssim 0.05$. Axial NSI, which do not contribute to the ordinary matter potential, are complementary to neutrino oscillation and high energy scattering measurements. More broadly, detector motion provides a new way to distinguish interactions with different energy dependence.

</details>


### [111] [Language-Guided Hypotheses Generation for Sparse SMEFT Analyses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04100)
*Ahmed Hammad,Veronica Sanz*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了llm4smeft，一个开源框架，通过将基于SMEFT文献微调的语言模型与基于SMEFiT全球拟合定量摘要的检索增强生成相结合，从大量算符中自动选择与给定观测量相关的候选有效算符，并输出对应的费舍尔信息（Fisher information），整个框架可在本地运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型有效场论（SMEFT）的全球拟合面临算符数量庞大而任何给定数据库仅约束其中一小部分的挑战，因此选择相关算符假设需要理论洞见来理解算符间的关联以及观测量对算符的敏感性。

Method: 该框架结合两种技术：(1) 在SMEFT文献上微调的语言模型（fine-tuned language model）；(2) 基于SMEFiT包全球拟合定量摘要的检索增强生成（retrieval augmented generation）。框架给定一组观测量后，提出候选相关算符及其费舍尔信息，并通过检索确保模型输出在可用时基于现有拟合结果。框架支持交互模式，被接受的假设会存入不断增长的知识库中。

Result: 该框架能够为给定观测量提出候选相关算符及对应的费舍尔信息，检索模块确保模型输出与已有拟合结果保持一致。整个框架（包括微调语言模型）可在本地完全运行，无需互联网访问或付费云服务，并已作为开源包公开发布。

Conclusion: llm4smeft为SMEFT全局拟合中的算符选择问题提供了一种结合机器学习与检索的实用解决方案，在开源、本地可运行的前提下实现了对相关算符假设的自动生成与知识积累。

Abstract: Global fits of the Standard Model Effective Field Theory are challenged by the large number of operators, while any given database constrains only a small subset. Selecting relevant operator hypotheses therefore requires theoretical insight into operator correlations and the sensitivity of observables. We present llm4smeft, an open source framework that addresses this problem by combining a language model, fine-tuned on the SMEFT literature, with retrieval augmented generation based on quantitative summaries of SMEFiT package global fits. Given a set of observables, the framework proposes candidate relevant operators together with their corresponding Fisher information, while retrieval ensures that model outputs are grounded in existing fit results whenever available. The framework runs in an interactive mode in which accepted hypotheses are stored in a growing knowledge base. We publicly release the llm4smeft package together with the fine-tuned language model, in which the entire framework runs locally, requiring neither internet access nor paid cloud services.

</details>


### [112] [Quarkoniumlike states above open-flavor thresholds in Born-Oppenheimer EFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04105)
*Nora Brambilla,Roberto Bruschini,Abhishek Mohapatra,Fang-Zheng Peng,Tommaso Scirpa*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Born–Oppenheimer有效场论（BOEFT）框架下研究了介于自旋-同位旋平均S+S与S+P阈值之间的同位旋标量隐粲（hidden-charm）和隐底（hidden-bottom）夸克偶素态（quarkoniumlike states）。通过弦断裂（string breaking）机制将夸克偶素静态势与最低四夸克/开味（open-flavor）BO势混合，并结合T矩阵、K矩阵和复标度方法确定束缚态与共振极点，揭示了重夸克自旋对称性多重态组织规律，为多数实验候选态提供了分配方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量夸克偶素类似态在开味阈值之上被观测到，但其组织和内部结构尚未确定，需要系统的理论框架来理解和分类这些态。

Method: 作者在Born–Oppenheimer有效场论（BOEFT）框架中研究两个能量区间（自旋-同位旋平均S+S与S+P阈值之间）的同位旋标量隐粧和隐底扇区。在领头阶，重夸克自旋退耦，夸克偶素静态势通过弦断裂与最低四夸克/开味BO势混合，这些势由QCD对称性、短长距离行为和晶格QCD数据约束。唯一的校准参数是最低1⁻⁻伴随介子质量，固定于与χ_c1(3872)相关的浅多重态。使用T矩阵、K矩阵和复标度方法求解束缚态与共振极点及其性质。

Result: 谱系表现出普遍的重夸克自旋对称性多重态组织结构；大多数极点是以短距离为主的夸克偶素共振，最大开味分量靠近阈值；同一方程组也产生浅近、空间延展、开味主导的分子态，其束缚能、半径和小的夸克偶素分量对伴随介子质量高度敏感，而较高能谱更为稳定。结合混合参考能级，为大多数实验候选态提供了多重态分配。

Conclusion: 谱系中无法自然容纳的状态表明，需要引入隐奇异（hidden-strange）和S+P四夸克/开味BO扇区，以及混合-四夸克和混合-夸克偶素混合效应，以完善该理论框架。

Abstract: Many quarkoniumlike states have been observed above open-flavor thresholds, but their organization and internal structure remain unsettled. We study the isoscalar hidden-charm and hidden-bottom sectors in Born--Oppenheimer effective field theory (BOEFT), between the spin--isospin averaged $S+S$ and $S+P$ thresholds. At leading order, heavy-quark spin decouples, and the quarkonium static potential mixes through string breaking with the lowest tetraquark/open-flavor BO potentials of the same quantum numbers. These potentials are constrained by QCD symmetries, their short- and long-distance behavior, and lattice-QCD data. The only calibrated parameter is the lowest $1^{--}$ adjoint meson mass, fixed from the shallow multiplet associated with the $χ_{c1}(3872)$. Using $T$-matrix, $K$-matrix, and complex-scaling methods, we determine bound states and resonance poles, their masses, pole widths from the included nonstrange $S+S$ channels, normalized pole couplings, and prescription-dependent quarkonium--open-flavor composition measures. Uncoupled hybrid BOEFT multiplets are included as reference levels. The spectrum exhibits a common heavy-quark-spin-symmetry multiplet organization. Most poles are predominantly quarkonium resonances localized at short distances, with the largest open-flavor components closest to threshold. The same equations also generate shallow, spatially extended, open-flavor-dominated states with molecular long-distance characteristics. Their binding energies, radii, and small quarkonium components are highly sensitive to the adjoint meson mass, whereas the higher spectrum is more stable. Together with the hybrid reference levels, the spectrum provides multiplet assignments for most candidates. States not naturally accommodated point to the need for hidden-strange and $S+P$ tetraquark/open-flavor BO sectors and for hybrid--tetraquark and hybrid--quarkonium mixings.

</details>


### [113] [Open-Charm Vector Mesons in Hot and Dense Nuclear Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04236)
*N. Er,K. Azizi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在有限温度和有限密度的量子色动力学求和规则（QCD sum rules）框架下，研究了热密核物质中开放粲味矢量介子 D_s* 和 D* 的介质内性质，发现两种介子均发生显著的质量软化，其质量和轻子衰变常数随重子密度变化呈现不同趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索开放粲味矢量介子在热密核物质中的介质内性质，以理解强相互作用物质在极端条件下的行为及其对介子质量谱和衰变常数的调制效应。

Method: 采用有限温度和有限密度的量子色动力学求和规则方法，纳入随温度和密度变化的夸克凝聚（quark condensate）与胶子凝聚（gluon condensate），并引入由轻夸克凝聚约束的介质内连续谱阈值；通过求解求和规则获得介子质量和轻子衰变常数的介质内演化。

Result: 两种矢量介子均发生显著介质内软化，质量随重子密度增加出现非单调变化，而轻子衰变常数在研究的密度范围内单调递减；最大质量偏移在中等至高密度区间达到约 -413 MeV（D_s*⁻）和 -207 MeV（D*⁻），轻子衰变常数在最高密度下减少超过 68%；温度升高总体削弱密度诱导的修正，并抑制粒子-反粒子对称性劈裂，但重子密度仍是介质内演化的主导因素。

Conclusion: 有限重子密度打破电荷共轭态的真空简并，产生粒子-反粒子质量与轻子衰变常数的劈裂；奇异与非奇异通道在定性行为上相似，但介质内修正和粒子-反粒子劈裂存在定量差异。

Abstract: We investigate the in-medium properties of the open-charm vector mesons $D_s^{*}$ and $D^{*}$ in hot and dense nuclear matter within the framework of finite-temperature and finite-density QCD sum rules. The analysis incorporates temperature- and density-dependent quark and gluon condensates together with an in-medium continuum threshold constrained by the light-quark condensate. By solving the resulting QCD sum rules, we determine the in-medium masses and leptonic decay constants of the $D_s^{*\pm}$ and $D^{*\pm}$ mesons over a broad region of the $(T,ρ)$ plane. Both vector mesons undergo substantial in-medium softening, with their masses and leptonic decay constants decreasing as the baryon density increases. The masses exhibit a non-monotonic dependence on baryon density, whereas the leptonic decay constants decrease monotonically throughout the investigated density range. Increasing temperature generally weakens the density-induced modifications, although baryon density remains the dominant driver of the in-medium evolution. The largest mass shifts occur at intermediate-to-high densities, reaching approximately $-413~\mathrm{MeV}$ for the $D_s^{*-}$ meson and $-207~\mathrm{MeV}$ for the $D^{*-}$ meson, while the leptonic decay constants are reduced by more than $68\%$ in both channels at the highest densities considered. We further investigate the particle--antiparticle splittings of the masses and leptonic decay constants induced by finite baryon density. Finite baryon density lifts the vacuum degeneracy between the charge-conjugate states, while increasing temperature generally suppresses the resulting asymmetries. Although the strange and non-strange channels exhibit similar qualitative behavior, quantitative differences emerge in both the in-medium modifications and the particle--antiparticle splittings. ....

</details>


### [114] [Direct versus resolved photons in DPS in photoproduction on proton and nuclei](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04259)
*B. Blok,R. Segev*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了未来电子-离子对撞机（EIC）和HERA上光生过程中双部分子散射（DPS）的机制，发现分辨光子的1→2过程即使在硬过程横动量很小的情况下也能将DPS增强约1.6倍，并确定了直接光子贡献主导的运动学区域边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解光生过程中的双部分子散射（DPS）机制对于未来EIC和HERA的物理研究至关重要，尤其是分辨光子与直接光子贡献的区分及其对DPS大小的影响有待系统研究。

Method: 论文针对EIC和HERA的未来光生过程，比较了分辨光子（resolved photon）情形下1→2过程与基于平均场（mean field）模型的预测，分析了DPS的增强因子，并研究了直接光子（direct photon）贡献主导的运动学区域，包括粲夸克和轻夸克喷注的相空间边界。

Result: 在分辨光子情形下，即使硬过程的横动量较小，1→2过程在相空间的重要区域内可使DPS相对于平均场模型预测增强约1.6倍。对粲夸克喷注而言，直接光子主导的区域为x_γ≥0.2–0.4，且在核对撞（nuclei）光生中该区域进一步扩大。

Conclusion: 研究表明分辨光子贡献对DPS有显著增强效应，并明确了直接光子贡献主导的动力学边界；在核对撞机上粲夸克喷注的直接光子主导区域更宽，有助于后续实验对DPS机制的检验。

Abstract: We study the process of double parton scattering (DPS) associated with the photoproduction at a future Electron-Ion collider (EIC) and HERA. We show that in the case of the resolved photon the 1 to 2
  processes lead, even at small transverse momenta of the hard processes , to the increase of the DPS by a factor of order 1.6 in the significant part of the phase space, relative to the predictions of the mean field based models . Moreover we study the kinematic region where direct photon contribution is dominant and show it s boundaries for charm and light quark jets. For charmed jets we see that the relevant region is $x_γ\ge 0.2-0.4$. This region is even enhanced if we consider the photoproduction on the nuclei.

</details>


### [115] [Probing the CP violation effects via the angular coefficients in Drell-Yan production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04437)
*Qingyi Situ,Bin Yan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Drell–Yan过程中的naive-T-odd角系数A₅–A₇作为CP破坏新物理探针，在标准模型有效场论（SMEFT）框架下分析维度六电弱与色磁夸克偶极算符的贡献，发现A₆和A₇在高双轻子横动量区域最敏感，结合ATLAS和CMS数据可将Wilson系数约束到O(0.1)水平，HL-LHC下可提升至O(10⁻³)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型（Standard Model, SM）中Drell–Yan过程的naive-T-odd角系数A₅–A₇被强烈压低，使其成为探测超越标准模型CP破坏相互作用的灵敏观测量。该研究旨在利用这些可观测量在SMEFT框架下约束CP破坏的偶极相互作用。

Method: 作者在标准模型有效场论（Standard Model Effective Field Theory, SMEFT）框架内分析维度六电弱与色磁夸克偶极算符的贡献。利用偶极算符的手征翻转（chirality-flipping）结构，计算其与电弱和色磁偶极振幅干涉产生的O(1/Λ⁴)阶前导贡献，并分别评估A₅、A₆和A₇在不同双轻子横动量区域的表现。

Result: 研究发现灵敏度主要由A₆和A₇在高双轻子横动量区域驱动，偶极贡献在该区域得到增强，而A₅基本不敏感。结合ATLAS和CMS测量，在Λ=1 TeV下可将CP破坏偶极Wilson系数组合约束至O(0.1)水平；假设HL-LHC下统计主导的不确定性，灵敏度可改善至O(10⁻³)。

Conclusion: 结果表明A₆和A₇是强子对撞机上探测CP破坏偶极相互作用的互补性探针（complementary probes），为该类新物理信号提供了新的实验检验途径。

Abstract: The naive-$T$-odd angular coefficients $A_5$--$A_7$ in Drell--Yan production are highly suppressed in the Standard Model (SM), making them sensitive probes of CP-violating interactions beyond the SM. We study these observables within the Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT), focusing on dimension-six electroweak and chromomagnetic quark dipole operators. Due to their chirality-flipping structure, the leading contributions from CP-violating dipole interactions arise at $\mathcal{O}(1/Λ^4)$ through the interference between electroweak and chromomagnetic dipole amplitudes. We find that the sensitivity is primarily driven by $A_6$ and $A_7$ in the high dilepton transverse momentum region, where the dipole contributions are enhanced, while $A_5$ remains largely insensitive. Combining the ATLAS and CMS measurements, we constrain the CP-violating dipole combinations of Wilson coefficients at the $\mathcal{O}(0.1)$ level for $Λ=1~{\rm TeV}$. Assuming statistically dominated uncertainties at the HL-LHC, the sensitivity can be improved to the $\mathcal{O}(10^{-3})$ level. Our results highlight $A_6$ and $A_7$ as complementary probes of CP-violating dipole interactions at hadron colliders.

</details>


### [116] [A large size of the pion-like excitations in the stringy fluid above $T_{ch}$ is model independent and required by current algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04563)
*L. Ya. Glozman*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于格点QCD证据与手征有效模型，论证QCD在手征对称恢复温度（约155 MeV）之上存在一个禁闭但手征对称的弦状流体相（stringy fluid regime），并以流代数（current algebra）证明该区域中π介子的膨胀（swelling）是与模型无关的必然效应，这为实验观测到的高横动量集体性提供了解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解QCD在手征对称恢复温度之上区域的物质形态与动力学机制，特别是该区域中禁闭与手征对称共存的性质，以及为何实验上观测到该温度附近的夸克物质具有极强的集体性和极小的平均自由程。

Method: 结合多个格点QCD（lattice QCD）证据（空间与时间关联子中提取的π、π'峰）以及手征对称且含禁闭的模型分析，最终利用流代数（current algebra）这一模型无关的方法推导π介子膨胀的必然性。

Result: 格点数据显示T_ch~155 MeV之上存在手征自旋对称及其扩展的近似的禁闭区域，π、π'峰的宽度随温度增加并在约3T_ch处消失；模型的在手征极限下低自旋介子在T_ch之上无限膨胀；流代数证明该膨胀是模型无关的一般结论。

Conclusion: 手征对称恢复温度之上π介子的膨胀是流代数保证的普适效应，与具体模型无关，这一性质对解释实验观测到的高集体性和极小平均自由程的强子化物质行为至关重要。

Abstract: Multiple lattice evidences support the existence of a confining but chirally symmetric stringy fluid regime of QCD above the chiral symmetry restoration temperature at T_{ch} ~ 155 MeV. This regime is characterized by an approximate chiral spin symmetry and its extensions which means that the propagating excitations represent the chirally symmetric quarks connected into color singlets by the chromoelectric string. Clear π,π' peaks above T_{ch} were extracted on the lattice from the spatial and temporal correlators, which become broader with temperature and disappear roughly at $3T_{ch}$. The meson-like excitations above T_{ch} were studied within the manifestly confining and chirally symmetric model. It has been demonstrated that the chiral symmetry restoration in the confining regime happens because of Pauli blocking of the levels, required for the existence of the quark condensate, by the thermal quark excitation. The same Pauli blocking leads to a huge swelling of the low-spin mesons above T_{ch} which become infinitely large in the chiral limit. This property should be crucial for the explanation of the high collectivity and a very small mean-free path of the constituents above $T_{ch}$ seen experimentally. Here we demonstrate that the swelling of pions above T_{ch} is a model-independent effect required by current algebra.

</details>


### [117] [CP Polarimetry with Linearly Polarized Photon Fusion and Double-Tagged Protons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05034)
*Qi-Hui Chang,Shuai Zhao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用双前向质子标记（double forward-proton tagging）技术，将前向探测器转化为逐事件光子偏振计（photon polarimeters），从而实现对光子耦合的自旋零共振（spin-zero resonance）的CP相位进行与衰变分析器无关的测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的CP相位测量通常依赖于共振粒子的衰变产物分析，而该研究寻求一种独立的、基于产生侧（production-side）信息的新测量途径，以适用于轴子类粒子（axionlike particles）及其他自旋零共振。

Method: 利用双前向质子标记技术：每个被测量的质子反冲固定了发射光子的横动量及其线偏振轴，从而将前向探测器转化为逐事件光子偏振计。作者推导了具有标量和赝标量耦合的CP混合硬振幅的领阶光子密度收缩（leading-power photon-density contraction），并发现CP相位表现为带符号的质子-质子方位角二阶谐波的平移。

Result: 推导出产生侧偏振测量能够提供与衰变分析器无关的CP相位测量；CP相位以带符号的质子-质子方位角第二谐波（second harmonic）平移的形式显现，为轴子类粒子和更一般的自旋零共振提供了紧凑的产生侧CP测量方案。

Conclusion: 该方案确立了前向探测器作为光子偏振计的可行性，为光子耦合自旋零共振的CP性质研究提供了一种不依赖衰变通道的新实验途径。

Abstract: Double forward-proton tagging turns the forward detectors into event-by-event photon polarimeters because each measured proton recoil fixes the transverse momentum, and hence the linear-polarization axis, of the emitted photon. We show that this production-side polarimetry gives a decay-analyzer-independent measurement of the CP phase of a photon-coupled spin-zero resonance. We derive the leading-power photon-density contraction for a CP-mixed hard amplitude with scalar and pseudoscalar couplings. The CP phase appears as a translation of the second harmonic in the signed proton--proton azimuthal angle. This provides a compact production-side CP measurement for axionlike particles and more general spin-zero resonances.

</details>


### [118] [Generalized Glauber theorem for dark-matter axion and graviton detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05082)
*Jakub Bręczewski,Ayuki Kamada,Akira Miyazaki*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视广义Glauber定理（generalized Glauber theorem），将其应用于暗物质轴子（axion）探测和引力子（graviton）产生的量子动力学描述，通过将含二次项哈密顿量的时间演化算子分解为位移、压缩和旋转算子的乘积，将量子动力学约化为c数方程，并统一了从经典背景产生的粒子（particle production）描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期暗物质轴子搜索和引力子产生的研究推动了广义Glauber定理的新应用需求，但该理论框架尚未被系统梳理和推广。作者旨在提供一个统一的数学形式，涵盖从线性到非线性相互作用下的粒子产生，并为基础实验中的量子描述建立更严格的理论基础。

Method: 对含二次项（up to quadratic terms）的产生与湮灭算子耦合经典源的哈密顿量，将时间演化算子分解为位移（displacement）、压缩（squeezing）和旋转（rotation）算子三部分，导出其参数随时间演化的微分方程，将量子动力学位移为可解析或数值求解的c数方程。同时与Dyson级数（Dyson series）对比，展示时间排序（time ordering）的关键作用，并将定理推广至热初态。

Result: 轴子-光子转换（axion-photon conversion）在微波空腔实验中被恢复为线性相互作用极限，而黑洞并合产生的压缩引力子由二次相互作用自然导出。热初态推广给出了轴子实验微波腔的量子描述，高阶光子统计（photon statistics）呈现非平凡行为，为量子增强轴子搜索的蒙特卡洛模拟提供了严格基础。

Conclusion: 基于广义Glauber定理的框架为经典背景下的粒子产生提供统一描述，超越了此前基于启发式噪声估计的方法，为量子增强轴子搜索实验的模拟和设计奠定了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: We revisit the generalized Glauber theorem motivated by recent applications to dark-matter axion searches and graviton production. For Hamiltonian containing up to quadratic terms in annihilation and creation operators coupled to classical sources, the time-evolution operator can be factorized into displacement, squeezing, and rotation operators. We derive the differential equations governing time evolution of their parameters, reducing the quantum dynamics to c-number equations that can be solved analytically or numerically. To compare another form of the time-evolution operator, Dyson series, we demonstrate the essential role of time ordering. This formalism based on generalized Glauber theorem provides a unified description of particle production from classical backgrounds. Axion-photon conversion in microwave haloscopes is recovered as the linear-interaction limit, while squeezed graviton production from black-hole mergers follows naturally from quadratic interactions. Extending the theorem to thermal initial states yields a realistic quantum description of microwave cavities used in axion experiments. We show that higher-order photon statistics exhibit nontrivial behavior, providing a rigorous foundation for Monte Carlo simulations of quantum-enhanced axion searches beyond heuristic noise estimates.

</details>


### [119] [High energy probes of Higgs self-coupling via $W$ boson fusion at future lepton colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05096)
*Amir Subba,Hrishikesh Deka,Subhaditya Bhattacharya,Abhik Sarkar*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该研究在CLIC对撞机上以质心能量3 TeV的W玻色子融合双希格斯（di-Higgs）产生过程为研究对象，在κ框架内探讨希格斯自耦合修正因子（κλ）与希格斯-规范耦合修正因子（κV和κ2V）之间的相互作用，并基于图神经网络（graph neural network, GNN）分类器实现了约20σ的信号显著性，显著超越HL-LHC的预期灵敏度，展示了高能轻子对撞机结合图机器学习对电弱物理新物理的探测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测量希格斯自耦合是验证标准模型电弱对称性破缺机制的关键，然而现有探测手段（如HL-LHC）对希格斯自耦合的灵敏度有限，需要探索新的对撞机平台和分析方法以提高测量精度并甄别新物理信号。

Method: 在CLIC（质心能量√s=3 TeV）上研究W玻色子融合双希格斯产生过程，在κ框架内同时考察希格斯自耦合修正因子（κλ）与希格斯-规范耦合修正因子（κV、κ2V）之间的相互作用，并设计基于图神经网络（graph neural network, GNN）的分类器以增强信号与背景的区分能力。

Result: 基于GNN的分类器在5 ab⁻¹积分亮度下实现了约20σ的信号显著性，显著超过HL-LHC的预期灵敏度；结果表明高能轻子对撞机结合基于图的机器学习方法能够对希格斯自耦合提供出色的灵敏度，并可有效区分线性与非线性实现的电弱对称性破缺。

Conclusion: 高能轻子对撞机（如CLIC）与基于图的机器学习方法相结合，为希格斯自耦合的精确测量以及电弱扇区内新物理的探测提供了强大工具，能够甄别电弱对称性破缺的线性与非线性实现方式。

Abstract: We investigate the sensitivity to the Higgs self-coupling through $W$ boson fusion di-Higgs production at CLIC with a center-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s}=3$ TeV. We study the interplay between the Higgs self-coupling modifier ($κ_λ$) and Higgs-gauge coupling modifiers ($κ_{V}$ and $κ_{2V}$) within the $κ$ framework. To enhance the separation between signal and background, we develop a graph neural network (GNN) based classifier that achieves a signal significance of $\mathscr{Z}\approx 20~σ$ at $5~\mathrm{ab}^{-1}$, substantially exceeding projected HL-LHC sensitivity. Our results demonstrate that high-energy lepton colliders, combined with graph-based machine learning, provide excellent sensitivity to the Higgs self-coupling and offer a powerful probe of new physics in the electroweak sector, disentangling linearly and non-linearly realized electroweak symmetry breaking.

</details>


### [120] [Scalaron-driven Dark Matter during Warm Inflation via UV Freeze--in](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.00874)
*F. Millo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了暖Higgs–Starobinsky暴胀（warm Higgs–Starobinsky inflation）框架下的暗物质（Dark Matter, DM）产生机制。采用紫外冻结（UV freeze-in）机制，研究发现暗物质在HS势中的产生具有特征性时机——即暴胀结束并过渡到辐射主导（radiation dominated, RD）时期附近。对于两种暗物质质量（1 MeV和100 GeV），在强耗散区域中通过质量维度为D={8,9}的非重整化算符均可生成今日暗物质遗迹丰度（relic abundance）。结果表明HS势的形式及相应的标量场（scalaron）动力学有利于暗物质在势能最小值附近产生，此时能量向热浴的转移尤为高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索暖暴胀框架下暗物质产生的具体动力学机制，特别是Higgs–Starobinsky（HS）暴胀模型中紫外冻结（UV freeze-in）机制产生的暗物质是否具有区别于其他暴胀模型的特征行为，以深化对暗物质产生过程的理解。

Method: 采用紫外冻结机制（UV freeze-in mechanism），假设暗物质数密度初始可忽略，通过具有确定质量维度的非重整化相互作用随时间累积暗物质。在暖Higgs–Starobinsky（warm HS）暴胀势中考察两种暗物质质量（m_χ=1 MeV和m_χ=100 GeV）的产生过程，并分析不同质量维度（D={8,9}）的非重整化算符在强耗散（strong dissipation）区域中的作用。

Result: 研究发现暗物质产量（yield）在暴胀终止并向辐射主导（RD）时期过渡的附近时刻产生，这一特征性时序对两种暗物质质量均成立。在强耗散区域中，利用质量维度D={8,9}的非重整化算符可为两种质量（1 MeV和100 GeV）的暗物质产生与今日遗迹丰度（relic abundance）一致的结果。

Conclusion: HS势的形式及其标量场（scalaron）动力学倾向于在标量场接近势能最小值时促进紫外冻结（UV freeze-in）暗物质的产生，此时能量向热浴的转移特别高效。这一独特的过渡行为使HS模型区别于其他暴胀模型，推进了对暗物质产生机制的理解。

Abstract: We investigate the production of Dark Matter (DM) within the warm Higgs--Starobinsky (HS) inflation. By adopting the ultraviolet (UV) freeze--in mechanism -- where the DM number density is initially negligible but is populated over time through non-renormalizable interactions of defined mass dimensions -- we find that the DM production within the HS potential has a characteristic timing. For both DM masses, $m_χ=1$ MeV and $m_χ=100$ GeV, the yield of DM occurs close to the termination of inflation and its transition to the radiation dominated (RD) era. The present--day DM relic abundance is generated for both DM masses with non-renormalizable operators of mass dimension $D=\{8,9\}$ within strong dissipation regimes. The obtained results suggest that the form of the HS potential and the associated scalaron dynamics favor UV freeze--in DM production as the scalaron approaches the minimum of the potential, where energy transfer to the thermal bath becomes particularly efficient. This distinctive transition behavior differentiates the HS model from other inflationary models and advances our understanding of DM production.

</details>


### [121] [Consistent Thermal Resummation and Phase Transitions with 2PI Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04102)
*Amitayus Banik,Kimmo Kainulainen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出使用双粒子不可约（2PI）形式论作为宇宙学相变研究中一致的有限温度热重求和框架。在Hartree近似下重整化包含两个混合实标量场的模型，构建了适用于所有温度的Hartree重求和有限温度有效势，并研究了该模型中的一级与二级相变及引力波谱，证明了不同重求和方案对预测结果的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙学相变研究中传统重求和方案（如高温近似）存在局限，无法适用于所有温度范围。作者旨在通过2PI形式论建立一种一致的热重求和框架，克服传统方法的限制，以获得更可靠的相变研究和引力波谱预测。

Method: 作者将2PI形式论应用于包含两个混合实标量场的模型，在Hartree近似下工作，对2PI有效作用量进行重整化并将理论参数与物理参数建立联系，从而得到适用于所有温度的Hartree重求和有限温度有效势。

Result: 利用所得有效势，作者研究了模型中的一级和二级相变（one- and two-step transitions），与文献中广泛使用的重求和方案进行了对比，并评估了引力波谱。结果表明重求和方案的选择会对预测的引力波谱产生显著影响。

Conclusion: 2PI形式论为宇宙学相变研究提供了一致的热重求和框架，所得结果可用于更可靠地预测相变特性及相应的引力波信号，对引力波天文学与粒子物理交叉研究具有参考价值。

Abstract: We apply the two-particle irreducible (2PI) formalism as a framework for a consistent thermal resummation in studies of cosmological phase transitions. Considering a model with two mixing real scalar fields, we work within the Hartree approximation and renormalize the 2PI effective action, while introducing a connection to physical parameters. This yields the Hartree-resummed finite-temperature effective potential, which is valid for all temperatures and avoids the limitations of conventional methods based on the high-temperature approximation. With this potential, we study one- and two-step transitions within the model, and compare our results with those obtained using resummation schemes widely employed in the literature. Finally, we evaluate the gravitational wave spectrum generated from first-order phase transitions, demonstrating the impact of the choice of resummation scheme on the predicted spectrum.

</details>


### [122] [Amnesia in the Axion Misalignment Landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04139)
*Kierthika Chathirathas,Cem Eröncel,Mathieu Kaltschmidt,Javier Redondo,Kenichi Saikawa*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文使用格点模拟（lattice simulations）研究QCD轴子（axion）在动学错位（kinetic misalignment）场景下穿过QCD跨接（QCD crossover）直至物质-辐射等时（matter-radiation equality）的非线性演化，发现初始速度与角度构成广阔的错位历史（misalignment histories）景观，其中meV质量区存在连续的"峰顶着陆"（summit-landing）解，非线性碎裂会减少共动轴子数并改变错位历史与当前丰度之间的关系，而大质量轴子的强自相互作用会将小尺度谱驱动至暴胀后轴子（post-inflationary axion）吸引子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暴胀前轴子宇宙学允许广泛的初始速度和角度，生成多种错位历史景观，但其非线性演化和小尺度结构尚不确定，需要数值模拟来澄清。

Method: 采用格点模拟跟踪QCD轴子在动学错位场景下穿越QCD跨接进入非相对论区间，直至物质-辐射等时，覆盖meV质量范围。

Result: 发现meV质量区内存在对初始角度和速度都敏感的连续"峰顶着陆"解；非线性碎裂减少共动轴子数并修改错位历史与当前遗迹丰度（relic abundance）的关联；对大质量轴子，强自相互作用将小尺度谱驱动至暴胀后轴子的吸引子，使不同早期历史可收敛至相似的晚期结构。

Conclusion: 不同错位历史在晚期可能产生相似的小尺度结构，表明早期动力学信息在一定条件下会被吸引子机制抹去，这对理解和预言轴子暗物质的晚期结构具有重要含义。

Abstract: Pre-inflationary axion cosmologies admit a broad range of initial velocities and angles that generate a landscape of misalignment histories, whose nonlinear fate and small-scale structure remain uncertain. We use lattice simulations to follow the QCD axion in the kinetic misalignment scenario through the QCD crossover into the non-relativistic regime, towards matter-radiation equality. In the meV mass range, this landscape contains a continuum of ''summit-landing" solutions sensitive to both the initial angle and velocity. Nonlinear fragmentation reduces the comoving axion number and modifies the relation between the misalignment history and present relic abundance. For sufficiently large axion masses, strong self-interactions drive the small-scale spectrum towards the attractor found for post-inflationary axions, so that distinct early histories can lead to similar late-time structures.

</details>

### [123] [Laminar gaps mirror turbulent puffs in pipe flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04823)
*Shai Kapon,Tobias Grafke,Anna Frishman*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该研究通过直接数值模拟（direct numerical simulation）研究圆管流在中等雷诺数（Reynolds number）下的层流空隙（laminar gap）现象，发现这些空隙构成一种与"puff"类似的独立动力学状态，其寿命呈指数分布，并在有限雷诺数（Re≈2900）处失稳消失，标志湍流空间均匀性的起点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圆管流在层流与完全湍流之间存在多种时空间歇性状态，其中低雷诺数区的"puff"结构已得到充分研究，但高雷诺数段（Re∈2300-3000）中层流空隙的动力学性质尚未被系统阐明，其与puff的关系及稳定性机制有待揭示。

Method: 对圆管流在Re=2400、2450、2500和2550四个雷诺数下进行直接数值模拟，分析层流空隙的平均空间剖面（mean spatial profile），并统计其寿命分布以考察其随机动力学特征。

Result: 层流空隙形成与puff类似的独立动力学状态，即在湍流环境中迁移的层流口袋，由剪切依赖的自调节（shear-dependent self-tuning）机制维持稳定；其寿命呈指数分布，表明空隙闭合对应从混沌鞍（chaotic saddle）的逃逸；这些空隙在Re≈2900处失稳消失，对应空间与时间均匀湍流的起点。

Conclusion: 层流空隙是与puff对偶的动力学状态，二者共同揭示了圆管流过渡态的普适性结构；空隙在有限雷诺数处的失稳标志着湍流从间歇性向空间均匀性的转变，为理解管道流过渡（transition）机制提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Pipe flow at intermediate Reynolds numbers, between the laminar and fully turbulent regimes, takes the form of several spatially and temporally intermittent phases in which turbulent and laminar states coexist. In the lower range, $Re\in (1750,2300)$, turbulence appears in the form of localized traveling structures called "puffs", which form long-lived chaotic dynamical states, whose stochastic decays and splits control the steady state intermittency. At the other end, $Re\in (2300,3000)$, puffs are replaced by an extended turbulent state, with laminar pockets intermittently forming and disappearing within it. Using direct numerical simulations of pipe flow at $Re = 2400, 2450, 2500, 2550$, we provide evidence that these laminar gaps form a distinct dynamical state analogous to puffs: a traveling laminar pocket in a turbulent surrounding, stabilized by a shear-dependent self-tuning mechanism. We analyse the mean spatial profile of these gaps and show that their lifetimes are exponentially distributed, suggesting that gap closing corresponds to an escape from a chaotic saddle. Finally, we suggest these laminar gaps become unstable and disappear at a finite Reynolds number, $Re\sim 2900$, which can be interpreted as the onset point of spatially and temporally homogeneous turbulence.

</details>

### [124] [The Born Representation Theorem and the Unistochastic Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04354)
*Jacob A. Barandes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并构造性证明了关于随机矩阵（stochastic matrices）的两个新定理，即Born表示定理（Born Representation Theorem）和酉随机定理（Unistochastic Theorem），表明任意随机矩阵的元素均可通过量子力学Born规则（Born rule）的广义形式表达，并可通过扩张向量空间转化为更大酉随机矩阵的边际化结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究随机矩阵与量子理论中酉矩阵（unitary matrix）及Born规则之间的深层数学联系，并建立酉随机矩阵相对于一般随机矩阵的某种优先性（primacy），从而暗示随机过程与量子酉时间演化的内在关联。

Method: 采用自洽的构造性证明（constructive proofs）方法，提出了两个新定理。第一个定理将任意随机矩阵的每个元素表示为成对矩阵乘积的迹，其中第一个因子属于正算子值测度（POVM），第二个因子属于投影值测度（PVM）；第二个定理通过对底层向量空间进行有界维数的扩张（dilation），使得成对乘积的两个因子均属于PVM，从而由更大的酉随机矩阵通过边际化（marginalization）得到原随机矩阵。

Result: 证明了任意随机矩阵的每个条目均可表示为广义Born规则的迹形式；当第一定理中的POVM退化为PVM时，随机矩阵即为酉随机矩阵，其元素为相应酉矩阵元素的模平方。第二定理表明，通过有界维数扩张，任意随机矩阵可从更大的酉随机矩阵边际化得到。

Conclusion: 论文确立了酉随机矩阵相对于一般随机矩阵的优先地位，暗示了与量子理论中酉时间演化的密切关系，并讨论了在离散时间确定性过程（deterministic processes）和马尔可夫链（Markov chains）中的潜在应用。

Abstract: This paper presents self-contained, constructive proofs of two new theorems about stochastic matrices, with direct relevance to quantum theory. The first theorem, herein called the Born Representation Theorem, shows that each entry of any stochastic matrix can be expressed as the trace of a pairwise product of matrices, where the first factor in the pairwise product belongs to a positive-operator-valued measure (POVM) and the second factor belongs to a projection-valued measure (PVM). As its name suggests, this theorem entails that the entries of any stochastic matrix can be expressed in terms of a generalized version of the quantum-theoretic Born rule. It follows as a corollary that if the POVM in this first theorem is a PVM, then the stochastic matrix is unistochastic, meaning that its entries are each the modulus square of the corresponding entry of a unitary matrix of the same size. The second theorem proved in this paper, called the Unistochastic Theorem, then shows that by dilating the underlying vector space by a bounded number of additional dimensions if necessary, each entry of any stochastic matrix can be expressed in terms of the trace of a pairwise product for which both factors belong to PVMs, and can thus be derived via marginalization from a larger unistochastic matrix. This second theorem therefore establishes a kind of primacy of unistochastic matrices over stochastic matrices, and hints at a close connection with unitary time evolution in quantum theory. The paper concludes with a brief discussion of potential applications to discrete-time deterministic processes and Markov chains.

</details>


### [125] [Constructive realization of self-referential prediction limits in quantum control: Resource bounds and Gödel-safe architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04779)
*Salman Sajad Wani,Álvaro Perales-Eceiza,Saif Al-Kuwari,Mir Faizal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于Wolpert的通用自预测不可能性定理，利用Kleene递归定理将任何确定性有界时间预测器转化为可逆协议，构造了一个能够矛盾于预测器预测结果的实验设计，并将其具体实现为容错量子电路和最小Mach-Zehnder干涉仪。作者还提出并定义了Gödel安全架构，用以阻断协议描述到影响实验指针的致动器之间的因果路径，并分析了其对实时量子纠错的表达力权衡影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可编程量子控制系统日益依赖预测模块进行认证、实时反馈和自主决策，这引发根本性疑问：自分析量子平台能否普遍预测自身的实验输出？Wolpert已形式化证明了通用自预测的不可能性，但该限制尚未转化为可在有限资源下实现的明确实验室阻塞（laboratory obstruction），本文旨在填补这一理论与实验之间的鸿沟。

Method: 作者采用对角化（diagonal construction）方法，借助Kleene递归定理（Kleene's recursion theorem）将任何确定性有界时间预测器转换为可逆协议，该协议编码其自身规格并在同一运行中调用预测器，从而确定性地产生与预测相矛盾的经典指针记录。对于高效预测器，编译过程具有多项式开销，可具体实现为容错量子电路和最小Mach-Zehnder干涉仪。此外，作者引入并正式定义Gödel安全架构（Gödel-safe architectures），阻断从协议描述到可影响指针的致动器之间的禁止因果路径。

Result: 研究结果表明，通用自预测的不可能性可以转化为有限资源的显式实验室实验设计：所构造的可逆协议可确定性地产生与预测相矛盾的指针记录，且高效预测器对应的编译具有多项式开销。容错量子电路和最小Mach-Zehnder干涉仪两种具体实现将可计算性理论中的自指（self-reference）与可编程量子硬件连接起来。Gödel安全架构能够阻断禁止因果路径，但同时引入了实时量子纠错的表达力权衡。

Conclusion: 随着量子控制回路的计算表达力（computational expressiveness）不断增长，自指的极限不再只是数学抽象，而是成为自主量子技术可靠运行中明确的工程约束。该工作为理解自分析量子平台在预测自身输出时的内在局限提供了具体可操作的实验框架。

Abstract: Programmable quantum control systems increasingly rely on predictive modules for certification, real-time feedback, and autonomous decision-making. This development raises a fundamental question: can self-analyzing quantum platforms universally predict their own experimental outcomes? Wolpert formalized a general impossibility of universal self-prediction. Here we translate that limitation into an explicit laboratory obstruction that can be realized with finite resources. We consider settings with programmable quantum control in which predictors can be embedded as subroutines within the experiments they analyze. Our diagonal construction uses Kleene's recursion theorem to transform any deterministic bounded-time predictor into a reversible protocol encoding its own specification. The resulting protocol invokes the predictor on that specification and deterministically produces a classical pointer record that contradicts the forecast. For efficient predictors, the compilation has polynomial overhead and admits concrete physical realizations as a fault-tolerant quantum circuit and as a minimal Mach-Zehnder interferometer. These realizations connect computability-theoretic self-reference to programmable quantum hardware. We also introduce and formally define Gödel-safe architectures. These architectures block the forbidden causal path from the protocol description to an actuator that can affect the pointer during the same run. We analyze their implications for real-time quantum error correction, including the resulting expressiveness trade-offs. As quantum control loops grow in computational expressiveness, the limits of self-reference cease to be mere mathematical abstractions and become explicit engineering constraints for the reliable operation of autonomous quantum technologies.

</details>


### [126] [Two-dimensional Toda--Arnoldi correspondence: Holomorphic Krylov geometry and counterdiabatic transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04850)
*Urei Miura*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了非厄米哈密顿量的Arnoldi约化（Arnoldi reduction）与二维Toda格（two-dimensional Toda lattice）之间的联系，发现闭合Toda扇区精确对应Arnoldi系数的对角与次对角项，且这些系数同时承载Krylov子空间的几何信息，并可用于实现精确等谱输运与逆绝热驱动（counterdiabatic driving）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非厄米哈密顿量在Krylov空间约化中的代数与几何结构，特别是超越传统Hermitian Lanczos理论框架的推广需求。

Method: 通过将有限维非厄米哈密顿量的Arnoldi约化与二维Toda格理论建立精确对应，利用Krylov Gram行列式作为Toda晶格的τ函数（τ function），识别Arnoldi矩阵的对角与次对角系数为Flaschka变量，并进一步分析其几何意义及动态闭合性。

Result: 证明了闭合Toda扇区在Arnoldi矩阵的对角与次对角系数上存在且动力学闭合；次对角系数同时决定Fubini–Study度量与Berry曲率；沿光滑实路径，Arnoldi框架联络给出了厄米三对角等谱输运生成元，可实现可对角化且谱非简并情形下的逆绝热驱动。

Conclusion: Arnoldi约化的代数结构与Toda格动力学及Krylov子空间几何紧密关联，为非厄米量子体系的等谱输运和绝热控制提供了统一的理论框架。

Abstract: Although Arnoldi reduction of a generally non-Hermitian Hamiltonian yields an upper Hessenberg matrix rather than the tridiagonal form of Hermitian Lanczos theory, we show that a closed Toda sector survives in its diagonal and subdiagonal coefficients. For a fixed finite-dimensional Hamiltonian and a cyclic state vector deformed holomorphically, the Krylov Gram determinants are $τ$ functions of the finite two-dimensional Toda lattice, whose Flaschka variables coincide exactly with these Arnoldi coefficients. The Toda dynamics therefore closes on this sector without determining the remaining upper Hessenberg entries. The subdiagonal part of the same sector also has a direct geometric meaning: the squared subdiagonal coefficients determine both the Fubini--Study metric and the Berry curvature of holomorphic Krylov subspaces, whereas the geometric quantities associated with subspaces lost at Arnoldi breakdown cease to be defined. Along a smooth real path in the cyclic region, the Arnoldi-frame connection further provides a Hermitian tridiagonal generator of exact isospectral transport. When added to the Arnoldi matrix, this generator cancels transitions between instantaneous eigenspaces and realizes counterdiabatic driving whenever the matrix is diagonalizable with a nondegenerate spectrum.

</details>


### [127] [Sharp Continuity of Petz and Sandwiched Rényi Conditional Entropies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04947)
*Hao-Chung Cheng,Po-Chieh Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定了在$[\frac{1}{2},1)$阶范围内，优化的Petz与夹层Rényi条件熵（optimized Petz and sandwiched Rényi conditional entropies）关于迹距离（trace distance）的精确模连续性。对于迹距离为δ的两个二分态，两种条件熵之差不超过$\frac{1}{1-α}\log[(1-\varepsilon)^α+(D-1)^{1-α}\varepsilon^α]$，其中$\varepsilon:=\min\{δ,1-1/D\}$，$D$为有效维度。该界对每个距离约束δ均可由各向同性对（isotropic pair）达到，且当$α\uparrow1$时恢复了Berta等人的经典结果。证明通过对各向同性等式族指示的比较点处对相关凹Rényi泛函线性化，并结合Schmidt秩支配、迹距离对偶及非交换校准估计实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rényi条件熵在量子信息论中具有基础性地位，但其作为量子态函数关于迹距离的连续性行为此前仅部分已知。尤其在$\alpha\in[\frac12,1)$这一非平凡区间内，缺乏精确的模连续性界。该论文旨在填补这一空白，并为量子条件熵的已知结果提供更广泛的统一推广。

Method: 证明的核心策略是将相关的凹Rényi泛函在各向同性等式族所指示的比较点处线性化。通过Schmidt秩支配将等式几何扩展到任意锚定态（anchor state）；随后利用迹距离对偶（trace-distance duality）和非交换校准估计分别控制扰动项与锚定项，从而在不削弱精确常数的前提下完成证明。后者涉及矩阵分析工具，并借助ChatGPT 5.6 Sol辅助完成。

Result: 对于任意$α\in[\frac12,1)$，若两个二分态的迹距离为δ，则优化的Petz和夹层Rényi条件熵之差均不超过$\frac{1}{1-α}\log[(1-\varepsilon)^α+(D-1)^{1-α}\varepsilon^α]$。该界对于每个δ∈[0,1]均可被一个以最大纠缠态为锚的各向同性对达到，表明所给常数是最优的（sharp）。当$α\uparrow1$时，该结果恢复Berta等人[arXiv:2607.24687]最近得到的量子条件熵的精确连续性界。

Conclusion: 本文建立了优化的Petz与夹层Rényi条件熵在$\alpha\in[\frac12,1)$区间的精确模连续性，给出了可达的最优常数，并将其推广为已有量子条件熵连续性结果的统一框架。这一成果为理解Rényi族条件熵的稳定性提供了完整刻画，并展示了各向同性态族在不等式几何研究中的核心作用。

Abstract: We determine the sharp modulus of continuity, in trace distance, of the optimized Petz and sandwiched Rényi conditional entropies for every order $α\in[\frac12,1)$. If two bipartite states are within trace distance $δ$, then both conditional entropies differ by at most $\frac{1}{1-α}
  \log[(1-\varepsilon)^α
  +(D-1)^{1-α}\varepsilon^α]$, where $\varepsilon := \min\{δ,1-1/D\}$ and $D$ is the effective dimension, given by the dimension of the first subsystem times the largest possible Schmidt rank. For every distance constraint $δ\in[0,1]$, the bound is attained by an isotropic pair with a maximally entangled anchor. Taking $α\uparrow1$ recovers the recent sharp continuity bound of quantum conditional entropy by Berta et al. [arXiv:2607.24687].
  The proof linearizes the relevant concave Rényi functional at a comparison point dictated by the isotropic equality family. Schmidt-rank domination extends the equality geometry to an arbitrary anchor state, after which trace-distance duality and a noncommutative calibration estimate control the perturbation and anchor term without weakening the sharp constant. The latter estimate requires matrix analysis and is assisted by ChatGPT 5.6 Sol.

</details>


### [128] [Diffusive Speed Limits for U(1)-Covariant Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04953)
*Jianqi Sheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了受几何局域性和U(1)电荷守恒约束的一维协变编码器（covariant encoder）的快速制备问题，证明电荷-Haar码能够达到相邻电荷下界（精确到指数小修正），并发现局域数守恒砖墙（brickwork）电路中逻辑电荷的扩散导致Ω(n²)的编码时间下界，从而识别扩散是对称约束量子编码的操作性极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错码的快速制备（fast preparation）是可扩展量子存储器的关键，但几何局域性（geometric locality）和U(1)电荷守恒（U(1) charge conservation）施加了不可避免的输运约束。此前缺乏对这种约束下编码时间下界的精确刻画。

Method: 论文结合三种技术工具：精确互补通道几何（exact complementary-channel geometry）、电荷扇区Haar分析（charge-sector Haar analysis）以及门分辨连接矩展开（gate-resolved connected-moment expansion），研究一维协变编码器在标记擦除（flagged erasure）条件下的编码动力学。

Result: 电荷-Haar码达到相邻电荷下界（精确到指数小修正），得到精确的n^{-1/2}广域擦除律和锐利的半擦除相变；对局域数守恒砖墙电路证明了逻辑电荷扩散导致的Ω(n²)编码时间下界，同时证明了经典分量的O(n³)混合界，并将全通道上界约化为源受限低支撑算符扩散问题。

Conclusion: 扩散被识别为对称约束量子编码的操作性极限，该结果为精确形成时间（exact formation time）提供了路径。

Abstract: Fast preparation of quantum error-correcting codes is essential for scalable quantum memories, but geometric locality and $U(1)$ charge conservation impose an unavoidable transport constraint. We combine exact complementary-channel geometry, charge-sector Haar analysis, and a gate-resolved connected-moment expansion to study one-dimensional covariant encoders under flagged erasure. Charge-Haar codes attain the universal adjacent-charge lower bound up to exponentially small corrections, yielding an exact $n^{-1/2}$ extensive-erasure law and a sharp half-erasure transition. For local number-conserving brickwork circuits, diffusion of the logical charge enforces an $Ω(n^2)$ encoding-time lower bound; we also prove an $O(n^3)$ mixing bound for the classical component and reduce the remaining full-channel upper bound to a source-restricted low-support operator-spreading problem. These results identify diffusion as an operational limit on symmetry-constrained quantum coding and establish a route to its exact formation time.

</details>


### [129] [Reconstructing non-Abelian braiding and fusion without anyon transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04103)
*Lucy Byles,Matthew D. Horner,Benjamin T. H. Varcoe,Jiannis K. Pachos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并实验实现了一种仅基于测量（measurement-only）的协议，通过时间有序的带状操作（ribbon operations）重构量子双模型 $D(S_3)$ 的非阿贝尔编织（non-Abelian braiding）与融合（fusion）基本操作，无需物理任意子输运。该协议在 Quantinuum H2 捕获离子处理器上通过约简的双三态量子比特（two-qutrit）版本实现，重建的编织与融合变换分别达到 0.9988 和 0.9987 的平均归一化输出态保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非阿贝尔任意子为容错和通用量子计算提供了途径，但实验获取其定义的编织和融合数据受限于在量子硬件上实现扩展任意子过程的资源开销，因此需要更高效的实验方案。

Method: 提出一种基于时间有序带状操作的测量only协议，无需物理任意子输运即可重构 $D(S_3)$ 量子双模型的编织和融合基元；在 Quantinuum H2 捕获离子处理器上用约简双三态版本实现，采用辅助量子比特辅助的带状操作和任意子电荷投影（anyonic charge projections），并使用适配的 Hadamard 测试和后选择测量分别重构平方编织相位与融合振幅。

Result: 编织和融合变换的归一化输出态平均保真度分别为 $\overline{\mathcal{F}}_{R}=0.9988$ 和 $\overline{\mathcal{F}}_{F}=0.9987$；将这些基元组合产生了非克利福德（non-Clifford）编织和非稳定子（non-stabilizer）资源态。

Conclusion: 测量only的任意子编码可作为更大规模拓扑编码量子处理器的构建模块，支持拓扑量子计算的可扩展实验实现。

Abstract: Non-Abelian anyons offer a route to fault-tolerant and universal quantum computing, but experimental access to their defining braiding and fusion data remains limited by the resource overhead of implementing extended anyonic processes on quantum hardware. Here we introduce and experimentally realise a measurement-only protocol based on temporally ordered ribbon operations that reconstructs the non-Abelian braiding and fusion primitives of the quantum double model $D(S_3)$ without physical anyon transport. We implement a reduced two-qutrit version of the protocol on Quantinuum's H2 trapped-ion processors, realising ancilla-assisted ribbon operations and anyonic charge projections in a qubit encoding. We reconstruct the squared braiding phases and fusion amplitudes using an adapted Hadamard test and post-selected measurements, respectively. The associated braiding and fusion transformations reproduce their ideal actions with average normalised output-state fidelities of $\overline{\mathcal{F}}_{R}=0.9988$ and $\overline{\mathcal{F}}_{F}=0.9987$. Combining these primitives produces a non-Clifford braid and a non-stabilizer resource state, supporting measurement-only anyonic encodings as building blocks for larger topologically encoded quantum processors.

</details>


### [130] [Measurement-based simulation of lattice gauge theory dynamics with adaptive quantum circuits on a trapped-ion processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04290)
*Hiroki Sukeno,Enrico Rinaldi,Takuya Okuda*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报告了基于测量的量子模拟（measurement-based quantum simulation, MBQS）在(2+1)维Z₂规范场论（gauge theory）中实时动力学（real-time dynamics）的首次实验实现，使用Quantinuum System Model H2离子阱处理器，在2×2和3×3空间格点上观察到规范不变可观测量（gauge-invariant observables）的相干演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于门电路的量子电路实现晶格规范理论模拟面临硬件限制，而MBQS作为一种新架构，通过消耗模型特异的纠缠资源态并辅以自适应的中间电路测量来实现哈密顿量动力学（Hamiltonian dynamics），有望更自然地将规范理论中的局域约束编码进高阶形式对称性（higher-form symmetries）中，但其实验可行性尚未被验证。

Method: 利用Quantinuum System Model H2离子阱处理器，将2×2和3×3空间格点的Z₂规范理论映射为虚拟三维簇态（3D cluster states），分别消耗200和288个资源态量子比特，这些资源态由56量子比特寄存器中瞬时48和54个量子比特块，通过测量、重置和重新纠缠（measurement, reset, and re-entanglement）生成，并通过驱动演化的测量记录免费获取一形式对称性综合征（one-form-symmetry syndromes）。

Result: 在实验观测中实现了规范不变可观测量的相干演化，并利用测量记录提供的综合征进行后选择（postselection），强有力地抑制了观测到的高斯定律违例（Gauss-law violations），改善了与理想Trotter化动力学（Trotterized dynamics）的整体一致性。

Conclusion: 该结果表明MBQS是一种在当前硬件上可行的、具有对称性感知特性的架构，可用于模拟晶格场论（lattice field theories）。

Abstract: Measurement-based quantum simulation (MBQS)---a recently proposed architecture for simulating lattice gauge theories---implements Hamiltonian dynamics by consuming a model-specific entangled resource state with adaptive mid-circuit measurements, rather than by a gate-based circuit. The local constraints in lattice gauge theories are mirrored by the higher-form symmetries of the resource state. Here we report, to our knowledge, the first experimental realization of MBQS of real-time dynamics in the $(2+1)$-dimensional $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge theory using the Quantinuum System Model H2 trapped-ion processor. We observe coherent evolution of gauge-invariant observables on $2\times2$ and $3\times3$ spatial lattices, consuming virtual three-dimensional cluster states of 200 and 288 resource-state qubits that are generated from instantaneous blocks of 48 and 54 qubits within the 56-qubit register by measurement, reset, and re-entanglement. The measurement record that drives the evolution simultaneously provides one-form-symmetry syndromes at no additional cost, enabling postselection that strongly suppresses observed Gauss-law violations and improves aggregate agreement with ideal Trotterized dynamics. Our results demonstrate that MBQS is a viable, symmetry-aware architecture for simulating lattice field theories on present-day hardware.

</details>


### [131] [Single-eigenstate test of eigenstate thermalization hypothesis via perturbed eigenstate quench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04696)
*Zhouhao Guo,Jiaju Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并数值验证了一种基于微扰本征态淬火协议（perturbed eigenstate quench protocol）的高效单本征态诊断方法，用于判定本征态热化假设（ETH）是否成立。该方法通过分析子系统演化速度随子系统占系统比例的变化曲线形状（S形曲线 vs J形凸曲线）来区分满足与违反ETH的本征态，并在混沌、可积、多体局域化和量子多体疤痕等一维自旋链模型中取得与既有热化现象学完全一致的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ETH验证通常需要构建显式热系综（thermal ensemble），这在实际系统中往往难以实现。作者希望开发一种无需显式热系综构建、在单本征态层面即可诊断热化行为的数值可行探针。

Method: 作者向能量本征态引入弱随机微扰以破坏其平稳性，然后表征时间平均的子系统演化速度，将其作为子系统-总系统尺寸比的函数。诊断准则基于一个稳健的定性区分：满足ETH的本征态呈现带有清晰拐点的S形曲线（拐点位于半个系统尺寸附近），而违反ETH的本征态则呈现凸J形轮廓。

Result: 该方法在一维自旋链的四种范式性区域（chaotic、integrable、many-body localized、quantum many-body scar）中进行基准测试，所有结果均与既有的热化现象学（thermalization phenomenology）完全一致。

Conclusion: 该诊断方法避免了显式热系综构建的需要，提供了一种稳健且实验可行的单本征态热化探针，可在单本征态层面评估本征态热化行为。

Abstract: We propose and numerically validate an efficient single-eigenstate diagnostic for the eigenstate thermalization hypothesis (ETH) based on a perturbed eigenstate quench protocol. By introducing a weak random perturbation to an energy eigenstate to break its stationarity, we characterize the time-averaged subsystem evolution speed as a function of the subsystem-to-total system size ratio. The diagnostic relies on a robust qualitative distinction: eigenstates satisfying ETH exhibit an S-shaped curve with a clear inflection point near half the system size, while ETH-violating eigenstates display a convex J-shaped profile. We benchmark the criterion across paradigmatic one-dimensional spin chains covering chaotic, integrable, many-body localized, and quantum many-body scar regimes, obtaining full agreement with established thermalization phenomenology. Our method circumvents the need for explicit thermal ensemble construction, providing a robust, experimentally feasible probe of eigenstate thermalization at the single-eigenstate level.

</details>


### [132] [Unifying quantum measurement constructions via a relative-entropy minimum change principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04055)
*Nana Liu,Mark M. Wilde*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了量子统计推断中基于量子相对熵（quantum relative entropy）的最小变化原理（minimum change principle），给出最优测量通道的闭式刻画，并发现一类新的"softmin 热测量"（softmin thermal measurements），证明了其与熵正则化半定优化问题的联系以及可加性等性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小变化原理在经典概率论中为贝叶斯反转通道（Bayes reversal channel）提供了信息论刻画，近期被提出作为将贝叶斯规则推广到量子信息论的框架。本文旨在将该原理推广到量子统计推断情境，并系统刻画该原理下的最优测量。

Method: 以量子相对熵为目标函数，考虑基于经典到量子（classical-to-quantum）制备通道的前向过程和基于量子到经典（quantum-to-classical）测量通道的反向过程，通过含单一无约束厄米变量（unconstrained Hermitian variable）的对偶公式建立最优测量的闭式刻画。

Result: 该框架可复现已有的优秀测量（pretty good measurements）和费米-狄拉克热测量（Fermi-Dirac thermal measurements），并发现了新的 softmin 热测量族；证明 softmin 热测量是熵正则化半定优化问题（entropy-regularized semidefinite optimization problems）的最优解，其角色类似于统计力学中的热态（thermal states）；此外证明了相对熵最小变化原理满足可加性（additivity property），并针对量子假设检验考察了费米-狄拉克热测量的性能。

Conclusion: 最小变化原理为量子统计推断中的测量设计提供了统一框架，softmin 热测量揭示了测量理论与统计力学热态之间的深层类比，且该原理的可加性为其在量子假设检验等应用中的进一步使用奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: The minimum change principle provides an information-theoretic characterization of the Bayes reversal channel in classical probability theory and has recently been proposed as a framework for extending Bayes' rule to quantum information theory. Using quantum relative entropy, we investigate a minimum change principle for the setting of quantum statistical inference. Specifically, we consider a forward process based on a classical-to-quantum preparation channel and a reverse process based on a quantum-to-classical measurement channel. We establish a closed-form characterization of measurements that are optimal for this principle, and this optimal measurement can be found via a dual formulation involving a single unconstrained Hermitian variable. This perspective allows us to recover some notable measurements within the same framework, including pretty good measurements and Fermi-Dirac thermal measurements, and we use it to discover a novel family that we call softmin thermal measurements. We further show that softmin thermal measurements arise as optimal solutions to entropy-regularized semidefinite optimization problems, demonstrating that they play a role for measurements analogous to that of thermal states in statistical mechanics. Finally, we prove an additivity property for the relative-entropy minimum change principle and investigate the performance of Fermi-Dirac thermal measurements for quantum hypothesis testing.

</details>


### [133] [Quantum Many-Body Metrology of Rotation Sensing with Strong Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04082)
*Yong-Jun Baak,Uwe R. Fischer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了准一维环状陷阱（ring trap）中少量强相互作用玻色子进行旋转感应的量子极限，证明强关联多体陀螺仪（many-body gyroscope）的最终灵敏度需要自洽的多体解才能正确预测，并且在小旋转速度和小粒子数条件下，强相互作用耦合可使多体量子Fisher信息（quantum Fisher information）达到最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用强相互作用的小型化多体系统实现高精度旋转传感的量子极限，特别是在慢旋转和高空间分辨率的场景下，考察强关联效应对灵敏度的影响。

Method: 在带有两个弱连接的准一维环状陷阱中囚禁少量强相互作用玻色子，通过求解自洽的多体问题来分析该系统的旋转感应能力，并计算多体量子Fisher信息以评估传感灵敏度。

Result: 研究发现，对于小的旋转速度和小粒子数，多体量子Fisher信息在强相互作用耦合下达到最大值，表明强相互作用有助于提高对慢旋转的精确估计能力。

Conclusion: 强相互作用的小型化多体传感系统具备精确估计慢旋转并保持高空间分辨率的潜力，但要正确预测其最终灵敏度必须采用自洽的多体理论框架。

Abstract: We study the ultimate quantum limit of rotation sensing with a few strongly interacting bosons confined in a quasi-one-dimensional ring trap with two weak links. It is demonstrated that a self-consistent many-body solution of the problem is required to correctly predict the ultimate sensitivity of this strongly correlated many-body gyroscope to rotation. For both small rotation velocities and small particle numbers, the many-body quantum Fisher information becomes maximal for large interaction couplings, showing the potential of strongly interacting miniaturized many-body sensors to precisely estimate slow rotations with high spatial resolution.

</details>


### [134] [Equilibrium Thermodynamics of Non-Hermitian Dirac Fermions: Caloric and Magnetic Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04369)
*Francisco J. Peña,Bastian Castorene,Juan Pablo Esparza,Vladimir Juričić,Patricio Vargas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了实谱非厄米（real-spectrum non-Hermitian）狄拉克费米子在磁场中的平衡热力学标度关系。通过相似变换将系统映射到具有约化速度的厄米狄拉克模型，并利用约化有效磁场表示朗道能级谱，进而推导出化学势、熵、热容量和轨道磁响应的标度关系，覆盖不同热力学系综下的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米（non-Hermitian）量子体系在近年受到广泛关注，但其平衡热力学性质尚缺乏统一的理论框架。本文旨在建立实谱非厄米量子物质在磁场中的热力学标度关系，为理解这类体系的热力学行为提供系统性的理论基础。

Method: 假设系统相对于准厄米哈密顿量（quasi-Hermitian Hamiltonian）发生热化，通过相似变换将同一外加磁场下的非厄米系统映射到具有约化速度的厄米狄拉克模型，并将朗道能级谱表示为约化有效磁场的形式。基于这一结构，在不同热力学系综中推导化学势、熵、热容量和轨道磁响应的标度关系，并考察准静态非厄米形变下的绝热温度标度。

Result: 在固定投影填充因子（projected filling factor, PFF）下，自洽化学势沿压缩的朗道能级梯子分布，正则系综热力学函数为经重标度的厄米响应；在固定化学势下，朗道能级交叉产生由同一谱压缩支配的振荡量热和磁响应；准静态非厄米形变在固定PFF下还产生绝热温度标度。

Conclusion: 这些结果建立了实谱非厄米量子物质的普适热力学框架，并为在同一形式体系内纳入相互作用和无序效应提供了出发点。

Abstract: We establish scaling relations governing the equilibrium thermodynamics of real-spectrum non-Hermitian Dirac fermions in a magnetic field. Assuming thermalization with respect to the quasi-Hermitian Hamiltonian, a similarity transformation maps the system at the same applied field onto a Hermitian Dirac model with reduced velocity, while the Landau-level spectrum also admits a representation in terms of a reduced effective magnetic field. This structure yields scaling relations for the chemical potential, entropy, heat capacities, and orbital magnetic response in different thermodynamic ensembles. At fixed projected filling factor (PFF), the self-consistent chemical potential follows the compressed ladder of Landau levels, and the canonical thermodynamic functions are rescaled Hermitian responses. At fixed chemical potential, Landau-level crossings generate oscillatory caloric and magnetic responses governed by the same spectral compression. Quasistatic non-Hermitian deformation at fixed PFF further yields adiabatic temperature scaling. More broadly, these results establish a thermodynamic framework for real-spectrum non-Hermitian quantum matter and provide a starting point for incorporating the effects of interactions and disorder within the same formalism.

</details>


### [135] [Constructing Non-Hermitian Theories with Tunable Exceptional Points and Controlled State Purification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05052)
*Soumya Kanti Pal,Rupak Majumder,Shamik Gupta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出动量空间形变（momentum-space deformation）作为设计和控制二次多体哈密顿量中奇异点（exceptional points, EPs）的统一原理，揭示了单一动量扇区EP会引发多体本征矢量聚并（coalescence）的指数增殖，并建立了EP作为任意混合量子态纯化（purification）的普适机制，同时提供了系统逆向工程协议以生成各类非厄米量子物质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇异点是非厄米（non-Hermitian）量子系统的标志性特征，但其在多体系统中的普适设计原则尚不明确。本文旨在建立一种统一的设计原理，以实现对二次多体哈密顿量中奇异点的创建与控制，并挖掘其更深层次的物理应用（如量子态纯化）。

Method: 作者采用动量空间形变的方法，分析二次多体哈密顿量中动量扇区（momentum sector）的谱结构，识别出奇异点出现的普适判据及对应的临界形变强度；同时开发了系统性逆向工程（reverse-engineering）协议，用于生成短程/长程、互易（reciprocal）与非互易（nonreciprocal）的非厄米量子物质，并显式给出了 Lindblad 嵌入（Lindblad embedding）的构造。

Result: 研究发现单一动量扇区的奇异点可导致多体本征矢量聚并的指数增殖；建立了奇异点作为任意混合态纯化普适机制的框架，揭示了不同的纯化区（purification regimes）以及在热力学极限下基本的奇偶系统尺寸二分性（odd-even system-size dichotomy）。

Conclusion: 动量空间形变被确立为奇异点工程（exceptional-point engineering）和多体非厄米量子系统受控设计的统一路径，为实验实现和理论探索提供了系统化的设计工具。

Abstract: Exceptional points (EP's) are a hallmark of non-Hermitian quantum systems. We show that momentum-space deformation provides a general design principle for creating and controlling EP's in quadratic many-body Hamiltonians. We identify universal criteria for the momentum sectors to host EP's and the corresponding critical deformation strengths, while revealing that a single momentum-sector EP induces quite remarkably an exponential proliferation of many-body eigenvector coalescences. We further establish EP's as a universal mechanism for purifying arbitrary mixed quantum states, uncovering distinct purification regimes and a fundamental odd-even system-size dichotomy in the thermodynamic limit. Our framework also provides a systematic reverse-engineering protocol for generating short- and long-range, reciprocal and nonreciprocal non-Hermitian quantum matter, together with an explicit Lindblad embedding. These results thus establish momentum-space deformation as a unified route to exceptional-point engineering and controlled design of many-body non-Hermitian quantum systems.

</details>

### [136] [Valley polarization of moiré interlayer exciton complexes driven by many-body interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04151)
*Adrian O. Paulus,Mikołaj J. Metelski,Alain Dijkstra,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Nathan P. Wilson,Jonathan J. Finley*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过测量R型WSe₂/WS₂双层异质结中局域层间激子（localized interlayer excitons, IX）的光致发光寿命与谷极化（valley polarization），揭示了不同激子密度和电荷密度下的多体物理效应。研究发现，在电荷不可压缩相中测得的谷极化降低并非源于增强的谷间散射，而是由IX寿命的显著延长导致的谷极化稀释效应；在高激发密度下，双占据位（IXX）在衰减过程中展现出反常的强谷极化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 莫尔（moiré）过渡金属二硫族化物异质结构中的局域层间激子能够探测并参与莫尔超晶格所承载的多体态，理解IX在不同密度条件下的寿命与谷极化行为对于揭示其复杂多体物理至关重要，而此前研究对电荷不可压缩相中谷极化下降的机理尚未澄清。

Method: 研究采用R型WSe₂/WS₂双层结构，在宽范围的激子密度与电荷密度条件下，通过时间分辨光致发光（time-resolved photoluminescence）测量IX寿命，并结合时间积分测量分析谷极化行为；在高激发密度下进一步探测IXX的动力学特性。

Result: 在低IX密度下，分数电子莫尔填充形成不可压缩电荷晶体，延长了IX寿命；在电荷不可压缩相中观察到的谷极化下降实际源于IX寿命大幅延长造成的谷极化稀释，而非增强的谷间散射。在高激发密度下，IXX在衰减过程中虽名义上由反平行谷构型组成，却表现出强烈的反常谷极化。

Conclusion: IX的激子与谷动力学显著受到莫尔异质双层结构丰富多体物理的调控，本研究为理解层间激子在莫尔超晶格中的复杂行为提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Localized interlayer excitons (IX) in moiré transition metal dichalcogenide heterostructures can both probe and participate in many-body states hosted by the moiré superlattice. When the IX density is small compared to the moiré lattice density, the formation of incompressible charge crystals at fractional electronic moiré fillings modifies exciton-charge scattering, leading to enhanced lifetimes in photoluminescence (PL) measurements. At high IX densities, the exciton dynamics are altered by the emergence of an excitonic Mott insulator and the formation of doubly-occupied sites (IXX). Here, we investigate the IX PL lifetime and valley polarization in an R-type $\mathrm{WSe_2}$/$\mathrm{WS_2}$ bilayer across a wide range of IX and charge densities. While previous studies reported a decrease of polarization in time-integrated measurements in charge-incompressible phases, our results show that this arises not from enhanced intervalley scattering, but from a dilution of the valley polarization by the dramatic enhancement of IX lifetimes. At high excitation densities, we probe the dynamics of the IXX and show that despite the nominal antiparallel valley configuration of the two constituent excitons, a strong, anomalous valley polarization develops as the IXX population decays. Our results shed light on the complex exciton and valley dynamics of IX and demonstrate that they are strongly modified by the rich many-body physics of moiré heterobilayers.

</details>


### [137] [Magnetic Field Reorganization of Electronic States in Moiré Bilayer Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04269)
*Milan Sharma Mandigo-Stoba,William Wang,Jackson Kuklin,Jack Lichterman,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Qianhui Shi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过双层石墨烯/hBN莫尔超晶格（moiré system）实验，揭示了磁场诱导的半经典轨道网络演化，涵盖Lifshitz相变、磁击穿（magnetic breakdown）和电子-空穴口袋间的散射。磁场下量子振荡频率与霍尔密度出现显著变化，并表现出谷对比（valley-contrasting）行为：Berry曲率热点增强K谷磁击穿而抑制K′谷，谷反对称轨道磁矩分裂Lifshitz相变。高场下出现谷对称性破缺的Hofstadter能隙，低场高温下则观测到与费米面总面积相关的近密度无关电阻振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁场通常被用作诊断二维系统量子相的工具，但磁场本身也会重塑电子结构。本文旨在理解中等强度磁场如何驱动莫尔体系从半经典输运向Hofstadter regime演化，并揭示磁场诱导的轨道网络重构机制。

Method: 在双层石墨烯/hBN莫尔体系中，通过测量1 T至2 T磁场下的量子振荡频率和霍尔密度随载流子密度的变化，分析磁击穿和磁Lifshitz相变。结合Berry曲率热点与谷反对称轨道磁矩的作用，解析谷选择性轨迹及其在更高磁场下表现为谷对称性破缺的Hofstadter能隙；同时在高温低场条件下观测电子-空穴口袋间散射产生的电阻振荡。

Result: 在1 T至2 T磁场下，量子振荡频率和霍尔密度在宽载流子密度范围内发生显著变化，表明磁击穿与磁Lifshitz相变同时发生。该演化呈现谷对比特征：Berry曲率热点在K谷增强磁击穿而在K′谷抑制之；谷反对称轨道磁矩导致相应的Lifshitz相变分裂。高场下出现谷对称性破缺的Hofstadter能隙；高温低场下，电子-空穴口袋间的散射产生频率对应两者费米面面积之和的近密度无关电阻振荡，且在传统Onsager振荡被热抹除后仍持续存在。

Conclusion: 中等强度磁场即可显著重组莫尔电子态，使体系从半经典输运平滑过渡到Hofstadter regime。这一统一图像揭示了磁场诱导轨道网络重构的谷选择性机制，为理解磁场在莫尔体系中的调控作用提供了新视角。

Abstract: Magnetic fields are widely used to diagnose quantum phases in two-dimensional systems through quantum oscillations or by tuning spin and valley polarizations, but magnetic fields can also reshape the underlying electronic structure. Here, using a bilayer graphene/hBN moiré system, we reveal a rich magnetic field induced evolution of the semiclassical orbit network, encompassing Lifshitz transitions, magnetic breakdown, and scattering between coexisting electron and hole pockets. At magnetic fields of 1 T to 2 T, quantum oscillation frequencies and the Hall density change markedly over a broad carrier density range, signaling magnetic breakdown and magnetic Lifshitz transitions. This evolution is valley contrasting: Berry curvature hot spots near the breakdown junctions enhance magnetic breakdown in the K valley while suppressing it in the K$^\prime$ valley, whereas valley-antisymmetric orbital magnetic moments split the corresponding Lifshitz transitions. The resulting valley-selective trajectories manifest at higher fields as valley-symmetry-breaking Hofstadter gaps. At elevated temperatures and low magnetic fields, scattering between coexisting electron and hole pockets produces nearly density-independent resistance oscillations whose frequency tracks the sum of their Fermi surface areas, persisting after conventional Onsager oscillations are thermally washed out. Our results provide a unified picture of how modest magnetic fields reorganize moiré electronic states as the system evolves from semiclassical transport toward the Hofstadter regime.

</details>


### [138] [Methods for traceable scanning magnetometry using single nitrogen vacancy centers in diamond: determining orientation, distance and localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04632)
*Nikhita Khera,Ephraim Spindler,Yanis Abdedou,Marcel Gasser,Sandra Wolff,Bert Lägel,Robert Frömter,Mathias Weiler,Mathias Kläui,Elke Neu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文通过扫描氮空位（NV）中心成像（scanning NV imaging）于微图案化、垂直磁化的条纹与圆盘样品，直接推断NV中心与样品间距d_NV及NV高对称轴的方位角取向，无需外部矢量磁体控制；测得d_NV=31.5 nm，方位角精度达3°。同时利用商用硅针尖对金刚石纳米结构的顶点形貌成像以检测表面污染，并通过监测NV荧光估计NV在纳米结构内的横向位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单晶金刚石纳米结构中的单个可扫描NV中心能够实现纳米尺度定量磁场成像，但NV与样品间距以及NV高对称轴取向等关键参数通常无法精确获知，通常作为自由拟合参数参与数据处理。该论文旨在发展一种无需外部矢量磁体控制即可直接推断这些参数的方法。

Method: 采用扫描NV成像技术对微图案化、垂直磁化的条纹和圆盘样品进行测量，直接从测量数据中推断NV中心与样品的距离d_NV和NV的方位角取向。同时使用商用硅针尖对金刚石纳米结构顶点进行形貌成像以检测表面污染，并通过监测NV荧光随针尖位置的变化来估计NV在金刚石纳米结构内的横向位置。

Result: 测得NV中心与样品间距离d_NV=31.5 nm，推断的方位角取向精度达到3°。该方法无需外部矢量磁体控制即可实现高精度参数确定，同时利用硅针尖形貌成像成功检测到表面污染，并通过荧光监测估计了NV在纳米结构中的横向位置。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了无需外部矢量磁体控制即可直接、高精度推断NV中心关键几何参数（间距与方位角）的可行性，为金刚石纳米结构中的定量磁场成像提供了更可靠且简化的校准途径。

Abstract: Individual, scannable nitrogen vacancy (NV) centers in single crystal diamond nanostructures enable nanoscale, quantitative imaging of magnetic stray fields. Nevertheless, important parameters like distance between the NV center and the sample and the orientation of the NV high symmetry axis are often not known precisely and enter data evaluation as free fitting parameters. We here use scanning NV imaging on micro-patterned, perpendicularly magnetized stripes and discs. From these measurements, we directly infer NV - sample distance d_NV and the NV's azimuthal orientation without the need for an external vector magnet control. We determine d_NV = 31.5 nm, while we infer the azimuthal orientation with a precision of 3°. We additionally employ commercially available silicon needles to image the apex topography of our diamond nanostructures to detect surface contamination. Simultaneously, monitoring NV fluorescence as a function of the needle's position allows us to estimate the lateral placement of the NV inside the diamond nanostructure.

</details>


### [139] [High-speed time-series prediction using compact memristor circuits with adjustable dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04856)
*Dániel Molnár,János Volk,Tímea Nóra Török,Zoltán Balogh,Nadia Jimenez Olalla,Miklós Csontos,Juerg Leuthold,András Halbritter*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出将Ta2O5非易失性忆阻器（nonvolatile memristor）用作紧凑、可追溯且可控的动态储层计算（dynamic reservoir computing）层来执行时间序列预测任务。通过利用强电压依赖的开关速度实现可配置的编程与遗忘时间，仅需两个至六个忆阻输入通道即可在基准任务上获得优异预测精度，且通过调节输入电压可将信息处理速度从kHz提升至MHz范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时间序列预测框架在处理快速动态信号时面临计算开销和硬件复杂度的挑战，尤其是在逼近通信数据速率的场景下。作者尝试探索忆阻器这一新兴器件能否作为动态储层计算层，以紧凑、可控且可追溯的方式高效完成时间序列预测任务。

Method: 采用Ta2O5非易失性忆阻器构建动态储层计算层，利用其强电压依赖的开关速度进行信息处理，通过正负驱动电压脉冲配置可调的编程与遗忘时间。训练方式有两种：一是固定遗忘时间、仅用线性回归优化输出层；二是同时优化遗忘时间与输出层权重。

Result: 在时间序列预测基准任务上，可配置的遗忘动力学使系统在仅有六个（固定遗忘时间情形）或两个（同时优化遗忘时间情形）忆阻输入通道下即获得优异预测精度。此外，通过调节输入电压电平，同一忆阻动态层的信息处理速度可从kHz提升至MHz范围，同时保持优异的预测精度。

Conclusion: 该方案证明了基于忆阻器的动态网络在快速时间信号的分析、预测与恢复方面的优势，其处理速度可逼近通信数据速率，展示了忆阻储层计算在实际高速时间序列处理中的潜力。

Abstract: Ta$_2$O$_5$ nonvolatile memristors are used as compact, traceable, and well-controllable dynamic reservoir computing layers to perform time-series prediction tasks. The strongly voltage-dependent switching speed is utilized for information processing. It enables the configuration of tailorable programming and forgetting times in response to the positive and negative driving voltage pulses. Benchmarking this framework on time-series prediction problems reveals that the configurable forgetting dynamics enables a high prediction accuracy using a rather small number of memristive input channels. The training is based either on optimizing the output layer using linear regression with fixed forgetting times, or on optimizing the forgetting times as well. In the first case, six memristive channels, while in the second, only two memristive channels are used to demonstrate excellent prediction accuracy for the benchmark tasks. This scheme allows for the tunability of the operating frequency over many orders of magnitude: by adjusting the input voltage levels, the information processing speed of the same memristive dynamic layer can be increased from the kHz to the MHz range while maintaining excellent prediction accuracy. These findings demonstrate the merits of memristor based dynamic networks in the analysis, prediction and recovery of fast temporal signals, approaching telecommunication data rates.

</details>


### [140] [Large nonlinear Hall effect in strained moiré structures hosting high-pseudospin fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04875)
*Srijata Lahiri,Gourab Paul,Bilal Tanatar,Saurabh Basu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种莫尔水磨晶格（moiré watermill lattice）中的线性和非线性霍尔响应。堆叠与扭转在费米能级附近产生具有抑制群速度的四能带流形，通过引入破缺反演对称性的质量项打开能隙并驱动系统进入非平庸拓扑相。施加应变破坏C3对称性后，系统展现出显著的非线性霍尔响应，其Berry曲率偶极子在拓扑相边界处急剧翻转并呈现蝶形特征，其幅度远超对称性破缺的过渡金属二硫属化物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统线性霍尔效应难以完全刻画拓扑相变中的量子几何信息，而非线性霍尔响应作为Berry曲率偶极子（Berry curvature dipole）的直接探针，可更敏感地反映能带拓扑结构与量子几何性质。莫尔体系为调控能带色散和拓扑性质提供了丰富平台，但其中非线性霍尔效应的研究尚属空白，本文旨在系统地探索这一方向。

Method: 本文在莫尔水磨晶格中引入满足晶体对称性约束的局域质量项（onsite mass）来破缺层间反演对称性并打开能隙，通过构建质量强度与扭转角θ的相图来确定各拓扑区域及其Chern数。随后引入均匀应变以打破残余C3对称性，从而激活有限的Berry曲率偶极子并产生非线性霍尔响应，最后通过有限温度下的热效应分析评估该响应的可观测性。

Result: 相图显示系统存在多个高Chern数拓扑扇区。在引入应变打破C3对称性后，Berry曲率偶极子被激活并在穿越拓扑相边界时急剧变号，随系统参数变化呈现出蝶形图案。偶极子幅度显著超过以往报道的对称性破缺过渡金属二硫属化物（TMDs），这与该晶格伪自旋-3/2（pseudospin-3/2）特征带来的增强Wilson-loop缠绕和量子几何一致。

Conclusion: 该体系的Berry曲率偶极子对拓扑相变具有高度敏感性，可作为识别低温下拓扑相边界的重要工具。非线性霍尔响应幅度的大幅增强表明莫尔水磨晶格是一种优越的研究量子几何与拓扑性质的平台。

Abstract: We investigate the linear and nonlinear Hall response of a moiré \emph{watermill lattice}, in which stacking and twisting generate a four-band manifold near the Fermi level with suppressed group velocities at discrete magic angles. Including an inversion symmetry breaking onsite mass breaks the interlayer symmetry, opening a gap in this manifold and driving the system into a non-trivial bulk topological phase. We map the resulting phase diagram as a function of the strength of the mass and twist angle $θ$, revealing several sectors with high Chern numbers. We then introduce strain to break the residual $C_3$ symmetry of the lattice which activates a finite Berry curvature dipole and correspondingly, a nonlinear Hall response. The dipole reverses sign sharply across topological phase boundaries, producing butterfly like features when plotted against the relevant system parameters. Its magnitude substantially exceeds that reported for symmetry-broken transition metal dichalcogenides, consistent with the elevated Wilson-loop winding and enhanced quantum geometry associated with the lattice's pseudospin-$3/2$ character. We conclude by incorporating thermal effects on the Berry curvature dipole, asserting that it is an important tool for discerning topology at low temperatures.

</details>


### [141] [RKKY interaction as a probe of spin splitting and odd-parity nature in Floquet collinear magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04969)
*Hou-Jian Duan,Yong-Jia Wu,Xiaoliang Xiao,Ming-Xun Deng,Mou Yang,Rui-Qiang Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yosida（RKKY）相互作用可作为统一磁探针，用于检测共线奇数宇称磁体（odd-parity magnets）中的自旋劈裂及其奇数宇称自旋极化特征，通过共线f波磁体中的磁性反转和Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya（DM）项符号交替等独特磁信号，实现对这类磁体的明确判别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究提出，通过Floquet工程可在共线反铁磁体（collinear antiferromagnets, AFMs）中涌现奇数宇称磁体，其核心特征是依赖谷的自旋劈裂（valley-dependent spin splitting）引发奇数宇称自旋极化。这带来两大关键挑战：一是探测自旋劈裂以验证这类磁体的产生机制，二是识别该极化以确认其奇数宇称本质。

Method: 以共线f波磁体（f-wave magnets）为代表性案例，作者通过理论计算分析RKKY相互作用在该体系中的表现，考察Heisenberg/Ising项与Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya（DM）项在不同磁信号中的行为，并与相关反铁磁体进行对比以检验判别能力。

Result: 研究发现，RKKY相互作用在共线f波磁体中产生独特的磁信号：Heisenberg/Ising项出现磁性反转（magnetism reversal），DM项呈现符号交替（sign alternation），这些特征可将其与其他相关反铁磁体明确区分。此外，DM项展现出f波形状且满足奇数宇称对称性关系J^{αβ}_{DM}(R) = -J^{αβ}_{DM}(C_{2q}R)（q=3），直接对应动量空间中的奇数宇称自旋极化S_z(k) = -S_z(C_{2q}k)。该行为在p波磁体（q=1）中同样存在，表明该方法具有普适性。

Conclusion: 本工作确立了RKKY相互作用作为探测共线奇数宇称磁体能带特征的通用磁探针，其预测结果可通过自旋极化扫描隧道谱（spin-polarized scanning tunneling spectroscopy）等现有实验技术加以验证。

Abstract: Odd-parity magnets were recently proposed to emerge in collinear antiferromagnets (AFMs) via Floquet engineering, with valley-dependent spin splitting underlying the odd-parity spin polarization. This proposal brings about two key challenges: detecting the spin splitting to verify the generation mechanism of these magnets, and identifying such polarization to confirm the odd-parity nature. Here, we show that the Ruderman-Kittel-Kasuya-Yosida (RKKY) interaction provides a unified magnetic probe for both tasks. Taking collinear $f$-wave magnets as a representative example, we find that the RKKY interaction yields distinct magnetic signals of the spin splitting---including a magnetism reversal in the Heisenberg/Ising terms and a sign alternation of the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya (DM) term---that enable clear discrimination of collinear $f$-wave magnets from other related AFMs. Moreover, the DM term exhibits an $f$-wave shape with odd-parity symmetry, satisfying $J^{αβ}_{DM}(\mathbf{R}) = -J^{αβ}_{DM}(C_{2q}\mathbf{R})$ ($q=3$), which directly reflects the odd-parity spin polarization $S_z(\mathbf{k}) = -S_z(C_{2q}\mathbf{k})$ in momentum space. This behavior persists in $p$-wave magnets ($q=1$), demonstrating the generality of our approach. Our work establishes the RKKY interaction as a versatile probe for detecting band features of collinear odd-parity magnets, with predictions accessible to existing experimental techniques such as spin-polarized scanning tunneling spectroscopy.

</details>


### [142] [Magnetic catalysis and Hall conductivity of excitonic insulators in a planar four-Fermi model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04986)
*William R. Tavares,Rudnei O. Ramos,Nei Lopes*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文利用扩展的平面四费米模型研究了垂直磁场对半导体区域激子绝缘体（excitonic insulator, EI）相的影响。在大N近似下，发现磁场增强EI凝聚并提高其临界温度，实现激子版本的磁催化（magnetic catalysis）；同时磁场改变临界化学势的非单调行为、移动平均场三临界点并扩展一级相变区域，并显著影响霍尔电导率（Hall conductivity）的台阶结构与阈值，表明相结构与霍尔响应可共同作为平面费米体系中激子序的互补判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究磁场对激子绝缘体相的影响，特别是探索磁场调控无能隙费米子体系（gapless fermionic systems）中激子序（excitonic ordering）的判定途径，为平面费米体系中的量子相变和输运性质提供新的实验观测手段。

Method: 采用扩展的平面四费米（four-Fermi）模型描述半导体区域的激子绝缘体相，运用大N近似（large-N approximation）在有限温度、有限化学势和有限磁场下自洽求解标量和激子凝聚（coupled scalar and excitonic condensates）的方程，并进一步计算霍尔电导率。

Result: 磁场增强EI凝聚并提高其临界温度，体现为激子版本的磁催化；标量凝聚在EI相中保持不变。临界化学势随磁场呈非单调变化，源于朗道能级（Landau levels）的逐步填充。磁场还移动平均场三临界点并扩大温度-化学势相图中一级相变区域。霍尔电导率随磁场增强而台阶数减少、阈值被修改，且EI凝聚的出现伴随霍尔电导率特征性变化，包括连续相变附近随磁场变化的斜率改变。

Conclusion: 本文表明，磁场通过磁催化机制增强激子绝缘体相，且激子序可通过组合的相图结构与霍尔响应的特征性变化在平面费米体系中被同时探测，为识别激子有序提供了互补的多个实验判据。

Abstract: We study the effects of a perpendicular magnetic field on the excitonic insulator (EI) phase in the semiconductor regime using an extended planar four-Fermi model. Within the large-$N$ approximation, we determine the coupled scalar and excitonic condensates at finite temperature, chemical potential, and magnetic field. The field enhances the EI condensate and raises its critical temperature, providing an excitonic realization of magnetic catalysis, while the scalar condensate remains constant throughout the EI phase. By contrast, the critical chemical potential depends nonmonotonically on the field because of the successive occupation of Landau levels. The magnetic field also shifts the mean-field tricritical point and enlarges the first-order region of the temperature--chemical-potential phase diagram. We further analyze the Hall conductivity and find that increasing the field reduces the number of plateaus and modifies the threshold for a finite Hall response. For the parameters considered, the emergence of the EI condensate is accompanied by a characteristic change in the Hall conductivity, including a field-dependent change of slope near a continuous transition. These results show that the combined phase structure and Hall response can provide complementary signatures of excitonic ordering in planar fermionic systems.

</details>


### [143] [Accessing Chern Numbers From Bloch Eigenstates Singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04998)
*Rajesh Asapanna,Rabih El Sokhen,Paolo Aceto,Patrick Popescu-Pampu,Martin Guillot,Jacqueline Bloch,Sylvain Ravets,Clément Hainaut,Alberto Amo,Pierre Delplace*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文提出两种提取二维绝缘体布洛赫带（Bloch bands）陈数（Chern number）的方法，并将其应用于光子晶格（photonic lattice）实验。两种方法均需利用布洛赫本征矢的复值分量或其比值，通过观测本征态结构中的相位涡度（phase vorticity）来获得精确量子化的陈数，且对实验噪声具有鲁棒性。其中一种方法利用本征模的非归一化特性，特别适用于经典波动系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的断层扫描（tomography）方法依赖于对贝里曲率（Berry curvature）的近似重建及其在整个布里渊区（Brillouin zone）上的积分，精度有限。本研究旨在提出更鲁棒且能直接获得精确量子化陈数的替代方法。

Method: 提出两种基于布洛赫本征矢复值分量或其比值的陈数提取方法。两种方法均不依赖贝里曲率的积分近似，而是通过直接观察本征态结构中相位涡度（phase vorticity）来实现。其中一种方法利用本征模的非归一化（non-normalization）特性，使其特别适用于经典波系统。

Result: 两种方法均能给出精确量子化的陈数值，且对实验噪声具有鲁棒性（robust to experimental noise）。方法已在光子晶格实验中成功应用。

Conclusion: 所提出的相位涡度观测方法为二维绝缘体陈数的直接测量提供了替代现有贝里曲率积分路径的新途径，并为经典波动系统中的拓扑表征提供了更合适的工具。

Abstract: We propose two methods to extract the Chern numbers of the Bloch bands of a two dimensional insulator, and apply them to a photonic lattice experiment. These two methods require the knowledge of the complex-valued components of the Bloch eigenvectors, or their ratios. Unlike other tomography methods relying on the approximate reconstruction of the Berry curvature and its integration over the Brillouin zone, our methods boil down to the observation of phase vorticities in the eigensates structure. Those measurements, robust to experimental noise, yield exactly quantized values. One of the two methods exploits the non-normalization of the eigenmodes, making it particularly relevant for classical wave systems.

</details>
